# START HERE — VERCEL PREVIEW (MARA A1–B5)

**This is the entry point for deploying the existing integrated MARA application.** Do not select `webapp`, `segment-a`, or the repository root as Vercel's Root Directory.

## Identify the correct source

- **GitHub repository:** `digital-Alchemy-mcmg/Envoy_Diplomat`
- **Working branch:** `integration/a1-b5-vercel` (PR #5)
- **Vercel Root Directory:** `integrated`
- **Application source and build configuration:** `integrated/`
- **Application instructions:** [integrated/README.md](integrated/README.md)
- **Vercel-specific interface specification:** [integrated/INTERFACE-SPECIFICATION.md](integrated/INTERFACE-SPECIFICATION.md)
- **Build/test evidence:** [integrated/DEPLOYMENT-READINESS.md](integrated/DEPLOYMENT-READINESS.md)
- **Canonical interface requirements (on main):** [docs/interface/MARA_INTERFACE_PREREQUISITES.md](https://github.com/digital-Alchemy-mcmg/Envoy_Diplomat/blob/main/docs/interface/MARA_INTERFACE_PREREQUISITES.md)

## Vercel setup — preview only

1. Import `digital-Alchemy-mcmg/Envoy_Diplomat` into the **intended Vercel team**. Verify team access before creating a project; avoid duplicate projects.
2. Set **Root Directory = `integrated`**. Enable **Include source files outside of the Root Directory** (needed for existing Segment A imports).
3. Use **Install Command = `npm ci`**, **Build Command = `npm run build`**, **Output Directory = `dist`**, and **Node.js 24.x**. Build requires Git.
4. Ensure build-time read-only access to the pinned protected B source commit. For private shallow builds, `MARA_B_SOURCE_TOKEN` may be needed as a **build-only** secret. Never prefix it `VITE_`.
5. **Deploy the `integration/a1-b5-vercel` branch as Preview only.** Do not accidentally deploy default `main` as Production; if Vercel's import screen offers only `main`, stop and select an explicit-branch deployment mechanism.
6. Inspect deployment build logs, load the preview URL, and run the actual A1→A5→B1→B5 browser workflow with independently supplied candidate SDNA and external runner return evidence. Local fixtures do not establish production trust.

## Authority and STOP conditions

- No merge to `main`, no production deployment, no protected B edits, no reconstruction of A or B.
- A2 external runner execution is **manual**; exported JSON is not proof of runner execution.
- A5 browser-local ingress is not proof of authenticated production acceptance.
- B stages require explicit RUN and SEAL at their existing gates.
- The Vercel-specific specification still requires reconciliation with the now-present canonical document on `main`. This navigation file does **not** claim that reconciliation or deployment verification has passed.

**Quick recognition:** In the Vercel Root Directory picker, choose the folder literally named **`integrated`**. `src` is an internal source folder, not the deployment root.

# Canonical reconciliation — MARA A1–B5 Vercel interface

Status: **REFERENCE RESOLVED; CONTROL VERIFICATION INCOMPLETE**.
Canonical: [MARA_INTERFACE_PREREQUISITES.md](https://github.com/digital-Alchemy-mcmg/Envoy_Diplomat/blob/4f31b58ba048360a87136283f57e4e5d5b0f98d4/docs/interface/MARA_INTERFACE_PREREQUISITES.md), version 1.0, commit `4f31b58ba048360a87136283f57e4e5d5b0f98d4`.
Deployment branch: `integration/a1-b5-vercel`; application root `integrated/`.
Implementation baseline: protected B `d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a`. This document is a documentation audit, not a new test run.

## Control-to-implementation traceability

| --- | --- | --- |

| Identity, artifacts, hashes, receipts (section 3) | A4 exact strings and SHA-256, A5 invocation-bound worker receipts | Browser-local evidence; production authenticity UNVERIFIED |

## Unresolved gaps and deviations register

- **G-001 — Vercel team scope:** connected Vercel API returns 403 on intended team; preview not deployed. No substitute project or production deployment authorized.
- **G-002 — Canonical-to-UI coverage:** detailed control-by-control verification for evidence panels, recovery, accessibility and stage views remains unperformed.
- **G-003 — Runner provenance:** A2 external execution/return is manual; no verified authenticated runner acknowledgment or production receipt.
- **G-004 — Production B receiver:** browser-local worker evidence does not establish a production authenticated receiver.
- **G-005 — Ephemeral session:** reload discards progress; no durable state, queue, or global replay ledger.
- **G-006 — Source build access:** Vercel must read pinned B and baseline commits; validate build-time credentials without exposing secrets.
- **G-007 — Canonical text inconsistency:** section 19 calls the canonical document a draft not committed/approved, while its header declares Operator-authorized canonical status on main. Do not alter canonical main without Operator approval; treat the committed header and version binding as the reference and flag the contradictory historical paragraph for Operator decision.
- **G-008 — Deployment CSP/accessibility:** actual hosted behavior and accessibility remain untested.
- **D-001 — No approved deviations:** none recorded. Any proposed change to universal requirements requires Operator approval.

# A1–B5 Vercel integration — readiness report

Base: remote main 29c54dc2eff750c1d284e56775cb01e34de7fb9d, verified through GitHub and fetched with authenticated Git credentials. New isolated branch integration/a1-b5-vercel. Protected B remains d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a. Existing integration tags are untouched.

## Status

- INTEGRATION PASS: connected fixture A1 compilation → manual A2 dispatch/explicit return → A3 Operator selection → A4 minting → A5 verified browser-local ingress → B1–B5 explicit STOP/seal → verification/termination → sealed B export.
- VERCEL BUILD PASS: clean installation, strict TypeScript and Vite production build; separate browser worker and static assets emitted.
- DEPLOYMENT READY: static application configuration prepared and verified locally. Actual Vercel project/root/include-outside-root settings and build source credentials must be configured by the Operator. No deployed environment was tested or claimed.
- PRODUCTION TRUST PASS: BLOCKED. No verified production runner artifact, approved authenticated receiver, remote acceptance origin or deployed build attestation established.

## Boundaries and preservation

All changes are under integrated/. Existing segment-a source, A1–A4 semantics/serialization, A5 core verifier/local harness, compiler, topology, B frozen doctrine/stages, SDNA authority and C remain byte-unchanged. The shell loads the existing A main module through an adapter that appends getter/render exports in memory; original files and methods are not edited. Progressive disclosure is applied by the shell to existing sections. The worker invokes unchanged SegmentBSession/handleCommand from pinned Git source. No validator copies, automatic seals, correction loop, reminting or fake runner are added.

Candidate SDNA is an independent file selected in the integration boundary, never produced by A. Each target has its own B session. Every B RUN/SEAL/FINISH/EXPORT goes through the existing command/session gates. UI exposes only session progression/refusal diagnostics and the permitted sealed export, not raw B context, bindings, trees or vault. B3/B4/B5 semantics are those of the pinned source. U-001 timestamps remain unchanged; exact A input hashes and source/build fingerprints supply identity rather than serialized B output.

## Browser evidence

The existing Receiver.receive seam and local-evidence registry are preserved. The browser adapter creates a dedicated Worker, random request IDs and the existing A5 attempt UUID. Only the pending request's worker response can be consumed. Worker runs actual B ingress on exact A4 strings, independently measures both SHA-256s, captures SessionView/refusals and returns attempt/transaction/time/build identity. Adapter compares the source/dependency/build fingerprint generated for both sides of this build, then issues the existing process-local receipt capability. A5 verifies freshness, origin, one-use invocation binding and exact versions before acknowledgment.

This establishes controlled browser-realm execution evidence, not production authentication. Same-origin scripts, build loader and deployed assets are trusted. A malicious trusted script/build can subvert the realm; matching build strings alone are not a signed supply-chain attestation. No external receiver string is treated as authentication. Receipts cannot be serialized and imported as valid capabilities. Reload clears both capability and worker sessions; no cross-process/global replay protection is claimed. Timeout remains the existing one-second controlled boundary; large inputs/multi-target runs can safely block rather than accept late evidence.

## Infrastructure

Root integrated; install npm ci; build npm run build; dist output; Node 24.x recommended; Git required during build. Repository files outside root must be included. The pinned source must already be in the checkout or fetched with read-only repository access. Optional build-only MARA_B_SOURCE_TOKEN supports private shallow clones and is never exposed to client modules. No runtime secrets/services are needed. CSP worker-src self is the only added execution allowance; connect-src none remains.

## Fresh verification

October 13, 2026, America/New_York. Commands run from their stated roots:

- integrated: npm ci --cache /tmp/envoy-npm-cache --no-audit --no-fund — clean install passed.
- integrated: npm test — connected pipeline plus negative authority and receipt tests passed (20/20).
- integrated: npm run build — strict TypeScript and production worker build passed.
- integrated: ENVOY_CHROMIUM_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium npm run test:e2e — three connected workflows passed, including full A1–B5 under CSP, invalid SDNA, decision/cancellation and reload loss.
- segment-a: npm ci, npm test — original 73/73 passed, including replay, forged/missing/partial/stale/version receipt and timeout protections; original production build passed.
- integrated: npm run verify:source — exact protected-path and nine source-blob checks (rerun after staging).

Initial build found a top-level await TypeScript module marker missing in the build-only Git helper; corrected with export {}. Initial git fetch authentication failed before work began; explicit GitHub credential helper and remote-tracking ref fetch established the correct base before edits. No source semantics were changed to pass tests.

## Remaining blockers / Operator prerequisites

1. Configure Vercel root, inclusion of outside-root repository files, Node and read-only build source access; inspect a preview deployment separately when authorized.
2. Supply authoritative real runner return/provenance and independent SDNA. Fixtures only prove mechanics.
3. Approve production trust/receiving environment and authentic evidence mechanism before claiming production acceptance.

No main merge or production deployment. No source-derived stage semantics are invented to eliminate UNBOUND or refusal. No threshold tuning.

## Clean-checkout execution receipt

# MARA A1–B5 — Vercel deployment interface specification

Status: DRAFT — canonical document located; commit binding and requirement reconciliation pending.
Deployment branch: integration/a1-b5-vercel.
Deployment platform: Vercel static Vite hosting.
Execution environment: browser main realm plus a dedicated same-origin module worker; build-time Node 24.x and Git.

## Authority and version binding

The Operator's Deployment Branch Interface Policy supplied in this conversation controls the specification hierarchy:

canonical interface requirements on main → deployment-specific interface specification → implementation.

This document implements platform-specific mechanisms; it does not authorize changes to universal requirements. Deployment branches must not silently weaken or override canonical requirements. Universal changes require canonical-level review and Operator authorization; deployment-specific changes remain on this branch.

Canonical reference: `docs/interface/MARA_INTERFACE_PREREQUISITES.md`, version 1.0 (2026-10-13), approved on `main` at commit `4f31b58ba048360a87136283f57e4e5d5b0f98d4`. The original main baseline `29c54dc2eff750c1d284e56775cb01e34de7fb9d` predates that document. Canonical path/version/commit are now identified, but identification alone is not implementation compliance or deployed acceptance. See [canonical reconciliation](CANONICAL-RECONCILIATION.md) for control mapping, gaps and unresolved discrepancies.

Implementation references: existing standalone A source on main; protected B d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a; current integrated implementation and its readiness report. The earlier Operator implementation directives remain applicable pending reconciliation against the canonical document.

## Applicable requirements and controls

These existing implementation controls are mapped against the approved canonical specification in [CANONICAL-RECONCILIATION.md](CANONICAL-RECONCILIATION.md). The table is not a substitute for stage-by-stage verification.

| Requirement | Vercel/browser implementation mechanism | Verification / acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Preserve A1–A5 architecture and component authority | Existing A core/UI reused; build adapter adds only UI integration access; B source read from its pinned Git commit | Changes confined to integrated/; protected-file and vendor-blob verification |
| A2 remains an independent external runner | Complete manual dispatch JSON and explicit returned-input staging; no collection service or preloaded demo | Browser dispatch/return workflow; fixture evidence labelled test-only |
| Operator staging/decision authority | Existing PUSH/PIN/REJECT controls and confirmation dialogs | Decision, exclusion, cancellation and missing-taxonomy tests |
| Preserve minted A4 artifacts | Receiver passes exact strings to worker; worker measures exact input SHA-256 | Exact-version acknowledgment and mismatch tests |
| Independent candidate SDNA | Operator supplies candidate.sdna.yaml to integration boundary; B receives its existing three-file input | Missing/malformed/candidate mismatch refusals; no A SDNA production |
| A5 verified handoff | Existing receiver seam and local-evidence verifier; invocation/request IDs, worker-origin capture and build fingerprints | Acceptance requires actual ingress PASS and verified exact versions; forged/stale/replayed evidence blocks |
| B hard STOPs and explicit seals | Existing session/command gate execution, separate RUN/SEAL buttons | Every B1–B5 STOP halts progression; no automatic sealing |
| Preserve B3/B4/B5 semantics | Pinned unmodified binding, audit and prism source | Closed match vocabulary, UNBOUND, clearance/integrity-refusal and prism tests |
| Restricted B disclosure/egress | Only session progression/refusals shown; explicit sealed B export after verification/termination | Production browser export test; no raw context/vault/tree debug surface |
| Preserve U-001 | No B receipt-clock or hashing modifications | Pinned source preservation; no deterministic B-output-byte claim |

## Platform configuration and artifact lifecycle

- Vercel root: integrated. Include repository files outside root because existing segment-a modules are reused.
- Install npm ci; build npm run build; output dist. Node 24.x recommended. Git required during build.
- Source preparer verifies availability of exact B and protection-baseline commits. Private shallow clones require read-only source access. Optional MARA_B_SOURCE_TOKEN is build-only; credential helper restricts its destination. No VITE_ secret and no runtime secret are required.
- Vite emits same-origin module-worker and application assets under /assets. The application uses / with no client route rewrite requirement.
- CSP retains connect-src none; worker-src self permits the necessary B worker. This documented platform allowance does not authorize broader networking or script origins.
- Source/runtime transport is browser-local worker messaging; A2 collection/return remains manual. There is no invented B network endpoint.
- Session storage is ephemeral memory only. No database, localStorage/sessionStorage persistence, queue or automatic restoration. Reload loses state. Existing confirmed reset clears the session; cancellation preserves it.
- Exact A artifacts remain in the committed session and may be saved using existing A preparation controls. B exposes only its permitted sealed handoff. Independent SDNA is retained only for the active browser invocation and is not authored/exported by A.
- One active integrated transaction; separate B session per target UID. Aggregate A5 acknowledgment is all-or-zero, not a distributed durable transaction.

## Authentication and unsupported capabilities

Controlled browser evidence assumes trusted same-origin assets, shell, loader and worker. It is not authenticated remote delivery or signed deployed-build attestation. Receipt fields and self-declared receiver names do not independently establish production trust. Process/realm-local capabilities cannot be exported and reimported as valid authentication; reload clears replay state. No global persistent replay ledger is claimed.

Unsupported: automatic external collection; production runner provenance certification without real evidence; authenticated remote receiver; durable sessions; cross-environment receipt verification; retry/remint controller; C execution; production deployment authorization.

The existing one-second controlled handoff timeout remains. Large inputs or multiple targets may safely block rather than accept late evidence. No timeout expansion or threshold tuning is authorized by this document.

## Deviations and approval boundary

No deviation from universal requirements is authorized here. Because canonical commit binding and requirement-by-requirement reconciliation remain incomplete, full compliance and potential conflicts are UNVERIFIED. Record any discovered conflict explicitly and obtain Operator authorization at the canonical level before changing implementation.

The worker-src self allowance is a documented deployment mechanism required for worker execution, not approval to weaken universal CSP requirements. Its canonical compatibility remains subject to deployed CSP verification.

## Verification and acceptance

Existing receipts: 20 integrated tests and three integrated production Chromium workflows; original 73 tests and six Chromium workflows; nine source-blob checks; clean remote shallow-checkout install/build/test. These are recorded implementation evidence, not newly rerun tests for this documentation-only change.

Final interface acceptance requires:

1. Confirm the now-identified canonical path/version, bind its exact authority commit SHA, and complete requirement-by-requirement reconciliation.
2. Protected source, serialization, authority and stage topology preservation checks.
3. Connected A1–B5 browser execution under deployment CSP, explicit external fixture/real-return distinction, all STOPs and seals, exact artifacts and permitted export.
4. Negative tests for missing/invalid inputs, decisions, continuity, untrusted/replayed/stale acknowledgment and unavailable execution.
5. Reproducible install/build and verified Vercel root/outside-root/source-access configuration.
6. Separate production trust evidence: real runner provenance, approved production receiving environment and authentic execution evidence. Local integration results do not satisfy that gate.

INTEGRATION and BUILD evidence remain valid within their stated local scope. Canonical interface acceptance is BLOCKED pending exact authority commit binding and full reconciliation. Production trust remains BLOCKED independently.

# MARA A1–B5 integrated application

**START HERE:** [operator Vercel preview entry point](../START-HERE-VERCEL.md). Select **`integrated`** as the Vercel Root Directory; `webapp`, `segment-a` and `src` are not the integrated deployment root.

**Operator starting point:** [START-HERE-VERCEL.md](../START-HERE-VERCEL.md). Vercel Root Directory is `integrated`; do not select `src`, `webapp`, or `segment-a`.

This independent build root reuses the existing A UI/core and loads the protected B commit directly from Git at build time. It does not copy or modify B validators. Runtime execution is browser-local in a dedicated worker; no server, external runner, persistence or remote authentication service is introduced.

```sh
cd integrated
npm ci
npm run prepare:source
npm test
npm run build
npm run verify:source
ENVOY_CHROMIUM_PATH=/usr/bin/chromium npm run test:e2e
```

Use `npm run dev` for development. Production preview uses port 4185. Node >=22.12 is required; verification used Node 24.19.0. Install Chromium via Playwright if system Chromium is unavailable.

The build must have Git and pinned B commit d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a. prepare:source checks it and the exact main protection baseline, fetching those exact commits if absent, never a floating branch. For a private shallow checkout without inherited Git access, set **MARA_B_SOURCE_TOKEN** as a build-only secret with read-only Contents access to this repository. The credential helper restricts credentials to the expected GitHub host/repository and never imports the token into browser code. Do not use a VITE_ secret variable. If source access is unavailable the build refuses, rather than substituting validators.

## Operator workflow

1. Open A1, supply the existing compiler definition and compile it.
2. Open A2 and prepare/copy the full dispatch JSON. Send it to the actual external runner yourself. No collection is simulated or automatic.
3. Open A3, associate source scope if appropriate, import returned text/Markdown or the existing returned JSON structure, then decide PUSH/PIN/REJECT. Missing taxonomy stays a blocker.
4. Open A4, supply run/candidate references and explicitly confirm commitment. Artifacts remain unchanged.
5. Refresh the committed transaction list in the integration panel, independently supply candidate.sdna.yaml, then certify A5 and validate B ingress.
6. A5 checks exact artifact versions and invocation-bound worker evidence before acknowledging browser-local ingress. B1 is READY; no B stage runs automatically.
7. Select the target UID. Explicitly run each B stage, inspect its status/refusals, and seal at each HARD STOP.
8. After B5 seal, explicitly verify/terminate/seal, then export only the sealed B handoff.

Refresh/reload loses all state. Reset requires A's existing confirmation; cancelled reset preserves the session. Accepted SDNA is locked to the receiving invocation. Multiple selected targets have separate B sessions; select each UID to complete it. One integrated transaction is active at a time; reset to start another.

## Vercel configuration

Select branch integration/a1-b5-vercel and **Root Directory integrated**. Include repository files outside the Root Directory in the build (required for the existing segment-a imports). Install `npm ci`, build `npm run build`, output `dist`, Node 24.x. No runtime environment variables are required. Build-only Git source access is required as described above.

The app serves at `/`; no client-side route rewriting is needed. Vite emits the module worker and assets under `/assets`. CSP adds `worker-src 'self'` to A's restrictions because B now runs in a worker; `connect-src 'none'` and other restrictions remain. No outbound runtime requests or exposed secrets are required.

Browser tests apply actual deployment CSP to the production document. Source acquisition occurs only during build. Hosting the static result does not establish authenticated production delivery or real runner provenance.

See [readiness and verification report](DEPLOYMENT-READINESS.md). No production deployment is authorized by this branch.

Deployment interface authority and platform controls are recorded in [INTERFACE-SPECIFICATION.md](INTERFACE-SPECIFICATION.md). The canonical document now exists on `main` at `docs/interface/MARA_INTERFACE_PREREQUISITES.md` (version 1.0). The deployment-specific specification still requires commit-SHA binding and requirement-by-requirement reconciliation before final interface acceptance; this draft does not override universal requirements.

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/integrated/pinned-source.ts

import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname,join,posix } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import type { Plugin } from 'vite';
export const root=dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
export const PIN='d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a';
const hash=(s:string)=>createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
const git=(...args:string[])=>execFileSync('git',args,{cwd:root,encoding:'utf8'});
export function buildIdentity(){
 const paths=git('ls-tree','-r','--name-only','--full-tree',PIN).trim().split('\n').filter(p=>p.startsWith('src/'));
 const closure=paths.map(p=>[p,git('show',`${PIN}:${p}`)]);
 const dependencyHash=hash(git('show',`${PIN}:package-lock.json`)+readFileSync(join(root,'package-lock.json'),'utf8'));
 return {validator:PIN,sourceHash:hash(JSON.stringify(closure)),dependencyHash,buildHash:hash(JSON.stringify(closure)+dependencyHash+['pinned-source.ts','vite.config.ts','src/worker.ts','src/receiver.ts'].map(p=>readFileSync(join(root,p),'utf8')).join('\n'))};
}
export function pinnedPlugin():Plugin {
 const prefix='\0pinned-b:';
 return {name:'approved-b-source',resolveId(source,importer){
  if(source==='mara-a-ui')return join(root,'../segment-a/src/main.ts')+'?integrated';
  if(source==='b-build:identity')return '\0b-build:identity';
  if(source.startsWith('pinned-b:'))return '\0'+source;
  if(importer?.startsWith(prefix)&&source.startsWith('.')){const p=posix.join(posix.dirname(importer.slice(prefix.length)),source);return prefix+p+(p.endsWith('.ts')?'':'.ts');}
 },load(id){
  if(id.endsWith('segment-a/src/main.ts?integrated'))return readFileSync(join(root,'../segment-a/src/main.ts'),'utf8')+'\nexport function getPipeline(){return pipeline;} export function refreshAUI(){render();}\n';
  if(id==='\0b-build:identity')return 'export const identity='+JSON.stringify(buildIdentity());
  if(id.startsWith(prefix))return git('show',`${PIN}:${id.slice(prefix.length)}`);
 },async transform(code,id){if(id.startsWith(prefix)){const {transformWithEsbuild}=await import('vite');const r=await transformWithEsbuild(code,'reference.ts',{loader:'ts',tsconfigRaw:{compilerOptions:{target:'ES2022'}}});return {code:r.code,map:null};}}};
}

// Build-only source acquisition. No secrets enter the browser bundle.
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { PIN } from '../pinned-source';
export const BASE='29c54dc2eff750c1d284e56775cb01e34de7fb9d';
for(const commit of [PIN,BASE]){
 try{execFileSync('git',['cat-file','-e',`${commit}^{commit}`],{stdio:'ignore'});}
 catch{try{
 const helper=fileURLToPath(new URL('./git-credential.ts',import.meta.url));
 const args=process.env.MARA_B_SOURCE_TOKEN?['-c','credential.helper=!node --import tsx '+JSON.stringify(helper),'-c','credential.useHttpPath=true']:[];
 execFileSync('git',[...args,'fetch','--no-tags','--depth=1','https://github.com/digital-Alchemy-mcmg/Envoy_Diplomat.git',commit],{stdio:'pipe',env:{...process.env,GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT:'0'}});
 }catch{throw new Error('Approved source unavailable: '+commit+'. Supply read-only Git access. No source substitute is permitted.');}}
}
console.log('Pinned B and protection baseline sources available.');

import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { BASE as base } from './prepare-source';
const changed=execFileSync('git',['diff','--name-only',base],{encoding:'utf8'}).trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean);
if(changed.some(p=>!p.startsWith('integrated/')))throw new Error('Protected source changed outside integrated/.');
const {readFileSync}=await import('node:fs');
const {createHash}=await import('node:crypto');
const manifest=JSON.parse(readFileSync('../segment-a/docs/source-manifest.json','utf8'));
for(const f of manifest.files){const bytes=readFileSync('../segment-a/'+f.target);const blob=createHash('sha1').update(`blob ${bytes.length}\0`).update(bytes).digest('hex');if(blob!==f.blob)throw new Error('Source drift: '+f.target);}
console.log('9/9 source blobs preserved.');
console.log('Integrated changes confined to integrated/; A/B/compiler/topology unchanged.');

// Shell-only progressive disclosure; original A handlers and state remain intact.

Reload loses this session.</p><p id="flow-status" role="status">Complete A1–A4 above, then explicitly certify and hand off below.</p><label>Independent candidate.sdna.yaml<input id="sdna" type="file" accept=".yaml"></label><label>Committed A transaction<select id="handoff-tx"></select></label><button id="refresh-tx">Refresh committed transactions</button><button id="handoff">Certify A5 and validate B ingress</button><p id="integration-refusal" role="alert"></p></section><section id="b-runtime" class="panel" hidden><h2>Segment B — explicit STOPs and seals</h2><ul id="b-doctrine"></ul><label>Target UID<select id="b-target"></select></label><p id="b-status" role="status"></p><button id="b-run" disabled>Run next stage</button><button id="b-seal" disabled>Seal at STOP</button><button id="b-finish" disabled>Verify, terminate and seal B envelope</button><button id="b-export" disabled>Export sealed B handoff</button><ol id="b-refusals"></ol></section>`;

let bridge=new BrowserBridge(),busy=false,acknowledged=false,sdna='';
function refresh(){const select=el<HTMLSelectElement>('handoff-tx');const old=select.value;select.replaceChildren(...getPipeline().view().transactions.filter(t=>t.state!=='ACCEPTED').map(t=>new Option(`${t.id} · ${t.state}`,t.id)));if([...select.options].some(o=>o.value===old))select.value=old;}
function render(){document.getElementById('app')!.inert=busy;const uid=el<HTMLSelectElement>('b-target').value;const view=bridge.views.find(v=>v.uid===uid)?.view;el('b-runtime').hidden=!acknowledged;el<HTMLButtonElement>('handoff').disabled=busy||acknowledged;el<HTMLInputElement>('sdna').disabled=busy||acknowledged;el<HTMLButtonElement>('b-run').disabled=busy||!acknowledged||!view?.ready;el<HTMLButtonElement>('b-seal').disabled=busy||!acknowledged||!view?.stop;el<HTMLButtonElement>('b-finish').disabled=busy||!acknowledged||view?.progression.T06!=='READY'||view?.failed;el<HTMLButtonElement>('b-export').disabled=busy||!acknowledged||!view?.complete;el('b-run').textContent=view?.ready?`Run ${view.ready}`:'Run next stage';el('b-seal').textContent=view?.stop?`Seal ${view.stop} at STOP`:'Seal at STOP';el('b-status').textContent=view?.complete?'B5 complete; verification and termination passed. Sealed B handoff ready.':view?.failed?'REFUSED — reset required.':view?.stop?`${view.stop} HARD STOP. Explicit seal required.`:view?.ready?`${view.ready} READY. Awaiting Operator execution.`:'Awaiting verification.';el('b-refusals').replaceChildren(...(view?.refusals??[]).map((r:any)=>{const li=document.createElement('li');li.textContent=`${r.stage} / ${r.code} / ${r.resource}: ${r.reason} (${r.authority})`;return li;}));}
async function action(fn:()=>Promise<void>){if(busy)return;busy=true;el('integration-refusal').textContent='';render();try{await fn();}catch(e){el('integration-refusal').textContent=String(e);}finally{busy=false;render();}}

el('handoff').onclick=()=>void action(async()=>{const id=el<HTMLSelectElement>('handoff-tx').value;if(!id)throw new Error('No committed A4 transaction selected.');bridge.close();bridge=new BrowserBridge();bridge.sdna=sdna;const tx=await getPipeline().handoff(id,bridge);refreshAUI();if(tx.state!=='ACCEPTED')throw new Error(tx.failures.map(f=>`${f.resource}: ${f.reason}`).join('\n'));acknowledged=true;el<HTMLSelectElement>('b-target').replaceChildren(...bridge.views.map(v=>new Option(v.uid,v.uid)));el('flow-status').textContent='A5 verified browser-local ingress acknowledgment. B1 READY; no B stage ran.';});
for(const [button,kind] of [['b-run','RUN'],['b-seal','SEAL'],['b-finish','FINISH'],['b-export','EXPORT']] as const)el(button).onclick=()=>void action(async()=>{if(!acknowledged)throw new Error('A5 acknowledgment required.');const uid=el<HTMLSelectElement>('b-target').value;const view=bridge.views.find(v=>v.uid===uid)!.view;const result=await bridge.command(uid,kind==='RUN'?{action:kind,stage:view.ready}:kind==='SEAL'?{action:kind,stage:view.stop}:{action:kind});if(result.envelope){const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([result.envelope],{type:'application/json'}));const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='SEALED_B_ENVELOPE.json';a.click();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),0);}});
// Original A reset owns its confirmation. Detect its completed state change;
// cancel leaves B intact. No persistence or automatic restoration.
const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(acknowledged&&!getPipeline().view().transactions.length){acknowledged=false;bridge.close();bridge=new BrowserBridge();sdna='';el<HTMLInputElement>('sdna').value='';el('flow-status').textContent='Session reset. Supply new independent inputs.';render();}});
observer.observe(document.getElementById('transactions')!,{childList:true});
refresh();render();

import { identity } from 'b-build:identity';
import { currentLocalAttempt,issueLocalEvidence } from '../../segment-a/src/core/local-evidence';
import type { Receiver,HandoffReceipt,Pair } from '../../segment-a/src/core/model';
export class BrowserBridge implements Receiver {
 readonly worker:Worker;
 #requests=new Map<string,{resolve:(v:any)=>void;reject:(e:Error)=>void;timer:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>}>();
 views:{uid:string;view:any}[]=[];
 constructor(){this.worker=new Worker(new URL('./worker.ts',import.meta.url),{type:'module'});this.worker.onmessage=e=>{const p=this.#requests.get(e.data.id);if(!p)return;clearTimeout(p.timer);this.#requests.delete(e.data.id);e.data.error?p.reject(new Error(e.data.error)):p.resolve(e.data);};this.worker.onerror=()=>this.close();}
 sdna='';
 request(data:Record<string,unknown>):Promise<any>{return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const id=crypto.randomUUID();const timer=setTimeout(()=>{this.#requests.delete(id);reject(new Error('Browser B execution timed out.'));},1000);this.#requests.set(id,{resolve,reject,timer});this.worker.postMessage({...data,id});});}
 async receive(transactionId:string,pairs:readonly Pair[]):Promise<HandoffReceipt>{
  if(!this.sdna.trim())throw new Error('Independent candidate.sdna.yaml is required.');
  const attempt=currentLocalAttempt(this);const result=await this.request({type:'INGRESS',attempt,transactionId,pairs,sdna:this.sdna});
  if(result.attempt!==attempt||result.transactionId!==transactionId||JSON.stringify(result.identity)!==JSON.stringify(identity))throw new Error('Unverified worker invocation/build identity.');
  const receipt={transactionId,receiver:'BROWSER_LOCAL_PINNED_B_WORKER',artifacts:result.artifacts};
  issueLocalEvidence(this,receipt,{...identity,attempt,transactionId,environment:`browser-local:${location.origin}`,timestamp:result.timestamp,sdnaHash:result.sdnaHash,results:result.results});
  this.views=result.results;return receipt;
 }
 async command(uid:string,command:Record<string,unknown>){const r=await this.request({type:'COMMAND',uid,command});const entry=this.views.find(v=>v.uid===uid);if(entry)entry.view=r.response.view;return r.response;}
 close(){this.worker.terminate();for(const p of this.#requests.values()){clearTimeout(p.timer);p.reject(new Error('Browser B execution unavailable.'));}this.#requests.clear();}
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/integrated/src/worker.ts
------
import { SegmentBSession } from 'pinned-b:src/runtime/session.ts';
import { handleCommand } from 'pinned-b:src/runtime/commands.ts';
import { identity } from 'b-build:identity';
const sessions=new Map<string,InstanceType<typeof SegmentBSession>>();
const hash=async(s:string)=>Array.from(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',new TextEncoder().encode(s)))).map(b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
let queue=Promise.resolve();
self.onmessage=event=>{queue=queue.then(async()=>{
 const m=event.data;
 try{
 if(m.type==='INGRESS'){
  const results=[],artifacts=[];sessions.clear();
  for(const p of m.pairs){const session=new SegmentBSession();const response=await handleCommand(session,{action:'INGRESS',files:[{name:'SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json',text:p.envelope},{name:'STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json',text:p.sidecar},{name:'candidate.sdna.yaml',text:m.sdna}]});sessions.set(p.uid,session);results.push({uid:p.uid,view:response.view});artifacts.push({uid:p.uid,envelopeHash:await hash(p.envelope),sidecarHash:await hash(p.sidecar),accepted:response.view.progression.INGRESS==='PASS'&&response.view.ready==='B1'&&!response.view.failed,reason:JSON.stringify(response.view.refusals)});}
  self.postMessage({id:m.id,attempt:m.attempt,transactionId:m.transactionId,identity,sdnaHash:await hash(m.sdna),results,artifacts,timestamp:new Date().toISOString()});
 }else{const session=sessions.get(m.uid);if(!session)throw new Error('No acknowledged B session for UID.');self.postMessage({id:m.id,uid:m.uid,response:await handleCommand(session,m.command)});}
 }catch(e){self.postMessage({id:m.id,error:String(e)});}
 });};

import {it,expect,vi,afterEach} from 'vitest';
import {BrowserBridge} from '../src/receiver';
import {identity} from 'b-build:identity';
import {SegmentA} from '../../segment-a/src/core/pipeline';
import {returned} from '../../segment-a/tests/fixtures';
let mutation=(r:any)=>r;
class FakeWorker{onmessage:any;onerror:any;terminate(){}postMessage(m:any){queueMicrotask(()=>{const r={id:m.id,attempt:m.attempt,transactionId:m.transactionId,identity,sdnaHash:'a'.repeat(64),timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),results:[],artifacts:m.pairs.map((p:any)=>({uid:p.uid,envelopeHash:p.envelopeHash,sidecarHash:p.sidecarHash,accepted:true}))};this.onmessage({data:mutation(r)});});}}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/README.md
------
# MARA Segment A — Structural Revision 2

Independent A1–A5 application under `segment-a/`. Existing repository files,
legacy A implementations in their source repository, B1–B5, C and PR #2 remain
unchanged. The controlling directive is [Operator Revision 2](docs/operator-directive-r2.txt).

```sh
cd segment-a
npm ci
npm run dev
```

A1 uses the unchanged Scout compiler and 25-point validator. Fill the existing
definition fields; repeated invocations retain independent certified outputs.
A2 displays/selects/copies the complete JSON. Optional batch/runner fields use
the existing dispatch adapter for that invocation only. Prior displayed outputs
remain recallable. Folder/automated transports are visible and disabled.

A3 imports `.txt`/`.md` files or pasted text, including the existing JSON
`RunnerOutput.observations[]` / `ObservationArtifact` / `TravelingEnvelope` return
structures. UID/Observation_ID headers delineate plain records. Unmarked text is
kept as one unresolved record; the Operator may explicitly assign its UID.
Descriptions are not rewritten or semantically analyzed. Original payloads and
exact source bytes remain available; only display line endings are cleaned.
Duplicates remain visible. REJECT requires confirmation and is irreversible in
the session. PIN exports a lossless plain-text session file for A3 reimport.

A3→A4 commitment requires a warning and explicit confirmation. A4 snapshots the
PUSH selection, source corpus and original payloads. It never clears A3. Duplicate
selected UIDs, missing identity or exclusion conflicts refuse rather than alter
selections. One artifact pair is minted per target UID, using B's existing field
contract. Operator supplies run_id, candidate_id reference and pipeline_version;
this is not candidate SDNA. Raw SDNA is never created, parsed or exported by A.

A5 locally verifies artifact versions, identities, target/source hashes and B
structure. Qualified requirements come only from an already-returned target
fact_table; A does not classify plain descriptions to fill missing taxonomy.
Plain-text-only jobs can be staged and committed, but missing taxonomy blocks
B1 compatibility. Locally verified pairs have separate envelope/sidecar download
paths. Downloads are preparation for manual testing, not acknowledged handoff.

**Live B1 transmission remains disabled.** Frozen B has no established receipt
channel for exact transmitted artifact versions. The receiver seam is exercised
only in isolated tests. A5 accepts a transaction only if every UID and both hashes
are acknowledged as accepted by that receiver. Missing, partial, rejected,
duplicate, wrong-transaction or wrong-version acknowledgments give zero accepted
and preserve all committed artifacts. No Operator checkbox can fabricate receipt.

No database, automatic browser storage, draft persistence, server endpoint or
runtime network transport is added. Reset and navigation warn about losing the
ephemeral session. PIN export/artifact files must be manually saved.

```sh
npm run verify:source
npm test
npm run build
npx playwright install chromium
npm run test:e2e
```

Tests read B directly from its pinned Git commit
`d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a`; no B source file is modified. A clone
must contain that commit. If absent, fetch the B branch explicitly before running
contract tests. A1–A5 production code does not include B's SDNA parser/runtime.
Existing system Chromium may be selected using `ENVOY_CHROMIUM_PATH`.

For a separately approved Vercel deployment set **Root Directory = segment-a**,
install `npm ci`, build `npm run build`, output `dist`. The local `vercel.json`
provides static hosting and a CSP forbidding network connections. Root repository
workspace/build settings are intentionally untouched. HTTPS static hosting is
also possible. No deployment, merge or B transmission is performed here.

See [reuse and compatibility](docs/reuse-and-contracts.md),
[unresolved interfaces](docs/unresolved-contracts.md) and
[change manifest](docs/change-manifest.md).

Plan 02 adds the read-only gate register, complete sidecar source/exclusion accounting and archived PIN scope validation. See [Plan 02 data-flow and gate report](docs/plan02-data-flow-and-gate-report.md) for final addresses, junctions, fresh verification and separately reported milestones. Production B handoff remains unaccepted.

# Change manifest — A1–A5 structural Revision 2

Branch: a1-a5-structural-r2. Base: Envoy_Diplomat main
57f9d054a9c0ddf3c1cfed70086a03c509f9e378.

Implementation commit: f464b67d6d15f7ff24ac46ceee818e25ae75b707.
The subsequent receipt-only commit records this reference; it changes no runtime
or test behavior. The branch is local and has not been pushed, merged or deployed.

All added files are under segment-a/. Existing root/workspace, webapp, legacy B,
B standalone branch/PR #2, C, compiler source repository and legacy A repository
are unchanged. This document accompanies the implementation commit; use
`git log --oneline -- segment-a` for exact implementation commit references.

| Added surface | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| package.json, package-lock.json, tsconfig.json, vite.config.ts, index.html, vercel.json | Independent clean TypeScript/Vite build and proposed static deployment root |
| src/vendor/scout/*.ts; src/vendor/types.ts; src/vendor/b-ingress/*.ts | Nine byte-identical reused files, pinned by docs/source-manifest.json |
| src/core/model.ts | Session-only structural state and provisional test receiver seam |
| src/core/imports.ts | Existing return-contract ingestion, text/Markdown delineation, exact source preservation and PIN reimport |
| src/core/pipeline.ts | Compiler/dispatch routes, operator decisions, safe commitment, minting and all-or-zero acknowledgment gate |
| src/core/certification.ts | Local B structure, provenance and exact artifact-version validation |
| src/main.ts, src/style.css | Five-stage UI, manual copy/download, review controls and explicit destructive confirmation dialogs |
| tests/*.ts, playwright.config.ts, vitest.config.ts | Stage, frozen-B contract and browser verification; reference B source read from Git in memory |
| scripts/verify-source.ts | Independent source-blob preservation checks and legacy/B/C change guard |

# MARA-INTEGRATION-05 — controlled local execution receipt

Result: **LOCAL INTEGRATION VERIFIED**. Date: October 13, 2026, approximately 4:27 p.m. America/New_York (20:27 UTC). No production acceptance claimed.

## 01 — Implementation inventory

Isolated branch `integration/a5-b1-trusted-handoff`, base `84f08b27c719bbc7fbefa6735fb7446fe846c47e`. Original A and B worktrees remain clean. Protected B and compiler refs reconfirmed remotely at `d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a` and `191cf1c04c2ac36a2577f2938cb33ca9ba2d2564`.

| Changed file | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| src/core/pipeline.ts | A5 handoff only: create invocation challenge and verify local execution capability before existing acknowledgment checks |
| src/core/local-evidence.ts | Process-local origin, integrity, freshness, exact-version and single-use verification |
| src/integration/local-receiver.ts | Harness-only Receiver adapter invoking unchanged pinned SegmentBSession.ingress |
| vitest.config.ts | Extend existing in-memory Git loader with source/transformed-module execution measurements |

| tests/protection.test.ts | Exact A1–A4 implementation/serialization and protected module comparisons |
| tests/pipeline.test.ts | Preserve historical compatibility cases, now refuse unauthenticated test receivers |
| docs/integration-05-receipt.md | This report |

No dependency changes. Existing installed dependencies reused; no fresh clean-install claim. Browser UI remains disabled for live transmission. The bridge runs through existing Vitest infrastructure, not an invented service or browser deployment.

## 02 — Actual interface trace

Existing fixture return → unchanged A3 staging/PUSH → unchanged A4 commit → A5 handoff → Receiver.receive(transactionId,pairs) → independently supplied test SDNA → fresh pinned SegmentBSession → ingress(files) → actual SessionView → exact-byte receiver hashes and execution record → A5 verifyLocalEvidence → existing all-pairs acknowledgment checks → ACCEPTED locally.

Each pair uses a fresh B session. No next-stage execution/sealing occurs. Multi-target acceptance is all-or-zero at A5; independent B sessions are not a distributed production transaction. No A4 string is parsed/reserialized by the bridge; strings are passed directly to B, which performs its own unchanged parsing.

## 03 — Evidence mechanism and actual sample

Each A5 call creates a random attempt UUID, separate from session-local transaction IDs. A process-local WeakMap binds the receiver object, actual returned receipt object, immutable serialized receipt snapshot, execution record and one-use status. Altered or copied JSON is not a valid capability. Prior-attempt or consumed capabilities cannot authorize a new execution; timestamp freshness is bounded by the existing one-second local wait.

The pinned loader reads unchanged B source with git show. Harness instrumentation outside B records evaluated module paths, original source hashes and transformed-code hashes. Original bytes are independently compared with the pinned Git source. The identity also measures B/local lockfiles, installed yaml, installed execution tooling, loader and harness source, plus Node runtime version. Measured identity must match the harness's captured approved identity before B invocation. The receiver hashes the actual submitted strings and retains actual SessionView/refusals. A5 verifies capability origin, transaction, invocation, freshness, intact receipt and complete exact-version bindings before acceptance.

This is a controlled-process trust boundary: the harness, loader and code executing inside the process are trusted. Issuance helpers are internal integration APIs, not exposed receipt endpoints. Malicious code with execution inside this trusted process can subvert that boundary; this is not sandbox isolation or production cryptographic authentication. Merely supplying plausible receipt fields or a receiver string does not satisfy the verifier.

Actual recorded fixture execution:

- Dependency SHA-256: 86bc124e25a4dd715c39ce2691c9b7c5868c82922825ee01c47e82f4edd098a8

CODEX PLAN 01
MARA SEGMENT A — A1 THROUGH A5
STRUCTURAL CONSTRUCTION DIRECTIVE
REVISION 2

MISSION
Build a functional A1–A5 pipeline by assembling existing components, preserving established contracts, and implementing the missing structural connections.

Deployment target: Vercel.
Target result: A working Segment A that prepares and delivers the required artifacts to the existing B1 ingress.

AUTHORITY AND SCOPE
- Operator retains final architectural authority.
- Codex implements and reports findings.
- Existing compiler and runner contracts are preserved.
- B1–B5 is an existing downstream system.
- C1–C5 is an existing downstream Resume Factory.
- No redesign, refactoring, or modification of B or C is authorized.
- No assumption that existing A code already follows the new five-stage boundaries.
- Do not remove legacy functionality merely because its stage numbering differs.
- Identify reusable existing functionality before creating replacements.
- No unapproved merges to main.

EXISTING SYSTEM RELATIONSHIP

A = Search preparation, inbound staging, state minting, and certified handoff.

B = Existing Spatial DNA processing and validation pipeline.

C = Existing Resume Factory.

A → B → C is the established system direction.

This assignment concerns A only.

B and C are not implementation targets.

The existing B1 ingress contract is the acceptance target for A5.

A1 — SEARCH-SCOPE COMPILER

Reuse the existing compiler.

The operator enters:
- SOC and occupational information
- Job categories and search targets
- Prerequisites
- Search parameters
- Other fields already supported by the compiler

The compiler produces one search-scope JSON artifact per invocation.

The same compiler can be used repeatedly for different search scopes.

Do not bind a compiler instance permanently to a runner.

Do not redesign the existing compiler's internal schema.

A2 — OUTBOUND DISPATCH

Receive the JSON produced by A1.

Provide three dispatch endpoints:

1. Manual display and copy
   ENABLED
   Display the complete JSON in a selectable text area.

2. Folder destination
   VISIBLE BUT DISABLED
   Reserve the interface for a future configured destination.

3. Automated transport
   VISIBLE BUT DISABLED
   Reserve an interface for API, Zapier, or other transport.

Do not implement external runner execution.

Do not require runner connectivity for A1 or A2 to function.

The outbound search JSON should preserve existing batch identity, search parameters, and runner return instructions wherever the existing contracts support them.

EXTERNAL RUNNER BOUNDARY

Runners operate outside Segment A.

They receive search instructions through the operator's chosen transport.

They perform job searches and return results.

Segment A must not require a particular runner vendor, execution environment, or transport.

A3 — INBOUND STAGING

Create an operator-controlled staging workspace.

Accept:
- Plain-text uploads
- Markdown uploads
- Pasted plain text
- Multiple incoming runner payloads

Use the existing return contracts where available.

Perform limited preparation:
- Delineate individual job records
- Associate records with job UIDs
- Perform non-destructive text cleaning
- Identify duplicates
- Organize source material into a navigable text corpus

Do not conduct Spatial DNA analysis.

Do not score job suitability.

Do not rewrite descriptions to match B.

Do not invent missing source information.

The interface displays individual jobs with sufficient identifying information for operator review.

Supported states:

PUSH
Job selected for the next commitment.

PIN
Job retained for later consideration.

REJECT
Requires explicit confirmation.
After confirmation, mark excluded, dim the row, and prevent restoration within the current staging session.

PENDING
Job remains available for review.

The sidecar staging corpus preserves returned evidence and records exclusions.

The workspace is intentionally ephemeral.

Do not introduce a database or automatic draft-persistence subsystem.

Provide a plain-text export for pinned jobs.

The export must preserve enough original content, UID identity, and context for reimport through A3.

The operator is responsible for saving the exported text.

A3 COMMITMENT

The operator explicitly commits the selected PUSH jobs.

Before a destructive transition:
- Warn that staging state may be lost.
- Identify what is being preserved.
- Identify what must be manually saved.
- Require affirmative operator confirmation.

Cancellation must leave the workspace unchanged.

Do not clear staging before the receiving stage has successfully committed the transferred state.

A4 — STATE RESOLUTION AND MINTING

Receive A3's committed selections and staged evidence.

Resolve:
- Selected job UIDs
- Source references
- Batch identity
- Operator decisions
- Exclusion status
- Relevant search-scope context

Mint the traveling envelope.

Finalize the sidecar representation for the committed transaction.

The traveling envelope carries operational state, navigation information, UID references, and information needed for downstream processing and UI control.

The sidecar carries source evidence for detailed lookup.

Every selected UID must resolve to the correct sidecar record.

Rejected records must not become active envelope entries.

Do not infer missing evidence.

Do not silently change operator selections.

A5 — CERTIFICATION AND HANDOFF

Receive the committed envelope and sidecar.

Validate:
- Artifact identity
- Required fields
- UID correspondence
- Source provenance
- Exclusion integrity
- Sidecar references
- Content integrity
- Compatibility with the existing B1 ingress contract

Prepare two independent outbound artifact paths:

Traveling envelope → B1 envelope ingress
Sidecar → B1 sidecar ingress

Spatial DNA is an independent Operator-supplied input to B1.

Segment A must not create, transform, or export candidate SDNA.

A5 owns the handoff transaction until B1 confirms receipt and acceptance of the exact transmitted artifact versions.

A5 local validation alone is insufficient.

Acceptance rule:

100% VERIFIED = ACCEPTED
ANYTHING LESS = ZERO ACCEPTED

On mismatch, incomplete transfer, rejection, or missing acknowledgment:
- Block completion.
- Preserve the committed artifacts.
- Record the exact failure.
- Do not report successful handoff.
- Do not silently repair the artifacts.

B1–B5 and C1–C5 remain unchanged.

DESTRUCTIVE TRANSITION POLICY

Every destructive operation requires an explicit warning and confirmation.

Never silently discard operator decisions or source material.

A failed downstream commitment must not clear the upstream state.

IMPLEMENTATION ORDER

First inspect the existing A implementation and identify reusable components.

Then build the five-stage structural shells and data routes.

Implement the A1/A2 manual compiler-to-JSON path.

Implement A3 inbound staging and operator controls.

Implement A4 artifact minting.

Implement A5 validation and handoff interfaces.

Discover actual data contracts during construction.

Keep unresolved interfaces explicitly identified.

Do not invent permanent schemas to fill unknowns.

Do not activate B1 transmission until actual contract compatibility has been verified.

TESTING

Demonstrate:
- Repeated compiler invocations
- Independent JSON outputs
- Manual JSON copy
- Multiple runner payload imports
- Variable-length job descriptions
- UID delineation
- Duplicate detection
- Push, pin, reject, and pending states
- Rejection confirmation
- Pinned-job export and reimport
- Destructive-transition warning
- A4 envelope and sidecar minting
- UID and exclusion reconciliation
- A5 mismatch blocking
- A5 acknowledgment handling

DELIVERABLES

Working A1–A5 structural implementation.

Stage-by-stage test results.

Existing-component reuse inventory.

Unresolved contract register.

B1 compatibility findings.

Change manifest with file paths and commit references.

Do not claim completion without test evidence.

END PLAN 01

GATE NOTATION

Component>G1: = Egress
Component<G1: = Ingress
Component_G1: = Bidirectional

Each component maintains one local gate counter.

Counters begin at G1.

Ingress and egress share the same local sequence.

Reserved endpoints consume gate addresses.

Never reuse a gate address on the same component.

Gate addresses identify endpoints, not artifact ownership or provenance.

PROPOSED JUNCTION INVENTORY

The following are logical connections.

Assign final addresses only after component inventory and endpoint ordering are confirmed.

J01

J14

J16

These junction IDs identify logical connections, not assigned gate numbers.

IMPORTANT ROUTING DISTINCTION

A2's manual-copy endpoint is the currently enabled delivery mechanism.

J06 describes the external logical delivery route, not an implemented automatic runner connection.

Do not activate J06 as a software transport merely because A2 displays JSON.

Similarly, B1's actual gate addresses must be verified from its existing implementation.

Do not invent physical B1 endpoints.

A3 DATA PRESERVATION

Runner payloads may contain descriptions of different lengths.

Preserve actual returned content.

Do not require uniform description lengths.

Cleaning may normalize presentation but must not invent facts or erase provenance.

Duplicate handling must retain sufficient identity to trace repeated source records.

The sidecar is the supporting source-evidence corpus.

The envelope is the operational state and navigation artifact.

The envelope references evidence through UIDs and source associations.

A4 STATE MINTING

A4 converts A3's committed decisions into authoritative artifact state.

Only committed PUSH jobs become active downstream selections.

PIN and PENDING remain outside the committed active set.

REJECT records are excluded.

Every active envelope UID must resolve to a corresponding sidecar record.

The envelope must preserve relevant batch and search-scope identity.

The sidecar must preserve evidence required by the existing B1 contract.

Do not assume B1 accepts a particular job cardinality without inspecting its contract.

A5 VALIDATION

Check:
- Local gate uniqueness
- Correct direction
- Component identity
- Complete endpoint accounting
- Required artifact fields
- UID correspondence
- Sidecar exclusion markings
- Provenance and batch identity
- Artifact integrity
- Existing B1 contract compatibility

Gate Validation Doctrine:

GV-01 — Single local gate counter
GV-02 — Direction must be evidenced
GV-03 — Classify before allocating
GV-04 — Complete source accounting
GV-05 — Separate extraction from certification
GV-06 — Preserve branch semantics
GV-07 — Component identity fidelity

Treat these as general validation rules, not historical model-specific corrections.

A5 → B1 HANDSHAKE

A5 performs local certification.

A5 initiates the handoff.

B1 receives the exact transmitted artifacts.

B1 verifies the received artifact identities and integrity according to its existing contract.

B1 returns acceptance or rejection.

A5 retains transaction ownership until verified acceptance.

An acknowledgment of connection alone is not acceptance.

Partial transmission is not acceptance.

A mismatch is not acceptance.

Missing acknowledgment is not acceptance.

The transaction must be traceable to the exact transmitted artifact versions.

100% ACCEPTED OR 0% ACCEPTED.

No partial release.

No silent repair.

No false completion receipt.

If B1's current implementation lacks a compatible acknowledgment mechanism, report the integration gap.

Do not modify B to manufacture compliance.

B AND C AUTHORITY BOUNDARIES

B1–B5 are existing completed components.

C1–C5 are existing completed components.

Their internal behavior is not part of this assignment.

The existing B1 ingress contract determines the accepted A5 output shapes.

Do not specify or alter B's internal sidecar disposal point.

Do not redesign Spatial DNA processing.

Do not alter Resume Factory input policy.

The present assignment ends at B1's verified acceptance.

CONTRACT DISCOVERY

During construction, inspect:
- Existing compiler types and schemas

- Existing UID generation
- Sidecar and envelope representations

Separate confirmed facts from proposed adaptations.

Preserve upstream contracts whenever possible.

No source-data fabrication.

NO-BREAK BUILD POLICY

Build structural pipes first.

Do not modify existing upstream contracts to make an unfinished pipeline appear compatible.

Do not modify B or C.

ACCEPTANCE EVIDENCE

[Source manifest](source-manifest.json) records all nine independent Git blob hashes. Source verification passed 9/9 and confirms tracked legacy/B/C paths unchanged. Pinned [B protocol](https://github.com/digital-Alchemy-mcmg/Envoy_Diplomat/blob/d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a/src/runtime/protocol.ts) returns WorkerResponse `{view,envelope?}`; this is not an A transaction receipt acknowledging both input version hashes. Its worker/session ingress feedback does not establish such a receipt.

## Component contracts and boundaries

| Component | Ingress → egress | Ownership, dependency and refusal boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A1 | Existing DiskDefinition → certified Cartridge | Frozen compileDisk/validateCartridge; invalid definition/certification refuses. Repeated invocations retain independent outputs |
| A2 | Stored compiler invocation → full manual JSON display/copy | Existing optional batch adapter; folder/automation remain disabled. Display/copy does not run external software |
| A3 | Runner text/Markdown or saved PIN text → confirmed decisions and exact evidence | Existing returned structures preserve UID/source/batch/runner context. Duplicate identity, malformed return, hash mismatch or exclusion bypass refuses; PIN/PENDING/REJECT are not active PUSH targets |
| A4 | Explicitly confirmed PUSH selection plus corpus/payloads → one envelope/sidecar pair per UID | Confirmation/revision guard, identity/source checks and original qualified taxonomy. No auto decomposition; commit preserves staging and hashes exact serialized artifacts |
| A5 | Committed pair versions plus committed source snapshot → locally certified downloads; test-only receipt evaluation | Independent certification, topology and source accounting. All selected pairs must have exact-version acceptance or zero accepted; no repair or rewrite |

Stage boundaries here are operator-controlled transitions in one stateless browser application, not independently deployed APIs. In particular A3→A4 decisions and source evidence are separate logical fields carried by the same commit call. Extraction/minting and certification remain separate. A artifact completion markers do not mean B acceptance; A5 production transmission is disabled. A does not create or consume candidate SDNA.

## Gate inventory

One uninterrupted counter per A component, starting G1, shared across directions. Disabled reservations consume their numbers. Direction classification precedes allocation. Addresses are logical, not invented physical endpoints.

| Component | Final addresses and roles |
| --- | --- |
| A1 | A1<G1: configuration; A1>G2: certified search JSON |
| A2 | A2<G1: search JSON; A2>G2: manual display/copy; A2>G3: reserved folder; A2>G4: reserved automation |
| A3 | A3<G1: returned result; A3<G2: PIN reimport; A3>G3: decisions; A3>G4: source evidence; A3>G5: PIN export |
| A4 | A4<G1: decisions; A4<G2: source evidence; A4>G3: envelope; A4>G4: sidecar |
| A5 | A5<G1: envelope; A5<G2: sidecar; A5>G3: certified envelope; A5>G4: certified sidecar; A5_G5: bidirectional test-only handoff control |

A5_G5 reflects request and response through Receiver.receive, not a confirmed public B endpoint. No external runner or B gate numbers are allocated. Runtime declarations and source-symbol evidence: [topology.ts](../src/core/topology.ts). UI exposes this inventory read-only.

| Junction | Source → destination | Status |
| --- | --- | --- |
| J01 | Operator → A1<G1 | Local |
| J02 | A1>G2 → A2<G1 | Local |
| J03 | A2>G2 → Operator display/copy | Local |
| J04 | A2>G3 → future folder | Reserved, disabled |
| J05 | A2>G4 → future automation | Reserved, disabled |
| J06 | Manual A2>G2 output → external runner | External unverified; manual operator action |
| J07 | Returned external result → A3<G1 | Local import verified; runner execution unverified |
| J08 | Saved PIN text → A3<G2 | Local |
| J09 | A3>G3 → A4<G1 | Local |
| J10 | A3>G4 → A4<G2 | Local |
| J11 | A4>G3 → A5<G1 | Local |
| J12 | A4>G4 → A5<G2 | Local |
| J13 | A5>G3 → existing B envelope ingress | Isolated test only; physical gate undeclared |
| J14 | A5>G4 → existing B sidecar ingress | Isolated test only; physical gate undeclared |
| J15 | Independent Operator SDNA → existing B SDNA ingress | Isolated test only; outside A |
| J16 | B acknowledgment → A5_G5 | Test seam only; production mechanism absent |
| J17 | A3>G5 → Operator saving → J08 | Local; completes accounting of existing PIN export |

J17 accounts for a preexisting Plan 01 endpoint omitted from the suggested J01–J16 list. It does not add an external service.

GV-01 checks unique consecutive shared counters; GV-02 checks directions/routes; GV-03 checks classification/source evidence; GV-04 checks complete, nonduplicate endpoint/junction accounting; GV-05 is enforced by independent certification without altering artifact bytes; GV-06 preserves reserved/test-only branch semantics; GV-07 preserves component ownership. Mutation tests cover invalid counters, directions, classification, missing/duplicate/moved endpoints, route reversal and unauthorized promotion.

## A5 → existing B compatibility

“B1 ingress” denotes the existing B system boundary before pure B1. B's three-file validation receives SDNA; pure B1 remains target-only. Neither target/candidate stage blindness nor B semantics is changed.

| Artifact | Producer / format / validator | Evidence and remaining unknown |
| --- | --- | --- |
| SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json | A4, B existing envelope shape; A5 validatePair/validateTransaction and frozen B acceptThreeFiles/executeIngress | Actual generated bytes accepted locally for supported qualified return; production exact-version receipt absent |
| STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json | A4, B existing sidecar fields plus provisional A_staging corpus extension; same validators | Actual extension accepted by unchanged B. Formal external extension contract not established |
| candidate.sdna.yaml | Operator or independent candidate producer, never A; unchanged B parser/gate | Separate test-receiver fixture; real candidate provenance and transfer outside A remain Operator-owned |

Frozen B has one target_id/raw_posting per invocation. A therefore mints a pair per PUSH UID; aggregate all-or-zero acknowledgment is A-side test behavior. A multi-target production receiver transaction is unresolved.

Newly minted A_staging records account for every PUSH/PIN/PENDING/REJECT record, exclusion/activity flags, exact raw source/hash, references, batch/runner and certified search-scope association. Original payloads and their hashes are retained. Only the selected UID is active in each pair; other PUSH records are inactive in that pair and REJECT is always excluded/inactive. A5 compares this against the committed corpus and envelope provenance/audit trail. Tampering tests recompute outer artifact hashes, demonstrating that underlying integrity checks still refuse. This extension is explicitly an A-side adaptation permitted by B's existing extensible type and observed validator behavior, not a new permanent upstream runner schema. Prior artifact versions are never rewritten.

New PIN exports preserve archived compiler scope certification across reset/reimport. Older exports without that archive do not acquire invented historical context. Plain descriptions without authoritative qualified fact_table cannot pass A5; thresholds/taxonomy are not manufactured.

## Fresh verification and receipts

Final run: October 13, 2026, 08:23:40 UTC onward, Node 24.19.0, npm 11.9.0, system Chromium / Playwright 1.56.1.

| Check | Actual result |
| --- | --- |

| npm run build | Strict TypeScript and production Vite build passed |

| Clean install | Plan 01 receipt records executed clean install; dependencies unchanged in Plan 02; not falsely reported as rerun |

Tests exercise real unchanged B ingress with actual A4 strings and separate SDNA, including a real candidate identity refusal. Test receipt acceptance binds exact sent versions. Connection-only, missing, partial, duplicate, rejected, wrong-transaction/version, transport-error and timeout responses all yield zero accepted while preserving artifacts. Browser tests verify manual routes, disabled paths, read-only inventory, cancellation/rejection/PIN behavior, local downloads and missing-taxonomy refusal under deployment CSP. Tests are local evidence, not real vendor delivery or production handoff.

Detailed receipts: [verification receipt](verification-receipt.md); tests: [pipeline](../tests/pipeline.test.ts), [topology](../tests/topology.test.ts), [browser](../tests/workflow.e2e.ts).

## Deployment assessment and decisions

Vercel-compatible static build: branch a1-a5-structural-r2, Root Directory segment-a, npm ci, npm run build, output dist. Existing repository root workspace settings are untouched. Static HTTPS hosting is an alternative. CSP forbids runtime network connections; activating any real transport requires separately established contract and authorized configuration, not deployment alone.

Blockers and Operator decisions:

- Supply authoritative production receiver endpoint/adapter, authentication, exact-version receipt and timeout/retry rules; confirm atomic multi-pair acceptance. Existing B WorkerResponse is insufficient. Keep B/C unchanged.
- Establish real runner delivery/returned formats and provenance. Manual copy/import is not an automatic runner connection.
- Supply authoritative qualified taxonomy for prose-only returns; A will not infer it.
- Confirm external acceptance/version policy for provisional A_staging, despite successful unchanged-B local validation. Do not silently turn it into a permanent producer schema.
- Preserve B-U001: wall-clock timestamps prevent deterministic full serialized B envelopes. No timestamp substitution or B repair performed.

Completion receipt: local A construction, gate/accounting validation, evidence-preserving certification, negative acknowledgment checks, source verification, browser verification and production build performed. Branch remains local. No push, PR creation, main/B/C change, merge, settings change, live runner, live B transmission or deployment performed. Production handoff remains blocked; no additional implementation of external contracts follows from local test success.

# Existing-component reuse and B1 compatibility

Authority: Operator Plan 01 Revision 2, supplied 2026-10-12. New structural stage
names supersede the earlier reconnaissance's stage-number assumption. They do
not rename/delete legacy A0–A11 code or modify its source repository.

## Reuse inventory

| Component | Source | Use / preservation |
| --- | --- | --- |
| compileDisk / validateCartridge / hashing / source normalization | A-A2_multicompiler @ 191cf1c04c2ac36a2577f2938cb33ca9ba2d2564, webapp/src/components/compiler.ts | Byte-identical; authoritative A1 path; hash/dedup helpers reused in A3/A4/A5 |
| Compiler, runner and traveling-envelope types | Same ref, types.ts | Byte-identical; existing definition/return shapes retained |
| toScoutIngressEnvelope | Same ref, batch-ingress-adapter.ts | Byte-identical; optional per-dispatch batch/runner metadata, limits and return instructions retained |
| batch-compiler.ts | Same ref | Byte-identical type dependency; its permanent batch request shape is not imposed on compiler invocation |
| observation-artifacts.ts / decomposition-engine.ts | Same ref | Byte-identical type closure; actual artifact builder exercised in tests. A never invokes decomposition on staged prose |
| hydrateEnvelope / hydrateSidecar / B types | Envoy_Diplomat @ d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a | Byte-identical copies for A5 local structural checks; original B files and behavior unchanged |
| Legacy Python A0–A11 package | maraSEGMENTS/segment/A @ d50abda722aa4e6222e7a5e6d2ca1c9176271db0, STAGE_A_YAML_FIRST.zip | Inspected during reconnaissance. Not substituted for R2's compiler/staging stages; not modified or deleted. Its SDNA processing is outside new A's authority |

The source manifest records independent Git blob IDs for all nine reused files.
`verify:source` checks exact bytes and ensures tracked changes stay in segment-a/.
There is no Amazon/package/archive dependency in the standalone package.

## Data routes and authority

- A1 produces a certified Cartridge through the frozen compiler. It is never
  connected to a permanent runner. A2 optionally invokes the original batch
  ingress adapter, recording exact output JSON in session dispatch history.
- A3 accepts existing returned envelope/ObservationArtifact structures. Their
  observation_id and canonical Observation_ID must agree; section_8_source's
  SHA-256 must match its exact decoded source_text. Batch/runner context is copied
  if supplied. Search invocation association is explicitly Operator-selected.
- Plain/Markdown UID headings are delineation affordances, not a new runner
  contract. Metadata absent from text remains absent. Exact input payloads are
  retained independently from cleaned display text.
- Existing returned fact_table requirement/preference qualifiers route verbatim
  into B's hard/soft requirements arrays. No decomposition, suitability score,
  SDNA operation or target-label rewriting runs in A. No returned keyword lexicon
  was established in this source; B permits its empty array.
- A4 emits one pair per selected UID because frozen B has one target_id and one
  raw_posting per invocation. It preserves operational provenance in existing
  history/audit_trail extension fields, retains the full corpus/payloads in the
  committed session snapshot, and emits only the selected target as raw_posting.
- B's Z_state fields are Operator references, not inferred. Optional SDNA manifest
  hash is absent because A has no candidate SDNA authority in R2. B verifies its
  independent candidate input using its unchanged gate. This does not resolve
  legacy A/B manifest canonicalization differences.

## Handoff boundary

Outputs use B's existing SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json and STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json shapes;
Operator supplies candidate.sdna.yaml directly to B. Source status strings are
the existing B fixture's A_COMPLETE / complete:HANDOFF_TO_B. They designate a
prepared A artifact, not a successful B receipt; UI transaction state stays
COMMITTED, LOCALLY_VERIFIED or BLOCKED until real acceptance.

Local checks reuse B hydration and validate required strings/taxonomy arrays,
run/UID correspondence, source provenance and exact serialized version hashes.
Tests feed actual A4-generated bytes through frozen B acceptThreeFiles and
executeIngress, with SDNA present only in the test receiver. A separate test uses
the unchanged existing runner artifact builder before A3→A4→B ingress.

No transmission implementation is activated. The provisional Receiver interface
is a test seam, not a new public endpoint or permanent receipt schema. An exact
receipt must account for every selected UID and both sent hashes, with no missing,
duplicate or rejected entry. The test seam has a bounded one-second acknowledgment
wait; live receiver timeout/authentication policy remains unresolved.

## Failure and destructive-transition policy

REJECT is explicitly confirmed and cannot be undone or bypassed via UID/source
duplicates. Excluded evidence stays in the corpus. Commit requires a current
warning token and affirmative confirmation; selection edits invalidate that
warning. A4 failure does not replace/clear staging. A5 failure retains committed
versions. Reset requires a warning about all evidence/output loss and manual
saving; cancellation leaves workspace state unchanged. Browser navigation uses
the browser's beforeunload protection; custom wording is browser-dependent.

## Plan 02 follow-up

The selected target remains the top-level raw_posting. Newly minted sidecars additionally retain the complete committed corpus and payloads in provisional A_staging, including inactive/excluded records. A5 independently validates that extension against the committed source snapshot. New PIN exports archive certified scope context; legacy exports without it are not repaired. See the Plan 02 report for compatibility limits.
