# Unresolved contract register

| ID | Interface / finding | Current behavior | Operator decision / source needed |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A-R01 | Live B1 transport and acknowledgment | Disabled; no transmission or success claim. Only isolated tests supply a receiver | Existing endpoint or approved external adapter, authentic receiver acknowledgment with exact bytes/hashes, authorization and retry/timeout rules. B/C remain unchanged |
| A-R02 | Plain-text return taxonomy | Raw source and decisions work; A4 can commit; A5 refuses missing authoritative hard requirements | Actual runner-return contract containing qualified target facts or an approved authoritative taxonomy producer. A will not classify/rewrite prose to make B pass |
| A-R03 | Runner wire-format scope | Existing RunnerOutput / ObservationArtifact / TravelingEnvelope supported as returned text; UID headings support manual delineation | Additional real vendor return contracts, delimiters and field mappings must be source-backed before activation |
| A-R04 | PIN export representation | Lossless .txt encoding of ephemeral staging records; reimport preserves UID, raw source and context. No runner contract is replaced | Approve long-term portable format if desired. Current export is a session affordance, not a permanent schema |
| A-R05 | Multiple selected targets | Independent pair per UID; acceptance must cover all pairs atomically | Receiver transaction correlation/cardinality must be confirmed by actual transport contract |
| A-R06 | Scope/batch/run identity | Compiler's existing identifiers and batch adapter preserved; A3 context explicitly selected; A4 identifiers Operator-supplied | External identity-allocation and multi-run uniqueness policy; no IDs are inferred from missing evidence |
| A-R07 | Legacy A artifact/schema mismatch | Old A0–A11 sources remain unchanged; R2 explicitly builds new structural connections | No wholesale legacy migration or schema replacement is authorized. Additional legacy adapter requires source-backed approval |
| B-U001 | Frozen B timestamps | Preserved: semantic bindings/hashes repeat, full B envelope bytes do not | Separate architecture decision; A does not modify B or fix U-001 |

Local B structure acceptance is not proof of an external acknowledgment channel,
signed provenance, real runner execution, or production A→B→C completion. No
permanent receipt schema or cryptographic signing protocol is invented here.

A-R08 — Newly minted sidecars include a provisional A_staging source-corpus extension. Frozen B accepts this in local tests; formal external schema/version acceptance is unresolved. Existing B contracts and prior artifact bytes are unchanged.

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/scripts/verify-source.ts

import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';

const manifest = JSON.parse(await readFile('docs/source-manifest.json','utf8'));
for(const f of manifest.files){
  const bytes=await readFile(f.target);
  const blob=createHash('sha1').update(`blob ${bytes.length}\0`).update(bytes).digest('hex');
  if(blob!==f.blob)throw new Error(`Source drift: ${f.target} from ${f.repository}@${f.ref}:${f.source}`);
}
const changed=execFileSync('git',['diff','--name-only','57f9d054a9c0ddf3c1cfed70086a03c509f9e378'],{encoding:'utf8'}).trim().split('\n').filter(Boolean);
if(changed.some(p=>!p.startsWith('segment-a/')))throw new Error('Legacy/B/C surface changed outside segment-a/.');
console.log(`${manifest.files.length} reused files are byte-identical to pinned source blobs; tracked legacy/B/C paths unchanged.`);

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/core/certification.ts

import { hydrateEnvelope } from '../vendor/b-ingress/envelope';
import { hydrateSidecar } from '../vendor/b-ingress/sidecar';
import { sha256, canonicalStringify, validateCartridge } from '../vendor/scout/compiler';
import { required, type Pair, type Refusal, type Committed } from './model';
import { validateGates, validateJunctions } from './topology';

export async function validatePair(pair: Pair): Promise<Refusal[]> {
  const failures: Refusal[] = [];
  const fail = (resource: string, reason: string) => failures.push({ stage: 'A5', resource, reason });
  let e: any, s: any;
  try { e = JSON.parse(pair.envelope); hydrateEnvelope(e); } catch (error) { fail('envelope', String(error)); }
  try { s = JSON.parse(pair.sidecar); hydrateSidecar(s); } catch (error) { fail('sidecar', String(error)); }
  if (e && s) {
    try {
      for (const k of ['run_id', 'candidate_id', 'target_id', 'pipeline_version', 'current_stage', 'status']) required(e.Z_state?.[k], `Z_state.${k}`);
      if (!e.A_scout?.target_entity || typeof e.A_scout.target_entity !== 'object' || Array.isArray(e.A_scout.target_entity)) throw new Error('A_scout.target_entity must be an object.');
      const t = e.A_scout.requirements_taxonomy;
      for (const k of ['hard_requirements', 'soft_requirements', 'keyword_lexicon']) {
        if (!Array.isArray(t?.[k]) || !t[k].every((v: unknown) => typeof v === 'string' && v.trim())) throw new Error(`requirements_taxonomy.${k} must contain only nonempty strings.`);
      }
      if (!t.hard_requirements.length) throw new Error('No authoritative hard requirements were returned. A will not infer them from source text.');
      if (e.Z_state.target_id !== pair.uid || s.run_binding.target_id !== pair.uid) throw new Error('UID correspondence mismatch.');
      if (e.Z_state.run_id !== pair.runId || s.run_binding.run_id !== pair.runId) throw new Error('Run identity mismatch.');
      const source = await sha256(s.raw_posting);
      if (source !== s.original_content_sha256 || source !== e.A_scout.original_content_sha256) throw new Error('Three-way source provenance mismatch.');
      if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(e.A_scout.original_content_sha256)) throw new Error('Source hash is not lowercase SHA-256.');
      if (await sha256(pair.envelope) !== pair.envelopeHash || await sha256(pair.sidecar) !== pair.sidecarHash) throw new Error('Committed artifact version integrity mismatch.');
    } catch (error) { fail(pair.uid, error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)); }
  }
  return failures;
}

// GV-05: topology classification/allocation and source extraction are separate
// from certification. This validator checks captured state without rewriting it.
export async function validateTransaction(tx: Committed): Promise<Refusal[]> {
  const failures: Refusal[] = [...validateGates(), ...validateJunctions()].map(f => ({ stage:'A5', resource:f.resource, reason:`${f.rule}: ${f.reason}` }));
  const fail = (resource:string,reason:string) => failures.push({stage:'A5',resource,reason});
  failures.push(...(await Promise.all(tx.pairs.map(validatePair))).flat());
  const selected=tx.corpus.filter(j=>j.decision==='PUSH');
  const keys=new Set(tx.corpus.map(j=>j.key));
  const uids=new Set(selected.map(j=>j.uid));
  if(!selected.length || selected.length!==tx.pairs.length || uids.size!==selected.length || keys.size!==tx.corpus.length || tx.pairs.some(p=>selected.filter(j=>j.uid===p.uid).length!==1))fail(tx.id,'UID and selection reconciliation failed.');
  const payloadRefs=new Set(tx.payloads.map(p=>p.ref));
  if(payloadRefs.size!==tx.payloads.length)fail(tx.id,'Source payload references are ambiguous.');
  for(const job of tx.corpus){
    if(await sha256(job.raw)!==job.sourceHash)fail(job.key,'Captured corpus source hash mismatch.');
    if(!job.sourceRefs.some(ref=>payloadRefs.has(ref)))fail(job.key,'No current-session payload resolves the source references.');
    if(job.scopeInvocation && !tx.scopes.some(s=>s.invocation===job.scopeInvocation))fail(job.key,'Search-scope association does not resolve.');
  }
  for(const pair of tx.pairs){
    try {
      const e=JSON.parse(pair.envelope),s=JSON.parse(pair.sidecar);
      const job=selected.find(j=>j.uid===pair.uid);
      if(!job)throw new Error('Active envelope UID has no committed PUSH source.');
      if(s.raw_posting!==job.raw || s.original_content_sha256!==job.sourceHash)throw new Error('Sidecar target is not the selected UID source.');
      const staging=s.A_staging;
      if(!Array.isArray(staging?.records) || staging.records.length!==tx.corpus.length || !Array.isArray(staging.payloads) || staging.payloads.length!==tx.payloads.length)throw new Error('Sidecar corpus/source accounting is incomplete.');
      const active=staging.records.filter((r:any)=>r.active===true);
      if(active.length!==1 || active[0].key!==job.key || active[0].uid!==pair.uid || active[0].decision!=='PUSH' || active[0].excluded!==false)throw new Error('Sidecar active UID/exclusion marking mismatch.');
      const recordKeys=new Set<string>();
      for(const r of staging.records){
        if(recordKeys.has(r.key))throw new Error('Duplicate sidecar corpus key.');recordKeys.add(r.key);
        const source=tx.corpus.find(j=>j.key===r.key);
        if(!source || r.uid!==source.uid || r.decision!==source.decision || r.excluded!==(source.decision==='REJECT') || r.active!==(source.key===job.key))throw new Error('Sidecar decision/exclusion integrity mismatch.');
        if(r.raw_posting!==source.raw || r.source_sha256!==source.sourceHash || await sha256(r.raw_posting)!==r.source_sha256)throw new Error('Sidecar corpus source integrity mismatch.');
        if(canonicalStringify(r.source_references)!==canonicalStringify(source.sourceRefs) || r.batch_id!==source.batchId || r.runner_id!==source.runnerId || r.search_scope_invocation!==(source.scopeInvocation ?? source.archivedScope?.invocation ?? null))throw new Error('Sidecar source/batch/search identity mismatch.');
      }
      const seenPayloads=new Set<string>();
      for(const p of staging.payloads){
        if(seenPayloads.has(p.ref))throw new Error('Duplicate sidecar payload reference.');seenPayloads.add(p.ref);
        const original=tx.payloads.find(o=>o.ref===p.ref);
        if(!original || p.raw!==original.raw || await sha256(p.raw)!==p.content_sha256)throw new Error('Sidecar returned payload integrity mismatch.');
      }
      const h=e.history?.[0];
      if(h?.stage!=='A4' || h.target_id!==pair.uid || h.batch_id!==job.batchId || h.runner_id!==job.runnerId || canonicalStringify(h.source_references)!==canonicalStringify(job.sourceRefs))throw new Error('Envelope provenance/batch identity mismatch.');
      const scope=job.scopeInvocation ? tx.scopes.find(s=>s.invocation===job.scopeInvocation) : job.archivedScope;
      if(canonicalStringify(h.search_scope)!==canonicalStringify(scope?.cartridge??null))throw new Error('Envelope search-scope identity mismatch.');
      if(scope){const v=await validateCartridge(structuredClone(scope.cartridge));if(!v.passed || v.certified_cartridge?.certification.content_hash!==scope.cartridge.certification.content_hash)throw new Error('Captured search scope is not compiler-certified.');}
      const audit=tx.corpus.map(j=>({target_id:j.uid,decision:j.decision,source_sha256:j.sourceHash}));
      if(canonicalStringify(e.audit_trail)!==canonicalStringify(audit))throw new Error('Envelope exclusion/decision audit mismatch.');
      if(tx.corpus.some(j=>j.decision==='REJECT' && (j.uid===pair.uid || j.sourceHash===job.sourceHash)))throw new Error('Rejected evidence is active.');
    }catch(error){fail(pair.uid,error instanceof Error?error.message:String(error));}
  }
  return failures;
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/core/imports.ts
------
import { sha256, validateCartridge } from '../vendor/scout/compiler';
import { required, type Job } from './model';

type Draft = Omit<Job, 'key' | 'decision' | 'duplicateOf' | 'scopeInvocation'>;
const text = (v: unknown): string => typeof v === 'string' ? v : '';

async function runnerObservation(value: unknown, sourceRef: string, batchId: string | null, runnerId: string | null): Promise<Draft> {
  const artifact = value as Record<string, any>;
  const envelope = artifact?.envelope ?? artifact;
  const scout = envelope?.payload?.scout;
  const identity = envelope?.target_identity;
  const canonical = artifact?.canonical ?? scout?.path_a_structured;
  const raw = scout?.section_8_source?.source_text;
  required(raw, 'Returned envelope payload.scout.section_8_source.source_text');
  required(identity?.observation_id, 'Returned observation_id');
  if (canonical?.Observation_ID && canonical.Observation_ID !== identity.observation_id) throw new Error('Returned UID conflicts with canonical Observation_ID.');
  const sourceHash = await sha256(raw);
  if (scout.section_8_source.source_hash !== `sha256:${sourceHash}`) throw new Error('Returned source_hash does not match the exact source_text.');
  const facts = scout.path_b_semantic?.fact_table;
  let taxonomy: Draft['taxonomy'] = null;
  if (Array.isArray(facts)) {
    for (const f of facts) {
      required(f?.text, 'Returned fact_table text');
      if (!['requirement', 'preference', 'availability'].includes(f.qualifier)) throw new Error('Unsupported returned fact qualifier. No A classification is inferred.');
    }
    // Route existing qualified target facts verbatim; never decompose raw text here.
    taxonomy = {
      hard_requirements: facts.filter(f => f.qualifier === 'requirement').map(f => f.text),
      soft_requirements: facts.filter(f => f.qualifier === 'preference').map(f => f.text),
      keyword_lexicon: [],
    };
  }
  return {
    uid: identity.observation_id, employer: text(identity.employer || canonical?.Employer),
    title: text(identity.job_title || canonical?.Job_Title), sourceUrl: text(canonical?.Source_URL),
    raw, display: raw.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n'), sourceHash, sourceRefs: [sourceRef], batchId, runnerId, taxonomy, archivedScope:null,
  };
}

export async function delineate(payload: string, sourceRef: string): Promise<Draft[]> {
  required(payload, 'Runner payload');
  if (payload.startsWith('# MARA pinned text export\n')) {
    const saved = JSON.parse(payload.slice('# MARA pinned text export\n'.length));
    if (!Array.isArray(saved)) throw new Error('Pinned text export must contain a record list.');
    return Promise.all(saved.map(async (r: Draft) => {
      required(r.raw, 'Pinned original source'); required(r.uid, 'Pinned UID');
      if (await sha256(r.raw) !== r.sourceHash) throw new Error('Pinned source integrity mismatch.');
      if (!Array.isArray(r.sourceRefs) || !r.sourceRefs.every(s => typeof s === 'string')) throw new Error('Pinned source references are malformed.');
      for (const key of ['employer', 'title', 'sourceUrl'] as const) if (typeof r[key] !== 'string') throw new Error(`Pinned ${key} is malformed.`);
      if (r.taxonomy !== null) {
        for (const key of ['hard_requirements', 'soft_requirements', 'keyword_lexicon'] as const) {
          if (!Array.isArray(r.taxonomy?.[key]) || !r.taxonomy[key].every(s => typeof s === 'string' && s.trim())) throw new Error('Pinned taxonomy is malformed.');
        }
      }
      if(r.archivedScope){
        required(r.archivedScope.invocation,'Saved search invocation');
        const checked=await validateCartridge(structuredClone(r.archivedScope.cartridge));
        if(!checked.passed || checked.certified_cartridge?.certification.content_hash!==r.archivedScope.cartridge.certification.content_hash)throw new Error('Pinned search-scope certification integrity mismatch.');
      }
      return { ...r, archivedScope:r.archivedScope??null, display: r.raw.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n'), sourceRefs: [...r.sourceRefs, sourceRef] };
    }));
  }
  const trimmed = payload.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith('{') || trimmed.startsWith('[')) {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed);
    // Reuse RunnerOutput.observations[] and existing TravelingEnvelope structures.
    const containers = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [parsed];
    const result: Draft[] = [];
    for (const c of containers) {
      const observations = Array.isArray(c?.observations) ? c.observations : [c];
      for (const o of observations) result.push(await runnerObservation(o, sourceRef, text(c.batch_id) || null, text(c.runner_id) || null));
    }
    return result;
  }
  // Plain-text/Markdown delineation is structural only. Explicit UID headers
  // separate records; unmarked input is retained as one unresolved record.
  const headers = [...payload.matchAll(/^(?:#{1,6}\s+)?(?:UID|Observation_ID):[ \t]*(.+)[ \t]*\r?$/gm)];
  const blocks = headers.length ? headers.map((h, i) => ({
    uid: h[1].trim(), block: payload.slice(h.index!, headers[i + 1]?.index ?? payload.length),
  })) : [{ uid: null, block: payload }];
  return Promise.all(blocks.map(async ({ uid, block }) => {
    const meta = (key: string) => block.match(new RegExp(`^${key}:[ \\t]*(.*)\\r?$`, 'm'))?.[1]?.trim() ?? '';
    const description = /^Description:[ \t]*\r?\n/m.exec(block);
    const raw = description ? block.slice(description.index + description[0].length) : block;
    required(raw, 'Original job source');
    return {
      uid, employer: meta('Employer'), title: meta('Job_Title'), sourceUrl: meta('Source_URL'),
      batchId: meta('Batch_ID') || null, runnerId: meta('Runner_ID') || null,
      raw, display: raw.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n'), sourceHash: await sha256(raw), sourceRefs: [sourceRef], taxonomy: null, archivedScope:null,
    };
  }));
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/core/local-evidence.ts
------
// Controlled-process capability registry. Not a network authentication protocol.
import type { Receiver, HandoffReceipt, Pair } from './model';
export interface ExecutionEvidence {
  attempt: string; transactionId: string; environment: string; timestamp: string;
  validator: string; sourceHash: string; dependencyHash: string; buildHash: string;
  sdnaHash: string; results: unknown[];
}
const pending = new WeakMap<Receiver,string>();
const issued = new WeakMap<HandoffReceipt,{receiver:Receiver; snapshot:string; evidence:ExecutionEvidence; used:boolean}>();
export function beginLocalAttempt(receiver:Receiver):string {
  const attempt=crypto.randomUUID(); pending.set(receiver,attempt); return attempt;
}
// Only the trusted local harness calls issuance; code inside this process is trusted.
export function currentLocalAttempt(receiver:Receiver):string {
  const attempt=pending.get(receiver); if(!attempt)throw new Error('No active A5 invocation.'); return attempt;
}
export function issueLocalEvidence(receiver:Receiver,receipt:HandoffReceipt,evidence:ExecutionEvidence):void {
  issued.set(receipt,{receiver,snapshot:JSON.stringify(receipt),evidence:structuredClone(evidence),used:false});
}
export function verifyLocalEvidence(receiver:Receiver,receipt:HandoffReceipt,attempt:string,transactionId:string,pairs:readonly Pair[]):ExecutionEvidence {
  const record=issued.get(receipt);
  if(!record || record.receiver!==receiver)throw new Error('Unverified execution origin; controlled local harness evidence required.');
  if(record.used)throw new Error('Replayed execution evidence.');
  record.used=true;
  const e=record.evidence;
  if(pending.get(receiver)!==attempt || e.attempt!==attempt || e.transactionId!==transactionId)throw new Error('Wrong invocation/transaction execution evidence.');
  if(JSON.stringify(receipt)!==record.snapshot)throw new Error('Execution receipt integrity mismatch.');
  const age=Date.now()-Date.parse(e.timestamp);
  if(!Number.isFinite(age) || age<0 || age>1000)throw new Error('Stale execution evidence.');
  if(e.validator!=='d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a' || ![e.sourceHash,e.dependencyHash,e.buildHash,e.sdnaHash].every(h=>/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(h)))throw new Error('Invalid validator/build identity evidence.');
  if(receipt.transactionId!==transactionId || receipt.artifacts.length!==pairs.length)throw new Error('Incomplete or wrong-transaction execution acknowledgment.');
  for(const p of pairs){const a=receipt.artifacts.filter(a=>a.uid===p.uid);if(a.length!==1 || a[0].envelopeHash!==p.envelopeHash || a[0].sidecarHash!==p.sidecarHash)throw new Error('Exact artifact execution binding mismatch.');}
  return structuredClone(e);
}

// Session-only structural types. No new permanent runner or wire schema.
import type { Cartridge, ValidationResult } from '../vendor/scout/types';
export type Decision = 'PENDING' | 'PUSH' | 'PIN' | 'REJECT';
export interface ScopeRun { invocation: string; cartridge: Cartridge; validation: ValidationResult }
export interface Job {
  key: string; uid: string | null; employer: string; title: string; sourceUrl: string;
  raw: string; display: string; sourceHash: string; sourceRefs: string[];
  batchId: string | null; runnerId: string | null; scopeInvocation: string | null;
  archivedScope: ScopeRun | null;
  decision: Decision; duplicateOf: string | null;
  taxonomy: { hard_requirements: string[]; soft_requirements: string[]; keyword_lexicon: string[] } | null;
}
export interface Refusal { stage: string; resource: string; reason: string }
export interface Pair {
  uid: string; runId: string; envelope: string; sidecar: string;
  envelopeHash: string; sidecarHash: string;
}
export interface Committed {
  id: string; pairs: Pair[]; corpus: Job[];
  payloads: { ref: string; raw: string }[];
  scopes: ScopeRun[];
  state: 'COMMITTED' | 'LOCALLY_VERIFIED' | 'AWAITING_ACK' | 'ACCEPTED' | 'BLOCKED';
  failures: Refusal[];
}
export interface HandoffReceipt {
  // Provisional adapter seam; no deployed receiver contract has been established.
  transactionId: string; receiver: string;
  artifacts: { uid: string; envelopeHash: string; sidecarHash: string; accepted: boolean; reason?: string }[];
}
export interface Receiver {
  receive(transactionId: string, pairs: readonly Pair[]): Promise<HandoffReceipt>;
}
export function required(value: unknown, field: string): asserts value is string {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim()) throw new Error(`${field} is missing; A will not invent it.`);
}


================================================================================
FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/core/pipeline.ts

import { beginLocalAttempt, verifyLocalEvidence } from './local-evidence';
import { compileDisk, validateCartridge, sha256, computeDedupKey } from '../vendor/scout/compiler';
import { toScoutIngressEnvelope } from '../vendor/scout/batch-ingress-adapter';
import type { DiskDefinition } from '../vendor/scout/types';
import { delineate } from './imports';
import { validateTransaction } from './certification';
import { required, type ScopeRun, type Job, type Decision, type Committed, type Pair, type Receiver, type Refusal } from './model';

export class SegmentA {
  #scopes: ScopeRun[] = [];
  #jobs: Job[] = [];
  #payloads: { ref: string; raw: string }[] = [];
  #dispatches: { id: string; invocation: string; json: string }[] = [];
  #transactions: Committed[] = [];
  #refusals: Refusal[] = [];
  #revision = 0;
  #busy = false;
  #commitWarning: { token: string; revision: number } | null = null;

  view() { return structuredClone({ scopes: this.#scopes, dispatches: this.#dispatches, jobs: this.#jobs, transactions: this.#transactions, refusals: this.#refusals, payloads: this.#payloads }); }
  #fail(stage: string, resource: string, reason: string): never {
    this.#refusals.push({ stage, resource, reason }); throw new Error(reason);
  }
  #editable() { if (this.#busy) this.#fail('A3', 'workspace', 'A receiving commitment is in progress. Staging cannot change.'); }

  async compile(definition: DiskDefinition): Promise<ScopeRun> {
    const source = structuredClone(definition);
    let validation;
    try { validation = await validateCartridge(compileDisk(source)); }
    catch (error) { return this.#fail('A1', 'DiskDefinition', error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)); }
    if (!validation.passed || !validation.certified_cartridge) return this.#fail('A1', 'compiler validation', validation.checks.filter(c => !c.passed).map(c => `${c.id}: ${c.name}`).join('; '));
    const run: ScopeRun = { invocation: `scope-${this.#scopes.length + 1}`, cartridge: validation.certified_cartridge, validation };
    this.#scopes.push(structuredClone(run));
    return structuredClone(run);
  }

  dispatch(invocation: string, binding?: { batchId: string; runnerId: string }): string {
    const run = this.#scopes.find(s => s.invocation === invocation);
    if (!run) return this.#fail('A2', invocation, 'Unknown compiler invocation.');
    if (binding) { required(binding.batchId, 'Batch identity'); required(binding.runnerId, 'Dispatch runner identity'); }
    // Per-dispatch binding only, using the unchanged existing adapter.
    const json = binding ? JSON.stringify(toScoutIngressEnvelope(binding.batchId, [{ runner_id: binding.runnerId, disk_id: run.cartridge.definition.id, compiled_cartridge: structuredClone(run.cartridge), validation: structuredClone(run.validation) }]), null, 2) : JSON.stringify(run.cartridge, null, 2);
    this.#dispatches.push({ id: `dispatch-${this.#dispatches.length + 1}`, invocation, json });
    return json;
  }

  async importPayload(raw: string, name: string, scopeInvocation: string | null = null): Promise<void> {
    this.#editable();
    if (scopeInvocation && !this.#scopes.some(s => s.invocation === scopeInvocation)) return this.#fail('A3', name, 'Operator-selected search context is unknown.');
    const revision = this.#revision;
    let drafts;
    const sourceRef = `payload-${this.#payloads.length + 1}:${name}`;
    try { drafts = await delineate(raw, sourceRef); }
    catch (error) { return this.#fail('A3', name, error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)); }
    this.#editable();
    if (revision !== this.#revision) return this.#fail('A3', name, 'Workspace changed during import; retry without discarding existing state.');
    const imported: Job[] = [];
    for (const d of drafts) {
      const existing = [...this.#jobs, ...imported];
      const dedup = d.sourceUrl && d.employer ? await computeDedupKey(d.sourceUrl, d.employer) : null;
      let duplicate: Job | undefined;
      for (const j of existing) {
        const sameSource = j.sourceUrl && j.employer && dedup === await computeDedupKey(j.sourceUrl, j.employer);
        if ((d.uid && d.uid === j.uid) || d.sourceHash === j.sourceHash || sameSource) { duplicate = j; break; }
      }
      const rejected = existing.some(j => j.decision === 'REJECT' && ((d.uid && j.uid === d.uid) || j.sourceHash === d.sourceHash));
      imported.push({ ...d, key: `job-${this.#jobs.length + imported.length + 1}`, scopeInvocation,
        decision: rejected ? 'REJECT' : raw.startsWith('# MARA pinned text export\n') ? 'PIN' : 'PENDING', duplicateOf: duplicate?.key ?? null });
    }
    this.#editable();
    if (revision !== this.#revision) return this.#fail('A3', name, 'Workspace changed during import; nothing was replaced.');
    this.#jobs.push(...imported); this.#payloads.push({ ref: sourceRef, raw }); this.#revision++;
  }

  assignUid(key: string, uid: string) {
    this.#editable(); required(uid, 'Operator UID');
    const job = this.#jobs.find(j => j.key === key); if (!job) return this.#fail('A3', key, 'Unknown job.');
    if (job.decision === 'REJECT') return this.#fail('A3', key, 'A rejected record cannot be restored or altered in this session.');
    if (this.#jobs.some(j => j.decision === 'REJECT' && j.uid === uid)) return this.#fail('A3', key, 'This UID was rejected and cannot be restored within the session.');
    if (job.uid && job.uid !== uid) return this.#fail('A3', key, 'An existing source UID cannot be silently replaced.');
    job.uid = uid; this.#revision++;
  }

  decide(key: string, decision: Decision, confirmed = false) {
    this.#editable();
    if (!['PENDING', 'PUSH', 'PIN', 'REJECT'].includes(decision)) return this.#fail('A3', key, 'Unknown decision.');
    const job = this.#jobs.find(j => j.key === key); if (!job) return this.#fail('A3', key, 'Unknown job.');
    if (job.decision === 'REJECT') return this.#fail('A3', key, 'Rejection is irreversible within the current staging session.');
    if (decision !== 'REJECT' && this.#jobs.some(j => j.decision === 'REJECT' && ((job.uid && j.uid === job.uid) || j.sourceHash === job.sourceHash))) return this.#fail('A3', key, 'Equivalent rejected evidence cannot be restored through a duplicate row.');
    if (decision === 'REJECT' && !confirmed) return this.#fail('A3', key, 'Rejection requires an explicit warning and affirmative confirmation.');
    job.decision = decision; this.#revision++;
  }

  exportPinned(): string {
    const jobs = this.#jobs.filter(j => j.decision === 'PIN').map(j=>({ ...j,
      archivedScope: structuredClone(this.#scopes.find(s=>s.invocation===j.scopeInvocation)??j.archivedScope??null) }));
    jobs.forEach(j => required(j.uid, 'Pinned UID'));
    // Lossless plain-text session export, not a new permanent runner schema.
    return '# MARA pinned text export\n' + JSON.stringify(jobs, null, 2);
  }

  prepareCommit(): { token: string; warning: string } {
    this.#editable();
    const token = `commit-${this.#revision}`;
    this.#commitWarning = { token, revision: this.#revision };
    return { token, warning: 'Committing transfers only PUSH jobs to A4. Original evidence, decisions, exclusions and unselected jobs will remain in this session until a separately confirmed reset. This workspace is ephemeral and will be lost when closed or reset. Export PIN jobs and save them manually before leaving.' };
  }

  cancelCommit() { this.#commitWarning = null; }

  async commit(token: string, confirmed: boolean, identity: { runId: string; candidateId: string; pipelineVersion: string }): Promise<Committed> {
    this.#editable();
    if (!confirmed || !this.#commitWarning || token !== this.#commitWarning.token || this.#revision !== this.#commitWarning.revision) return this.#fail('A3', 'commit', 'A current warning and affirmative Operator confirmation are required.');
    required(identity.runId, 'Operator run_id'); required(identity.candidateId, 'Operator candidate_id reference (not SDNA)'); required(identity.pipelineVersion, 'Operator pipeline_version');
    const corpus = structuredClone(this.#jobs);
    const selected = corpus.filter(j => j.decision === 'PUSH');
    if (!selected.length) return this.#fail('A4', 'selection', 'No PUSH jobs were selected.');
    const uids = new Set<string>();
    for (const job of selected) {
      required(job.uid, 'Selected UID');
      if (uids.has(job.uid)) return this.#fail('A4', job.uid, 'Duplicate selected UID. No selection was silently changed.');
      if (this.#transactions.some(t => t.pairs.some(p => p.runId === identity.runId && p.uid === job.uid))) return this.#fail('A4', job.uid, 'This run/UID is already committed. Use an explicit new run identity.');
      if (corpus.some(j => j.decision === 'REJECT' && (j.uid === job.uid || j.sourceHash === job.sourceHash))) return this.#fail('A4', job.uid, 'Selected evidence conflicts with a rejected record.');
      uids.add(job.uid);
    }
    this.#busy = true;
    try {
      const pairs: Pair[] = [];
      // Plan 02 source accounting. This is an A-side corpus extension, not a
      // new required B schema. Top-level B source text remains one target.
      const payloadEvidence = await Promise.all(this.#payloads.map(async p => ({ ...p, content_sha256: await sha256(p.raw) })));
      for (const job of selected) {
        if (await sha256(job.raw) !== job.sourceHash) throw new Error(`Source integrity mismatch for ${job.uid}.`);
        const scope = this.#scopes.find(s => s.invocation === job.scopeInvocation) ?? job.archivedScope;
        const envelope = JSON.stringify({
          Z_state: { run_id: identity.runId, candidate_id: identity.candidateId, target_id: job.uid,
            pipeline_version: identity.pipelineVersion, current_stage: 'A_COMPLETE', status: 'complete:HANDOFF_TO_B' },
          A_scout: { target_entity: { company_name: job.employer, job_title: job.title }, source_url: job.sourceUrl,
            original_content_sha256: job.sourceHash,
            requirements_taxonomy: job.taxonomy ?? { hard_requirements: [], soft_requirements: [], keyword_lexicon: [] } },
          history: [{ stage: 'A4', operation: 'OPERATOR_PUSH_COMMIT', target_id: job.uid,
            batch_id: job.batchId, runner_id: job.runnerId, source_references: job.sourceRefs,
            search_scope: scope?.cartridge ?? null }],
          audit_trail: corpus.map(j => ({ target_id: j.uid, decision: j.decision, source_sha256: j.sourceHash })),
        }, null, 2);
        const sidecar = JSON.stringify({
          run_binding: { run_id: identity.runId, target_id: job.uid }, raw_posting: job.raw, original_content_sha256: job.sourceHash,
          A_staging: {
            // Provisional local extension: retain decisions/exclusions/source
            // rather than fabricate a permanent runner-side wire contract.
            records: corpus.map(j => ({ key: j.key, uid: j.uid, decision: j.decision,
              excluded: j.decision === 'REJECT', active: j.key === job.key,
              raw_posting: j.raw, source_sha256: j.sourceHash, source_references: j.sourceRefs,
              batch_id: j.batchId, runner_id: j.runnerId, search_scope_invocation: j.scopeInvocation ?? j.archivedScope?.invocation ?? null })),
            payloads: payloadEvidence,
          },
        }, null, 2);
        pairs.push({ uid: job.uid!, runId: identity.runId, envelope, sidecar, envelopeHash: await sha256(envelope), sidecarHash: await sha256(sidecar) });
      }
      const transaction: Committed = { id: `transaction-${this.#transactions.length + 1}`, pairs, corpus, payloads: structuredClone(this.#payloads), scopes: structuredClone(this.#scopes), state: 'COMMITTED', failures: [] };
      this.#transactions.push(transaction);
      this.#commitWarning = null;
      // No upstream clearing: receiving A4 owns its snapshot; A3 remains intact.
      return structuredClone(transaction);
    } catch (error) { return this.#fail('A4', 'commit', error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)); }
    finally { this.#busy = false; }
  }

  async certify(id: string): Promise<Committed> {
    const tx = this.#transactions.find(t => t.id === id); if (!tx) return this.#fail('A5', id, 'Unknown committed transaction.');
    if (tx.state === 'AWAITING_ACK') return this.#fail('A5', id, 'Certification cannot replace an in-flight acknowledgment state.');
    if (tx.state === 'ACCEPTED') return structuredClone(tx);
    tx.failures = await validateTransaction(tx);
    tx.state = tx.failures.length ? 'BLOCKED' : 'LOCALLY_VERIFIED';
    return structuredClone(tx);
  }

  async handoff(id: string, receiver?: Receiver): Promise<Committed> {
    const tx = this.#transactions.find(t => t.id === id); if (!tx) return this.#fail('A5', id, 'Unknown committed transaction.');
    if (tx.state === 'AWAITING_ACK') return this.#fail('A5', id, 'A handoff transaction is already awaiting acknowledgment.');
    if (tx.state === 'ACCEPTED') return structuredClone(tx);
    await this.certify(id);
    if (tx.state !== 'LOCALLY_VERIFIED') return structuredClone(tx);
    if (!receiver) { tx.state = 'BLOCKED'; tx.failures.push({ stage: 'A5', resource: 'B1 acknowledgment', reason: 'No verified B1 receipt channel exists. No artifacts were transmitted; zero accepted.' }); return structuredClone(tx); }
    const attempt = beginLocalAttempt(receiver);
    tx.state = 'AWAITING_ACK';
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    try {
      // No production receiver is supplied by the UI. This is a testable seam.
      const receipt = await Promise.race([
        receiver.receive(tx.id, structuredClone(tx.pairs)),
        new Promise<never>((_, reject) => { timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('B1 acknowledgment timed out.')), 1000); }),
      ]);
      verifyLocalEvidence(receiver, receipt, attempt, tx.id, tx.pairs);
      if (receipt.transactionId !== tx.id) throw new Error('Wrong-transaction acknowledgment; zero accepted.');
      if (!receipt.receiver || !Array.isArray(receipt.artifacts)) throw new Error('Connection/receiver information alone is not artifact acceptance; zero accepted.');
      if (receipt.artifacts.length !== tx.pairs.length) throw new Error('Incomplete artifact acknowledgment; zero accepted.');
      for (const pair of tx.pairs) {
        const matches = receipt.artifacts.filter(a => a.uid === pair.uid);
        if (matches.length !== 1) throw new Error(`Missing or duplicate acknowledgment for ${pair.uid}; zero accepted.`);
        if (matches[0].accepted !== true) throw new Error(`B1 rejected ${pair.uid}: ${matches[0].reason ?? 'receiver supplied no rejection reason'}; zero accepted.`);
        if (matches[0].envelopeHash !== pair.envelopeHash) throw new Error(`B1 envelope version mismatch for ${pair.uid}; zero accepted.`);
        if (matches[0].sidecarHash !== pair.sidecarHash) throw new Error(`B1 sidecar version mismatch for ${pair.uid}; zero accepted.`);
      }
      tx.state = 'ACCEPTED';
    } catch (error) { tx.state = 'BLOCKED'; tx.failures.push({ stage: 'A5', resource: 'B1 acknowledgment', reason: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) }); }
    finally { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer); }
    return structuredClone(tx);
  }
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/core/topology.ts
------
// Plan 02: classify endpoints first, allocate one shared counter per local
// component second, validate third. Addresses are A-side logical interfaces,
// not invented HTTP URLs, ownership assertions, or gate numbers for B/runners.
export type Component = 'A1' | 'A2' | 'A3' | 'A4' | 'A5';
export type Direction = 'ingress' | 'egress' | 'bidirectional';
export type Mode = 'LOCAL' | 'RESERVED_DISABLED' | 'TEST_ONLY';
export interface Endpoint {
  component: Component; endpoint: string; direction: Direction; mode: Mode; evidence: string;
}
export interface Gate extends Endpoint { number: number; address: string }
export interface GateFailure { rule: string; resource: string; reason: string }

// Confirmed local ordering. Shared functions may carry two logical fields;
// this inventory does not pretend they are separate deployed services.
export const ENDPOINTS: readonly Endpoint[] = Object.freeze([
  { component:'A1', endpoint:'configuration', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts SegmentA.compile(definition)' },
  { component:'A1', endpoint:'search-json', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts compile returns/stores certified ScopeRun.cartridge' },
  { component:'A2', endpoint:'search-json', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts dispatch resolves stored invocation' },
  { component:'A2', endpoint:'manual-display-copy', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'main.ts prepare-json/copy-json and outbound textarea' },
  { component:'A2', endpoint:'folder', direction:'egress', mode:'RESERVED_DISABLED', evidence:'main.ts disabled Folder destination control' },
  { component:'A2', endpoint:'automated', direction:'egress', mode:'RESERVED_DISABLED', evidence:'main.ts disabled Automated transport control' },
  { component:'A3', endpoint:'runner-result', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts importPayload; imports.ts delineate existing returns/text' },
  { component:'A3', endpoint:'pinned-reimport', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'imports.ts pinned-text prefix branch' },
  { component:'A3', endpoint:'committed-decisions', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts commit confirmed PUSH selection snapshot' },
  { component:'A3', endpoint:'source-evidence', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts commit corpus/payload snapshot, without clearing A3' },
  { component:'A3', endpoint:'pinned-export', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts exportPinned; main.ts plain-text download' },
  { component:'A4', endpoint:'committed-decisions', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts commit validates confirmed selection/identity' },
  { component:'A4', endpoint:'source-evidence', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts commit verifies raw-source hashes, keeps corpus/payloads' },
  { component:'A4', endpoint:'traveling-envelope', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts commit Pair.envelope and its byte hash' },
  { component:'A4', endpoint:'sidecar', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'pipeline.ts commit Pair.sidecar and its byte hash' },
  { component:'A5', endpoint:'traveling-envelope', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'certification.ts validatePair JSON envelope/hydrateEnvelope' },
  { component:'A5', endpoint:'sidecar', direction:'ingress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'certification.ts validatePair JSON sidecar/hydrateSidecar' },
  { component:'A5', endpoint:'certified-envelope', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'main.ts separate locally validated envelope download' },
  { component:'A5', endpoint:'certified-sidecar', direction:'egress', mode:'LOCAL', evidence:'main.ts separate locally validated sidecar download' },
  { component:'A5', endpoint:'handoff-control', direction:'bidirectional', mode:'TEST_ONLY', evidence:'pipeline.ts handoff calls Receiver.receive and verifies returned HandoffReceipt; no production receiver' },
].map(e => Object.freeze(e)) as Endpoint[]);

const directionMark = { ingress:'<', egress:'>', bidirectional:'_' } as const;
export function allocateGates(endpoints: readonly Endpoint[]): Gate[] {
  const counters: Partial<Record<Component,number>> = {};
  return endpoints.map(e => {
    if (!['A1','A2','A3','A4','A5'].includes(e.component) || !Object.hasOwn(directionMark,e.direction) || !e.endpoint || !e.evidence || !['LOCAL','RESERVED_DISABLED','TEST_ONLY'].includes(e.mode)) throw new Error('Classify the local component and direction before allocating a gate.');
    const number = counters[e.component] = (counters[e.component] ?? 0) + 1;
    return { ...e, number, address:`${e.component}${directionMark[e.direction]}G${number}:` };
  });
}
export const GATES: readonly Gate[] = Object.freeze(allocateGates(ENDPOINTS).map(g => Object.freeze(g)));

export function validateGates(gates: readonly Gate[] = GATES): GateFailure[] {
  const failures: GateFailure[] = [];
  const fail = (rule:string,resource:string,reason:string) => failures.push({rule,resource,reason});
  const addresses = new Set<string>();
  const endpoints = new Set<string>();
  for (const g of gates) {
    const key = `${g.component}:${g.endpoint}`;
    const expected = ENDPOINTS.find(e => e.component === g.component && e.endpoint === g.endpoint);
    if (!expected) { fail('GV-07',key,'Endpoint does not belong to the confirmed local component inventory.'); continue; }
    if (addresses.has(g.address) || gates.some(other => other !== g && other.component === g.component && other.number === g.number)) fail('GV-01',g.address,'Local gate number/address is reused.');
    addresses.add(g.address);
    if (endpoints.has(key)) fail('GV-04',key,'Endpoint is accounted for more than once.');
    endpoints.add(key);
    if (g.direction !== expected.direction || g.address !== `${g.component}${directionMark[expected.direction]}G${g.number}:`) fail('GV-02',key,'Direction/address disagrees with the evidenced endpoint.');
    if (!g.evidence || g.evidence !== expected.evidence) fail('GV-03',key,'Endpoint classification lacks the confirmed source evidence.');
    if (g.mode !== expected.mode) fail('GV-06',key,'Reserved/test-only branch semantics were changed.');
  }
  for (const component of ['A1','A2','A3','A4','A5'] as const) {
    const numbers = gates.filter(g => g.component === component).map(g => g.number).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    if (numbers.some((n,i) => n !== i+1)) fail('GV-01',component,'Ingress, egress and reserved endpoints must share one uninterrupted counter starting at G1.');
  }
  for (const e of ENDPOINTS) if (!endpoints.has(`${e.component}:${e.endpoint}`)) fail('GV-04',`${e.component}:${e.endpoint}`,'Confirmed endpoint is missing, including any reserved endpoint.');
  return failures;
}

export interface Junction {
  id:string; from:string; to:string; status:'LOCAL'|'RESERVED_DISABLED'|'EXTERNAL_UNVERIFIED'|'TEST_ONLY';
}
const gate = (component:Component,endpoint:string) => GATES.find(g => g.component===component && g.endpoint===endpoint)!.address;
export const JUNCTIONS: readonly Junction[] = Object.freeze([
  {id:'J01',from:'Operator search configuration',to:gate('A1','configuration'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J02',from:gate('A1','search-json'),to:gate('A2','search-json'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J03',from:gate('A2','manual-display-copy'),to:'Operator manual display/copy',status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J04',from:gate('A2','folder'),to:'Future configured folder',status:'RESERVED_DISABLED'},
  {id:'J05',from:gate('A2','automated'),to:'Future automated transport',status:'RESERVED_DISABLED'},
  {id:'J06',from:gate('A2','manual-display-copy'),to:'External runner ingress [address unknown]',status:'EXTERNAL_UNVERIFIED'},
  {id:'J07',from:'External runner results [address unknown]',to:gate('A3','runner-result'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J08',from:'Operator-saved PIN text',to:gate('A3','pinned-reimport'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J09',from:gate('A3','committed-decisions'),to:gate('A4','committed-decisions'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J10',from:gate('A3','source-evidence'),to:gate('A4','source-evidence'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J11',from:gate('A4','traveling-envelope'),to:gate('A5','traveling-envelope'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J12',from:gate('A4','sidecar'),to:gate('A5','sidecar'),status:'LOCAL'},
  {id:'J13',from:gate('A5','certified-envelope'),to:'Existing B INGRESS: SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json [gate number undeclared]',status:'TEST_ONLY'},
  {id:'J14',from:gate('A5','certified-sidecar'),to:'Existing B INGRESS: STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json [gate number undeclared]',status:'TEST_ONLY'},
  {id:'J15',from:'Operator-supplied SDNA (outside A)',to:'Existing B INGRESS: candidate.sdna.yaml [gate number undeclared]',status:'TEST_ONLY'},
  {id:'J16',from:'B acceptance receipt [production mechanism absent]',to:gate('A5','handoff-control'),status:'TEST_ONLY'},
  // Complete accounting includes the existing Plan 01 PIN egress, omitted from J01–J16.
  {id:'J17',from:gate('A3','pinned-export'),to:'Operator manual saving → J08',status:'LOCAL'},
].map(j => Object.freeze(j)) as Junction[]);

export function validateJunctions(junctions: readonly Junction[] = JUNCTIONS): GateFailure[] {
  const failures:GateFailure[]=[];
  const ids=new Set<string>();
  for(const j of junctions){
    const expected=JUNCTIONS.find(e=>e.id===j.id);
    if(ids.has(j.id))failures.push({rule:'GV-04',resource:j.id,reason:'Duplicate logical junction.'});ids.add(j.id);
    if(!expected || j.from!==expected.from || j.to!==expected.to)failures.push({rule:'GV-02',resource:j.id,reason:'Direction/component endpoints differ from the confirmed route.'});
    if(expected && j.status!==expected.status)failures.push({rule:'GV-06',resource:j.id,reason:'Unverified, reserved or test-only route was promoted without evidence.'});
  }
  for(const j of JUNCTIONS)if(!ids.has(j.id))failures.push({rule:'GV-04',resource:j.id,reason:'Logical junction is unaccounted for.'});
  return failures;
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/integration/local-receiver.ts
------
// Harness-only module: never imported by the browser app. B is loaded from Git,
// in memory, by the existing pinned Vitest loader; no copied validator files.
import { SegmentBSession } from 'frozen-b:src/runtime/session.ts';
import { identity } from 'frozen-b:identity';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Receiver, Pair, HandoffReceipt } from '../core/model';
import { currentLocalAttempt, issueLocalEvidence } from '../core/local-evidence';
const hash=(s:string)=>createHash('sha256').update(s,'utf8').digest('hex');
const PIN='d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a';
const approved=Object.freeze(identity());
export function createLocalReceiver(operatorSdna:string):Receiver {
  const receiver:Receiver={async receive(transactionId:string,pairs:readonly Pair[]):Promise<HandoffReceipt>{
    const attempt=currentLocalAttempt(receiver);
    const measured=identity(); // Throws if loaded module bytes or lockfile drift.
    if(measured.sourceHash!==approved.sourceHash || measured.dependencyHash!==approved.dependencyHash || measured.buildHash!==approved.buildHash)throw new Error('Wrong dependency/build/source execution identity.');
    if(measured.commit!==PIN)throw new Error('Wrong approved B validator version.');
    const results:any[]=[],artifacts:HandoffReceipt['artifacts']=[];
    for(const p of pairs){
      const session=new SegmentBSession();
      const view=await session.ingress([{name:'SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json',text:p.envelope},{name:'STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json',text:p.sidecar},{name:'candidate.sdna.yaml',text:operatorSdna}]);
      results.push(view);
      artifacts.push({uid:p.uid,envelopeHash:hash(p.envelope),sidecarHash:hash(p.sidecar),accepted:view.progression.INGRESS==='PASS' && view.ready==='B1' && !view.failed,reason:JSON.stringify(view.refusals)});
    }
    const receipt={transactionId,receiver:'CONTROLLED_LOCAL_B_SESSION',artifacts};
    issueLocalEvidence(receiver,receipt,{attempt,transactionId,environment:`local-node:${process.version}:pid:${process.pid}`,timestamp:new Date().toISOString(),validator:measured.commit,sourceHash:measured.sourceHash,dependencyHash:measured.dependencyHash,buildHash:measured.buildHash,sdnaHash:hash(operatorSdna),results});
    return receipt;
  }};
  return receiver;
}

function confirm(message: string): Promise<boolean> {
  const dialog = el<HTMLDialogElement>('confirmation'); el('warning').textContent = message;
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    const finish = (yes: boolean) => { dialog.close(); dialog.oncancel = null; resolve(yes); };
    el('cancel-confirm').onclick = ()=>finish(false); el('affirm-confirm').onclick = ()=>finish(true);
    dialog.oncancel = e=>{e.preventDefault();finish(false);}; dialog.showModal();
  });
}
async function action(fn: ()=>Promise<void> | void) {
  if (working) return;
  working = true;
  try { await fn(); el('message').textContent = ''; } catch (error) { el('message').textContent = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error); }
  finally { working = false; render(); }
}

// ============================================================
// Envelope Hydration — exact existing state, no reconstruction
// ============================================================

import type { SealedAEnvelope, BLayer, SealedBEnvelope, RequirementTaxonomy, RawRequirementsTaxonomy } from '../types';

/**
 * Normalize canonical A_scout.requirements_taxonomy into the
 * RequirementTaxonomy[] that B1/B2 consume.
 *
 * Canonical shape (from Segment A):
 *   { hard_requirements: string[], soft_requirements: string[], keyword_lexicon: string[] }
 *
 * Legacy/test shape (array of objects):
 *   [{ requirement_id, label, classification, wording }, ...]
 *
 * Both are accepted. The canonical shape is the production contract.
 */
function normalizeTaxonomy(raw: any): { normalized: RequirementTaxonomy[]; rawTaxonomy: RawRequirementsTaxonomy | null } {
  // Canonical object shape: { hard_requirements, soft_requirements, keyword_lexicon }
  if (raw && typeof raw === 'object' && !Array.isArray(raw)) {
    if (!Array.isArray(raw.hard_requirements)) {
      throw new Error('A_scout.requirements_taxonomy.hard_requirements must be an array');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(raw.soft_requirements)) {
      throw new Error('A_scout.requirements_taxonomy.soft_requirements must be an array');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(raw.keyword_lexicon)) {
      throw new Error('A_scout.requirements_taxonomy.keyword_lexicon must be an array');
    }
    if (raw.hard_requirements.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('A_scout.requirements_taxonomy.hard_requirements must contain at least one requirement');
    }

    const normalized: RequirementTaxonomy[] = [];
    let idx = 0;

    for (const req of raw.hard_requirements) {
      idx++;
      normalized.push({
        requirement_id: `req-hard-${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}`,
        label: String(req),
        classification: 'HARD_REQUIREMENT',
        wording: String(req),
      });
    }

    let softIdx = 0;
    for (const req of raw.soft_requirements) {
      softIdx++;
      normalized.push({
        requirement_id: `req-soft-${String(softIdx).padStart(3, '0')}`,
        label: String(req),
        classification: 'SOFT_REQUIREMENT',
        wording: String(req),
      });
    }

    return { normalized, rawTaxonomy: raw as RawRequirementsTaxonomy };
  }

  // Legacy array shape — pass through
  if (Array.isArray(raw) && raw.length > 0) {
    return { normalized: raw as RequirementTaxonomy[], rawTaxonomy: null };
  }

  throw new Error('Envelope A_scout missing requirements_taxonomy');
}

export function hydrateEnvelope(raw: any): SealedAEnvelope {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') throw new Error('Envelope must be an object');

  // Identity lives under Z_state
  if (!raw.Z_state || typeof raw.Z_state !== 'object') throw new Error('Envelope missing: Z_state');
  const zRequired = ['run_id', 'candidate_id', 'target_id', 'pipeline_version',
    'current_stage', 'status'] as const;
  for (const key of zRequired) {
    if (raw.Z_state[key] === undefined || raw.Z_state[key] === null) {
      throw new Error(`Envelope missing: Z_state.${key}`);
    }
  }

  // A_scout must exist
  if (!raw.A_scout || typeof raw.A_scout !== 'object') throw new Error('Envelope missing: A_scout');
  if (!raw.A_scout.requirements_taxonomy) {
    throw new Error('Envelope A_scout missing requirements_taxonomy');
  }
  if (!raw.A_scout.original_content_sha256) {
    throw new Error('Envelope A_scout missing original_content_sha256');
  }

  // Normalize taxonomy: canonical object → internal RequirementTaxonomy[]
  const { normalized, rawTaxonomy } = normalizeTaxonomy(raw.A_scout.requirements_taxonomy);

  // Build the hydrated envelope, preserving all original fields
  const envelope: SealedAEnvelope = {
    ...raw,
    A_scout: {
      ...raw.A_scout,
      requirements_taxonomy: normalized,
      _raw_taxonomy: rawTaxonomy,
    },
  };

  Object.freeze(envelope.Z_state);
  Object.freeze(envelope.A_scout);
  if (envelope.history) Object.freeze(envelope.history);
  if (envelope.audit_trail) Object.freeze(envelope.audit_trail);
  return envelope;
}

export function appendBLayer(envelope: SealedAEnvelope, bLayer: BLayer): SealedBEnvelope {
  return {
    ...envelope,
    Z_state: {
      ...envelope.Z_state,
      current_stage: 'B5_COMPLETE',
      status: 'complete:STOP_BEFORE_RESUME_FACTORY',
    },
    B_sdna: bLayer,
  };
}

// ============================================================
// Sidecar Binding & Termination
// ============================================================

import type { StageASidecar, SidecarAttestation } from '../types';

export function hydrateSidecar(raw: any): StageASidecar {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') throw new Error('Sidecar must be an object');
  if (!raw.run_binding) throw new Error('Sidecar missing run_binding');
  if (!raw.run_binding.run_id) throw new Error('Sidecar missing run_binding.run_id');
  if (!raw.run_binding.target_id) throw new Error('Sidecar missing run_binding.target_id');
  if (typeof raw.raw_posting !== 'string') throw new Error('Sidecar missing raw_posting');
  if (!raw.original_content_sha256) throw new Error('Sidecar missing original_content_sha256');
  return raw as StageASidecar;
}

export function createSidecarAttestation(): SidecarAttestation {
  return {
    status: 'TERMINATED_AND_VERIFIED',
    destruction_verification: {
      method: 'APPLICATION_REFERENCE_PURGE',
      owned_references_unreachable: true,
      physical_memory_zeroization_guaranteed: false,
      scope: 'application/runtime object graph only',
    },
  };
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/vendor/scout/batch-compiler.ts

import { compileDisk, validateCartridge } from './compiler';
import type { Cartridge, DiskDefinition, ValidationResult } from './types';
import type { ObservationArtifact } from './observation-artifacts';

export interface BatchDiskRequest {
  runner_id: string;
  definition: DiskDefinition;
}

export interface BatchDiskResult {
  runner_id: string;
  disk_id: string;
  compiled_cartridge: Cartridge;
  validation: ValidationResult;
}

export interface RunnerOutput {
  runner_id: string;
  observations: readonly ObservationArtifact[];
}

export interface BatchDiskRuntimeResult extends BatchDiskResult {
  outputs: ObservationArtifact[];
}

/**
 * Compile and validate each definition through the authoritative single-disk path.
 */
export async function compileDiskBatch(
  requests: readonly BatchDiskRequest[],
): Promise<BatchDiskResult[]> {
  const runnerIds = requests.map((request) => request.runner_id);
  if (new Set(runnerIds).size !== runnerIds.length) {
    throw new Error('Each batch member must target a unique runner_id.');
  }
  const diskIds = requests.map((request) => request.definition.id);
  if (new Set(diskIds).size !== diskIds.length) {
    throw new Error('Each batch member must contain a unique disk definition id.');
  }

  return Promise.all(requests.map(async ({ runner_id, definition }) => {
    const compiled_cartridge = compileDisk(definition);
    const validation = await validateCartridge(compiled_cartridge);

    return {
      runner_id,
      disk_id: definition.id,
      compiled_cartridge,
      validation,
    };
  }));
}

/**
 * Preserve the connection between compiled disks and the observation artifacts
 * returned by their runners. The compiler does not process the observations;
 * it only keeps each runner's existing single-run outputs independently
 * addressable alongside the disk that controls that runner.
 */
export function bindRunnerOutputs(
  compiled: readonly BatchDiskResult[],
  returned: readonly RunnerOutput[],
): BatchDiskRuntimeResult[] {
  const outputsByRunner = new Map<string, readonly ObservationArtifact[]>();
  for (const output of returned) {
    if (outputsByRunner.has(output.runner_id)) {
      throw new Error(`Duplicate runner output for ${output.runner_id}.`);
    }
    outputsByRunner.set(output.runner_id, output.observations);
  }

  const compiledRunnerIds = new Set(compiled.map((result) => result.runner_id));
  for (const runnerId of outputsByRunner.keys()) {
    if (!compiledRunnerIds.has(runnerId)) {
      throw new Error(`Runner output has no compiled disk: ${runnerId}.`);
    }
  }

  return compiled.map((result) => {
    const observations = outputsByRunner.get(result.runner_id);
    if (!observations) {
      throw new Error(`Missing runner output for ${result.runner_id}.`);
    }

    return {
      ...result,
      outputs: observations.map((observation) => structuredClone(observation)),
    };
  });
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Scout A2-A3 Batch Ingress Adapter
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

import type { BatchDiskResult } from './batch-compiler';

export const SCOUT_MAX_JOB_PROCESSING_LIMIT = 50;

export interface RunnerIngressPackage {
  runner_id: string;
  runner_protocol: string;
  status: 'READY' | 'FAILED_VALIDATION';
  cartridge_id: string;
  content_hash: string;
  compiled_cartridge: BatchDiskResult['compiled_cartridge'];
  ingress_constraints: {
    pipeline_target: 'A2~A3_SCOUT_COLLECTION_GATES';
    system_max_limit: number;
    runner_job_limit: number;
    pre_gate_enforcement: 'TRUNCATE_OR_REJECT_AT_50';
    output_contract_fields: string[];
    deduplication_mode: string;
    normalization_mode: string;
  };
  validation_summary: {
    passed: boolean;
    checks_passed: number;
    total_checks: number;
    errors?: string[];
  };
}

export interface ScoutA2A3BatchIngressEnvelope {
  $schema: string;
  batch_id: string;
  schema_version: '2.2.0';
  generated_at: string;
  system_limits: {
    scout_max_job_processing_limit: number;
    enforcement_rule: 'Candidate pool must be clamped to <= 50 before Envoy 1 Gate 1';
  };
  total_runners: number;
  certified_runners: number;
  failed_runners: number;
  runner_ingress_packages: RunnerIngressPackage[];
}

export function toScoutIngressEnvelope(
  batchId: string,
  results: readonly BatchDiskResult[]
): ScoutA2A3BatchIngressEnvelope {
  const packages: RunnerIngressPackage[] = results.map((result) => {
    const cart = result.compiled_cartridge;
    const isCertified = result.validation.passed === true;
    const failingChecks = result.validation.checks
      .filter((check) => !check.passed)
      .map((check) => `Check ${check.id} (${check.name}): ${check.detail}`);

    const requestedLimit = cart.machine_payload.exec.limit;
    const runnerJobLimit = Math.min(requestedLimit, SCOUT_MAX_JOB_PROCESSING_LIMIT);

    return {
      runner_id: result.runner_id,
      runner_protocol: `DIPLOMAT / SCOUT Protocol ${result.runner_id}`,
      status: isCertified ? 'READY' : 'FAILED_VALIDATION',
      cartridge_id: cart.metadata.cartridge_id,
      content_hash: cart.certification.content_hash,
      compiled_cartridge: cart,
      ingress_constraints: {
        pipeline_target: 'A2~A3_SCOUT_COLLECTION_GATES',
        system_max_limit: SCOUT_MAX_JOB_PROCESSING_LIMIT,
        runner_job_limit: runnerJobLimit,
        pre_gate_enforcement: 'TRUNCATE_OR_REJECT_AT_50',
        output_contract_fields: cart.machine_payload.contract.output_fields,
        deduplication_mode: cart.machine_payload.contract.deduplication,
        normalization_mode: cart.machine_payload.contract.normalization,
      },
      validation_summary: {
        passed: isCertified,
        checks_passed: result.validation.checks.filter((check) => check.passed).length,
        total_checks: result.validation.checks.length,
        ...(isCertified ? {} : { errors: failingChecks }),
      },
    };
  });

  return {
    $schema: 'https://digital-alchemy.net/schemas/scout/a2-a3-batch-ingress.schema.json',
    batch_id: batchId,
    schema_version: '2.2.0',
    generated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
    system_limits: {
      scout_max_job_processing_limit: SCOUT_MAX_JOB_PROCESSING_LIMIT,
      enforcement_rule: 'Candidate pool must be clamped to <= 50 before Envoy 1 Gate 1',
    },
    total_runners: packages.length,
    certified_runners: packages.filter((pkg) => pkg.status === 'READY').length,
    failed_runners: packages.filter((pkg) => pkg.status === 'FAILED_VALIDATION').length,
    runner_ingress_packages: packages,
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/vendor/scout/compiler.ts
------
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Scout Disk Compiler — Compilation & Validation Engine
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

import type {
  DiskDefinition, ExecutionProfile, ExecutionProfileParams,
  MachinePayload, Cartridge, ValidationCheck, ValidationResult,
  RingAssignment
} from './types';

// ── Execution Profile Registry ──
const PROFILE_REGISTRY: Record<ExecutionProfile, ExecutionProfileParams> = {
  standard:  { limit: 10,  deduplicate: true,  rank_results: false, strict_post_filter: true,  deep_scrape: 1, active_only: true },
  discovery: { limit: 50,  deduplicate: true,  rank_results: false, strict_post_filter: false, deep_scrape: 0, active_only: false },
  research:  { limit: 100, deduplicate: true,  rank_results: true,  strict_post_filter: true,  deep_scrape: 2, active_only: true },
  forensics: { limit: 250, deduplicate: false, rank_results: false, strict_post_filter: false, deep_scrape: 3, active_only: false },
  strict:    { limit: 25,  deduplicate: true,  rank_results: false, strict_post_filter: true,  deep_scrape: 2, active_only: true },
};

// ── SHA-256 Hashing (Web Crypto) ──
async function sha256(input: string): Promise<string> {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
  const data = encoder.encode(input);
  const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', data);
  const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
  return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

// ── Canonical JSON (sorted keys, no whitespace) ──
function canonicalStringify(obj: unknown): string {
  if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return 'null';
  if (typeof obj === 'string') return JSON.stringify(obj);
  if (typeof obj === 'number' || typeof obj === 'boolean') return String(obj);
  if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
    return '[' + obj.map(item => canonicalStringify(item)).join(',') + ']';
  }
  if (typeof obj === 'object') {
    const keys = Object.keys(obj as Record<string, unknown>).sort();
    const pairs = keys.map(k => {
      const val = (obj as Record<string, unknown>)[k];
      return JSON.stringify(k) + ':' + canonicalStringify(val);
    });
    return '{' + pairs.join(',') + '}';
  }
  return String(obj);
}

// ── Disk Compiler ──
export function compileDisk(definition: DiskDefinition): Cartridge {
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const diskNumeric = definition.id.replace(/^DSK-/, '');

  // 1. Identifier Derivation
  const cartridgeId = 'CRT-' + diskNumeric;
  const compilationId = 'CMP-' + diskNumeric;
  const payloadId = 'PAY-' + diskNumeric;

  // 2. Taxonomy Flattening
  const naicsCodes = definition.industry.map(i => i.code);
  const socCodes = definition.roles.map(r => r.code);

  // 3. Ring Provenance (Cartesian Product)
  const assignments: RingAssignment[] = [];
  for (const n of naicsCodes) {
    for (const s of socCodes) {
      assignments.push({ source: [n, s], ring: 'R4' });
    }
  }

  // 4. Total keywords count
  const totalKw = definition.keywords.scopes.reduce((acc, s) => acc + s.include.length, 0)
    + definition.keywords.global_exclude.length
    + definition.keywords.optional_specialty_flags.length;

  // 5. Machine Payload
  const machinePayload: MachinePayload = {
    id: payloadId,
    dsk: definition.id,
    v: definition.version,
    obs_type: definition.observation_type,
    contract: {
      output_fields: [
        'Observation_ID', 'Collection_Date', 'Vendor', 'Employer', 'Job_Title',
        'City', 'State', 'ZIP', 'Industry', 'Employment_Type', 'Compensation',
        'Source_URL', 'Duplicate_Count'
      ],
      deduplication: 'sha256(normalize(url) + U+001F + normalize(employer))',
      normalization: 'NFKC_lowercase_trim_collapse',
    },
    loc: {
      z: definition.territory.zipCode,
      r: definition.territory.radiusMiles,
      ...(definition.territory.coordinates ? { ll: [definition.territory.coordinates.lat, definition.territory.coordinates.lon] as [number, number] } : {}),
    },
    tax: { n: naicsCodes, s: socCodes },
    rng_provenance: {
      resolver_version: '2.2.0',
      resolution_basis: 'deterministic_naics_soc_cartesian',
      assignments,
    },
    kw_rules: {
      logic: definition.keywords.logic,
      role_scopes: definition.keywords.scopes,
      exclude: definition.keywords.global_exclude,
      optional_flags: definition.keywords.optional_specialty_flags,
      rule: 'candidate_must_match_at_least_one_phrase_in_the_role_scope_for_its_target_soc; global_exclusion_match_rejects_candidate; optional_flags_do_not_affect_acceptance',
    },
    exec: PROFILE_REGISTRY[definition.execution_profile],
  };

  // 6. Assemble Uncertified Cartridge
  const cartridge: Cartridge = {
    metadata: {
      cartridge_id: cartridgeId,
      disk_id: definition.id,
      compilation_id: compilationId,
      payload_id: payloadId,
      schema_version: '2.2.0',
      builder: 'Scout Disk Studio v1.0',
      compiler_version: '2.2.0',
      compiled_at: now,
    },
    certification: {
      status: 'UNCERTIFIED',
      decision: 'NOT_READY',
      content_hash: 'sha256:pending',
      hash_scope: ['metadata', 'report', 'definition', 'machine_payload'],
    },
    report: {
      status: 'SUCCESS',
      metrics: {
        naics_count: naicsCodes.length,
        soc_count: socCodes.length,
        ring_assignment_count: assignments.length,
        keywords_count: totalKw,
      },
    },
    definition,
    machine_payload: machinePayload,
  };

  return cartridge;
}

// ── 25-Point Validator ──
export async function validateCartridge(cartridge: Cartridge): Promise<ValidationResult> {
  const checks: ValidationCheck[] = [];
  const c = cartridge;

  // Integrity Checks (1-8)
  checks.push({
    id: 1, name: 'Top-Level Structure',
    passed: !!(c.metadata && c.certification && c.report && c.definition && c.machine_payload),
    detail: 'All 5 top-level blocks present',
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 2, name: 'Schema Version',
    passed: c.metadata.schema_version === '2.2.0',
    detail: `Schema version: ${c.metadata.schema_version}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 3, name: 'Disk ID Consistency',
    passed: c.metadata.disk_id === c.definition.id && c.definition.id === c.machine_payload.dsk,
    detail: `metadata=${c.metadata.disk_id}, def=${c.definition.id}, payload=${c.machine_payload.dsk}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 4, name: 'Payload ID Consistency',
    passed: c.metadata.payload_id === c.machine_payload.id,
    detail: `metadata=${c.metadata.payload_id}, payload=${c.machine_payload.id}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 5, name: 'Version Sync',
    passed: c.definition.version === c.machine_payload.v,
    detail: `def=${c.definition.version}, payload=${c.machine_payload.v}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 6, name: 'Observation Type Sync',
    passed: c.definition.observation_type === c.machine_payload.obs_type,
    detail: `def=${c.definition.observation_type}, payload=${c.machine_payload.obs_type}`,
  });
  const hasCoords = !!c.definition.territory.coordinates;
  const coordsMatch = hasCoords
    ? c.definition.territory.coordinates!.lat === c.machine_payload.loc.ll?.[0]
      && c.definition.territory.coordinates!.lon === c.machine_payload.loc.ll?.[1]
    : !c.machine_payload.loc.ll;
  checks.push({
    id: 7, name: 'Territory Sync',
    passed: c.definition.territory.zipCode === c.machine_payload.loc.z
      && c.definition.territory.radiusMiles === c.machine_payload.loc.r
      && coordsMatch,
    detail: 'Territory ↔ loc synchronization',
  });

  const defNaics = c.definition.industry.map(i => i.code).sort().join(',');
  const payNaics = [...c.machine_payload.tax.n].sort().join(',');
  const defSoc = c.definition.roles.map(r => r.code).sort().join(',');
  const paySoc = [...c.machine_payload.tax.s].sort().join(',');
  checks.push({
    id: 8, name: 'Taxonomy Code Sync',
    passed: defNaics === payNaics && defSoc === paySoc,
    detail: 'NAICS and SOC codes match between definition and payload',
  });

  // Structure Checks (9-16)
  checks.push({
    id: 9, name: 'Disk ID Format',
    passed: /^DSK-[0-9]{6}$/.test(c.definition.id),
    detail: `ID: ${c.definition.id}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 10, name: 'Cartridge ID Format',
    passed: /^CRT-[0-9]{6}$/.test(c.metadata.cartridge_id),
    detail: `Cartridge: ${c.metadata.cartridge_id}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 11, name: 'Compilation ID Format',
    passed: /^CMP-[0-9]{6}$/.test(c.metadata.compilation_id),
    detail: `Compilation: ${c.metadata.compilation_id}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 12, name: 'Payload ID Format',
    passed: /^PAY-[0-9]{6}$/.test(c.metadata.payload_id),
    detail: `Payload: ${c.metadata.payload_id}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 13, name: 'Version Semantic Format',
    passed: /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$/.test(c.definition.version),
    detail: `Version: ${c.definition.version}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 14, name: 'ZIP Code Format',
    passed: /^\d{5}$/.test(c.definition.territory.zipCode),
    detail: `ZIP: ${c.definition.territory.zipCode}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 15, name: 'Keyword Logic Valid',
    passed: c.definition.keywords.logic === 'match_any_role_scope_and_exclude_global',
    detail: `Logic: ${c.definition.keywords.logic}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 16, name: 'Execution Profile Valid',
    passed: ['standard', 'discovery', 'research', 'forensics', 'strict'].includes(c.definition.execution_profile),
    detail: `Profile: ${c.definition.execution_profile}`,
  });

  // Content Checks (17-21)
  checks.push({
    id: 17, name: 'NAICS Present',
    passed: c.machine_payload.tax.n.length > 0,
    detail: `Count: ${c.machine_payload.tax.n.length}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 18, name: 'SOC Present',
    passed: c.machine_payload.tax.s.length > 0,
    detail: `Count: ${c.machine_payload.tax.s.length}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 19, name: 'Ring Assignments',
    passed: c.machine_payload.rng_provenance.assignments.length === c.machine_payload.tax.n.length * c.machine_payload.tax.s.length,
    detail: `Expected ${c.machine_payload.tax.n.length * c.machine_payload.tax.s.length}, got ${c.machine_payload.rng_provenance.assignments.length}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 20, name: 'Contract Fields',
    passed: c.machine_payload.contract.output_fields.length === 13,
    detail: `Fields: ${c.machine_payload.contract.output_fields.length}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 21, name: 'Label Present',
    passed: c.definition.label.trim().length > 0,
    detail: 'Non-empty label',
  });

  // Report Checks (22-23)
  checks.push({
    id: 22, name: 'Report Status',
    passed: c.report.status === 'SUCCESS',
    detail: `Status: ${c.report.status}`,
  });
  checks.push({
    id: 23, name: 'Report Metrics Consistent',
    passed: c.report.metrics.naics_count === c.machine_payload.tax.n.length
      && c.report.metrics.soc_count === c.machine_payload.tax.s.length,
    detail: 'Report metrics match payload taxonomy',
  });

  // Hash Scope Check (24)
  checks.push({
    id: 24, name: 'Hash Scope Defined',
    passed: c.certification.hash_scope.length === 4
      && c.certification.hash_scope.includes('metadata')
      && c.certification.hash_scope.includes('report')
      && c.certification.hash_scope.includes('definition')
      && c.certification.hash_scope.includes('machine_payload'),
    detail: `Scope: ${c.certification.hash_scope.join(', ')}`,
  });

  // SHA-256 Certification (25)
  const allPassed = checks.every(ch => ch.passed);
  if (allPassed) {
    const hashInput = {
      metadata: c.metadata,
      report: c.report,
      definition: c.definition,
      machine_payload: c.machine_payload,
    };
    const canonical = canonicalStringify(hashInput);
    const digest = await sha256(canonical);

    checks.push({
      id: 25, name: 'SHA-256 Certification',
      passed: true,
      detail: `sha256:${digest}`,
    });

    const certified: Cartridge = {
      ...c,
      certification: {
        ...c.certification,
        status: 'CERTIFIED',
        decision: 'READY',
        content_hash: `sha256:${digest}`,
      },
    };

    return { passed: true, checks, certified_cartridge: certified };
  } else {
    checks.push({
      id: 25, name: 'SHA-256 Certification',
      passed: false,
      detail: 'Cannot certify — prior checks failed',
    });
    return { passed: false, checks };
  }
}

// ── Haversine Distance (miles) ──
export function haversineDistance(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number {
  const R = 3958.8; // Earth radius in miles
  const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;
  const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;
  const a = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2
    + Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;
  return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(a));
}

// ── Normalization Helpers ──
export function normalizeUrl(url: string): string {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url);
    u.search = '';
    u.hash = '';
    let path = u.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, '');
    if (!path) path = '';
    return (u.protocol + '//' + u.host.toLowerCase() + path).toLowerCase();
  } catch {
    return url.toLowerCase().trim();
  }
}

export function normalizeEmployer(name: string): string {
  return name.normalize('NFKC').trim().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').toLowerCase();
}

export async function computeDedupKey(url: string, employer: string): Promise<string> {
  const input = normalizeUrl(url) + '\u001F' + normalizeEmployer(employer);
  return sha256(input);
}

export { sha256, canonicalStringify };
