FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/vendor/scout/decomposition-engine.ts
------
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ENVOY 2 — Deterministic 12-Rule Decomposition Engine
// TypeScript port of the Python reference implementation v2.2.0
//
// COLD BASE: Every function is named, pure, and inspectable.
// No AI, no LLM, no randomness. Same input → same output.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

import type { SemanticCategories } from './types';

// ── Tagged fact with requirement/preference classification ──
export interface AtomicFact {
  text: string;
  category: keyof SemanticCategories;
  qualifier: 'requirement' | 'preference' | 'availability';
  rule_trace: string[];  // which rules fired, in order
}

export interface DecompResult {
  facts: AtomicFact[];
  categories: Partial<SemanticCategories>;
  total_facts: number;
  rule_log: string[];  // full trace of all rule applications
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RULE 1: Sentence Atomization
// "One fact per statement — compound sentences must be split
//  into atomic assertions."
// Splits on [.;!?], then splits on conjunctions (and/or)
// when both sides are substantial clauses.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export function rule1_atomize(text: string): string[] {
  // Phase 1: Split on terminal punctuation
  const raw = text.split(/(?<=[.;!?])\s+/);
  const atoms: string[] = [];

  for (const segment of raw) {
    const trimmed = segment.replace(/[.;!?]+$/, '').trim();
    if (trimmed.length < 5) continue;

    // Phase 2: Split compound clauses on ", and " / ", or " / "; and "
    // Only split if both halves are ≥ 20 chars (substantial clauses)
    const conjSplit = trimmed.split(/,\s+(?:and|or)\s+|;\s+(?:and|or)\s+/i);
    if (conjSplit.length > 1 && conjSplit.every(p => p.trim().length >= 20)) {
      for (const part of conjSplit) {
        const p = part.trim();
        if (p.length >= 5) atoms.push(p);
      }
    } else {
      atoms.push(trimmed);
    }
  }
  return atoms;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RULE 2: Pronoun Stripping
// "Strip unreferenced personal pronouns."
// Removes leading "We/You/They/He/She" + verb patterns that
// add no factual content.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export function rule2_strip_pronouns(text: string): string {
  // Strip leading pronoun + auxiliary patterns
  let result = text.replace(
    /^(we|you|they|he|she|it|our team|the team|the company)\s+(are|is|will be|will|would|should|must be|need)\s+(looking for|seeking|hiring|searching for|in need of)\s*/i,
    ''
  );
  // Strip simpler leading pronoun + verb
  result = result.replace(
    /^(we|you|they)\s+(need|want|require|offer|provide|expect|have)\s+/i,
    ''
  );
  // Capitalize first char if we stripped something
  if (result !== text && result.length > 0) {
    result = result.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + result.slice(1);
  }
  return result;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RULE 5: Marketing Fluff Removal
// "Strip all recruiting fluff."
// Returns null if the entire sentence is fluff.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const FLUFF_PATTERNS = [
  /\bjoin our\b/i, /\bfast[- ]paced\b/i, /\bdynamic (?:culture|environment|team)\b/i,
  /\bexciting opportunity\b/i, /\bamazing team\b/i, /\bworld[- ]class\b/i,
  /\bgame[- ]chang/i, /\brockstar\b/i, /\bninja\b/i, /\bguru\b/i,
  /\bpassionate about\b/i, /\bthrive in\b/i, /\bmake (?:a |an )?(?:impact|difference)\b/i,
  /\bcutting[- ]edge\b/i, /\binnovative(?:\s+and\s+collaborative)?\s+(?:environment|culture|team)\b/i,
  /\bwork hard[,]?\s*play hard\b/i, /\bfamily[- ](?:like|oriented)\s+(?:environment|culture)\b/i,
  /\bwe['']re (?:looking for|seeking) (?:a |an )?(?:passionate|motivated|driven)\b/i,
];

export function rule5_remove_fluff(text: string): string | null {
  for (const pattern of FLUFF_PATTERNS) {
    if (pattern.test(text)) {
      // Check if the ENTIRE sentence is fluff (< 30 chars of non-fluff content)
      const stripped = text.replace(pattern, '').trim();
      if (stripped.length < 30) return null;  // whole sentence is fluff
      // Otherwise, remove the fluff phrase and keep the rest
      return stripped;
    }
  }
  return text;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RULE 10: Preference vs. Requirement Separation
// "Distinguish mandatory requirements from desirable quals."
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const PREFERENCE_SIGNALS = [
  /\bpreferred\b/i, /\bprefer(?:ably|ence)?\b/i, /\ba plus\b/i,
  /\bnice to have\b/i, /\bdesirable\b/i, /\bideally\b/i,
  /\bbonus\b/i, /\bnot required\b/i, /\boptional\b/i,
  /\bhelpful\b/i, /\badvantageous\b/i,
];

const REQUIREMENT_SIGNALS = [
  /\bmust\b/i, /\brequired\b/i, /\bminimum\b/i, /\bmandatory\b/i,
  /\bessential\b/i, /\bnecessary\b/i, /\bshall\b/i,
];

export function rule10_classify_qualifier(text: string): 'requirement' | 'preference' {
  for (const pat of PREFERENCE_SIGNALS) {
    if (pat.test(text)) return 'preference';
  }
  for (const pat of REQUIREMENT_SIGNALS) {
    if (pat.test(text)) return 'requirement';
  }
  return 'requirement';  // default: treat as requirement
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RULE 11: Availability vs. Requirement Separation
// "Distinguish shift/schedule availability from qualifications."
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const AVAILABILITY_PATTERNS = [
  /\b(?:available|availability)\s+(?:to|for)\s+(?:work|travel|start)/i,
  /\b(?:monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sunday)\s+(?:through|to|[-–])\s+/i,
  /\b(?:first|second|third|day|night|swing|graveyard|evening|morning)\s+shift\b/i,
  /\b(?:on[- ]call|overtime|weekends?|holidays?)\s+(?:required|expected|as needed)/i,
  /\b(?:flexible|rotating)\s+(?:schedule|hours|shifts)\b/i,
  /\b(?:\d{1,2}:\d{2}\s*(?:am|pm|AM|PM))\b/,
];

export function rule11_is_availability(text: string): boolean {
  return AVAILABILITY_PATTERNS.some(pat => pat.test(text));
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CATEGORY CLASSIFIER
// Ordered pattern bank mapping sentences → 14 categories.
// Each entry: [category, patterns[]]. First match wins.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
type CatKey = keyof SemanticCategories;

const CATEGORY_RULES: Array<[CatKey, RegExp[]]> = [
  ['Schedule', [
    /\b(?:monday|tuesday|wednesday|thursday|friday|saturday|sunday)\b/i,

  ]],
  ['Employment Type', [

    /\bpto\b/i, /\bpaid\s+(?:time\s+off|leave|vacation|holiday|sick)\b/i,
    /\bdental\b/i, /\bvision\b/i, /\blife\s+insurance\b/i,
    /\bdisability\s+insurance\b/i, /\btuition\s+(?:reimbursement|assistance)\b/i,
    /\bretirement\b/i, /\bwellness\b/i, /\bparental\s+leave\b/i,

  ['Experience', [

    /\bproven\s+(?:track\s+record|experience|ability)\b/i,
    /\bbackground\s+in\b/i, /\bexposure\s+to\b/i,
    /\bhands[- ]on\s+experience\b/i,
  ]],
  ['Education', [
    /\b(?:bachelor|master|doctoral|associate)['']?s?\s+degree\b/i,
    /\b(?:BS|BA|MS|MA|MBA|PhD|MD|JD)\b/, /\bdegree\s+(?:in|from)\b/i,
    /\beducation\b/i, /\bgraduate\b/i, /\buniversity\b/i,
    /\bhigh\s+school\s+diploma\b/i, /\bGED\b/i,
  ]],
  ['Certifications', [
    /\bcertif(?:ied|ication|icate)\b/i, /\blicens(?:ed|ure|e)\b/i,
    /\bclearance\b/i, /\b(?:AWS|Azure|GCP|PMP|CPA|CISSP|CCNA)\b/,
    /\bboard[- ]certified\b/i, /\baccredit/i,
  ]],
  ['Physical Requirements', [
    /\b(?:lift|carry|push|pull)\s+(?:up\s+to\s+)?\d+\s*(?:lbs?|pounds?|kg)\b/i,
    /\b(?:stand|sit|walk|bend|kneel|crouch|crawl|climb)\s+for\b/i,
    /\bphysical(?:ly)?\s+(?:demanding|fit|able|capable)\b/i,
    /\bmanual\s+(?:dexterity|labor)\b/i,
    /\brepetitive\s+(?:motion|movement|task)/i,
  ]],
  ['Working Conditions', [
    /\bremote\b/i, /\bhybrid\b/i, /\bon[- ]site\b/i, /\bin[- ]office\b/i,
    /\bwork\s+from\s+home\b/i, /\btravel\s+(?:up to|required|\d+%)/i,
    /\boutdoor/i, /\bwarehouse\b/i, /\bclean\s*room\b/i,
    /\bhazardous\b/i, /\bPPE\b/i, /\bnoise\s+(?:level|exposure)\b/i,

  ['Leadership', [

    /\b(?:docker|kubernetes|terraform|ansible|jenkins|git|ci\/cd)\b/i,
    /\b(?:aws|azure|gcp|cloud)\b/i,
    /\b(?:sql|nosql|postgres|mysql|mongodb|redis|elasticsearch|dynamodb)\b/i,
    /\b(?:machine\s+learning|deep\s+learning|NLP|computer\s+vision|data\s+science)\b/i,
    /\b(?:agile|scrum|kanban|devops|microservices|rest(?:ful)?|graphql|api)\b/i,
    /\b(?:tableau|power\s*bi|excel|jira|confluence|figma|sketch)\b/i,
    /\b(?:communication|problem[- ]solving|analytical|critical\s+thinking)\b/i,
    /\bproficien(?:t|cy)\b/i, /\bskill(?:s|ed)\b/i, /\bknowledge\s+of\b/i,
    /\bfamiliar(?:ity)?\s+with\b/i, /\bexpertise\s+in\b/i,
  ]],
  ['Responsibilities', [
    /\b(?:responsible|responsibility)\b/i, /\brequir(?:es?|ed|ing|ement)\b/i,
    /\bmust\s+(?:have|be|possess)\b/i, /\bability\s+to\b/i,
    /\bduties?\s+include\b/i, /\baccountable\b/i,
    /\byou\s+will\b/i, /\bthe\s+role\s+(?:involves?|includes?|requires?)\b/i,
    /\bdesign(?:ing)?\s+and\s+(?:develop|implement|build)/i,
    /\bcollaborat(?:e|ing)\s+with\b/i, /\bensure\b/i, /\bmaintain\b/i,
  ]],
];

export function classify_category(text: string): CatKey {
  for (const [category, patterns] of CATEGORY_RULES) {
    for (const pat of patterns) {
      if (pat.test(text)) return category;
    }
  }
  return 'Other';
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// RULE 9: Duplicate Consolidation
// "Consolidate repetitive statements into a single entry."
// Compares by lowercase-trimmed text identity.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export function rule9_deduplicate(facts: AtomicFact[]): AtomicFact[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const result: AtomicFact[] = [];
  for (const f of facts) {
    const key = f.text.toLowerCase().trim();
    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
    seen.add(key);
    result.push(f);
  }
  return result;
}

// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MAIN PIPELINE: decomposeDescription
// Executes rules 1→2→5→classify→10→11→9 in fixed order.
// Returns full trace for inspection.
// ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
export function decomposeDescription(text: string): DecompResult {
  const rule_log: string[] = [];

  // Rule 1: Atomize
  const atoms = rule1_atomize(text);
  rule_log.push(`Rule 1 (Atomize): ${atoms.length} atoms from input`);

  const facts: AtomicFact[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < atoms.length; i++) {
    let sentence = atoms[i];
    const trace: string[] = [];

    // Rule 2: Strip pronouns
    const beforePronoun = sentence;
    sentence = rule2_strip_pronouns(sentence);
    if (sentence !== beforePronoun) {
      trace.push('Rule 2: Pronoun stripped');
      rule_log.push(`  [${i}] Rule 2: "${beforePronoun.substring(0, 40)}..." → "${sentence.substring(0, 40)}..."`);
    }

    // Rule 5: Marketing fluff removal
    const afterFluff = rule5_remove_fluff(sentence);
    if (afterFluff === null) {
      trace.push('Rule 5: Entire sentence is fluff — DROPPED');
      rule_log.push(`  [${i}] Rule 5: DROPPED "${sentence.substring(0, 50)}..."`);
      continue;
    }
    if (afterFluff !== sentence) {
      trace.push('Rule 5: Fluff phrase removed');
      rule_log.push(`  [${i}] Rule 5: Trimmed fluff from "${sentence.substring(0, 40)}..."`);
      sentence = afterFluff;
    }

    // Rules 3,4,7,8,12: Guaranteed by deterministic regex — no interpretation,
    // no inference, no unsupported normalization, verbatim specificity and
    // proper nouns preserved. (Regex cannot alter matched text.)

    // Category classification
    const category = classify_category(sentence);
    trace.push(`Classify: → ${category}`);

    // Rule 10: Preference vs. requirement
    const qualifier = rule10_classify_qualifier(sentence);
    trace.push(`Rule 10: ${qualifier}`);

    // Rule 11: Availability override
    const isAvail = rule11_is_availability(sentence);
    const finalQualifier = isAvail ? 'availability' as const : qualifier;
    if (isAvail) {
      trace.push('Rule 11: Reclassified as availability');
    }

    facts.push({ text: sentence, category, qualifier: finalQualifier, rule_trace: trace });
  }

  // Rule 9: Deduplicate
  const before = facts.length;
  const deduped = rule9_deduplicate(facts);
  rule_log.push(`Rule 9 (Dedup): ${before} → ${deduped.length} facts (${before - deduped.length} duplicates removed)`);

  // Build category map
  const categories: Partial<SemanticCategories> = {};
  for (const f of deduped) {
    if (!categories[f.category]) categories[f.category] = [];
    categories[f.category]!.push(f.text);
  }

  return {
    facts: deduped,
    categories,
    total_facts: deduped.length,
    rule_log,
  };
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/src/vendor/scout/observation-artifacts.ts
------
import { decomposeDescription, type DecompResult } from './decomposition-engine';
import { sha256 } from './compiler';
import type {
  CandidateJob,
  CanonicalRow,
  Cartridge,
  EnvelopeFact,
  TravelingEnvelope,
} from './types';

export interface ObservationArtifact {
  canonical: CanonicalRow;
  envelope: TravelingEnvelope;
  decomp: DecompResult;
}

/**
 * Construct the authoritative single-run output artifacts for one accepted
 * observation. This is the existing PipelineSimulator behavior extracted into
 * a reusable function; it does not change collection or decomposition semantics.
 */
export async function buildObservationArtifact(
  cartridge: Cartridge,
  candidate: CandidateJob,
  dedupKey: string,
  duplicateCount: number,
): Promise<ObservationArtifact> {
  const observationId = `OBS-${dedupKey.substring(0, 12).toUpperCase()}`;
  const naicsEntry = cartridge.definition.industry
    .find((industry) => industry.code === candidate.naics_code);
  const canonical: CanonicalRow = {
    Observation_ID: observationId,
    Collection_Date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
    Vendor: candidate.vendor,
    Employer: candidate.employer,
    Job_Title: candidate.title,
    City: candidate.city,
    State: candidate.state,
    ZIP: candidate.zip,
    Industry: naicsEntry
      ? `${naicsEntry.code} — ${naicsEntry.title}`
      : candidate.naics_code,
    Employment_Type: candidate.employment_type,
    Compensation: candidate.compensation,
    Source_URL: candidate.source_url,
    Duplicate_Count: duplicateCount,
  };

  const decomp = decomposeDescription(candidate.description);
  const sourceHash = await sha256(candidate.description);
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const factTable: EnvelopeFact[] = decomp.facts.map((fact) => ({
    text: fact.text,
    category: fact.category,
    qualifier: fact.qualifier,
    trace: fact.rule_trace,
  }));

  const envelope: TravelingEnvelope = {
    $schema: 'https://json-schema.spatial-dna.org/v0.2/envelope.json',
    envelope_id: `ENV-${observationId}`,
    created_at: now,
    target_identity: {
      observation_id: observationId,
      employer: candidate.employer,
      job_title: candidate.title,
      deduplication_key: dedupKey,
    },
    stage_state: {
      current_stage: 'b',
      completed_stages: ['scout'],
      status: 'ready',
    },
    append_history: [{
      stage: 'scout',
      operation: 'initialize_and_append',
      timestamp: now,
    }],
    payload: {
      scout: {
        path_a_structured: canonical,
        path_b_semantic: {
          categories: decomp.categories,
          fact_table: factTable,
          rule_log: decomp.rule_log,
          total_facts: decomp.total_facts,
        },
        section_8_source: {
          source_text: candidate.description,
          source_hash: `sha256:${sourceHash}`,
          capture_timestamp: now,
          is_preserved_source: true,
        },
      },
      b: null,
      b1: null,
      b2: null,
      b3: null,
      b4: null,
      b5: null,
    },
  };

  return { canonical, envelope, decomp };
}

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/tests/execution-attestation.ts
------
// Independent harness inspection of pinned source closure and installed tooling.
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
const pin='d1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a';
const hash=(s:string|Uint8Array)=>createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
const read=(p:string)=>readFileSync(p,'utf8');
const paths=execFileSync('git',['ls-tree','-r','--name-only','--full-tree',pin],{encoding:'utf8'}).trim().split('\n').filter(p=>p.startsWith('src/'));
const source=paths.map(p=>[p,execFileSync('git',['show',`${pin}:${p}`],{encoding:'utf8'})]);
const frozenLock=execFileSync('git',['show',`${pin}:package-lock.json`],{encoding:'utf8'});
const localLock=read('package-lock.json');
function tree(p:string):string {return readdirSync(p,{withFileTypes:true}).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name)).map(e=>e.isDirectory()?tree(join(p,e.name)):e.name+hash(readFileSync(join(p,e.name)))).join('\n');}
const yaml=tree('node_modules/yaml');
const loader=read('vitest.config.ts');
const harnessPaths=['tests/execution-attestation.ts','src/integration/local-receiver.ts','src/core/local-evidence.ts'];
const harness=harnessPaths.map(p=>[p,read(p)]);
const tooling=['vite','vitest','tsx','typescript','esbuild'].map(p=>[p,tree(`node_modules/${p}`)]);
const toolingHash=hash(JSON.stringify(tooling));
const loaded=new Map<string,string>();
export function recordLoaded(path:string,sourceHash:string,transformedHash:string){
 const expected=source.find(([p])=>p===path);if(!expected || hash(expected[1])!==sourceHash)throw new Error('Executed B source drift.');
 loaded.set(path,transformedHash);
}
export function attest(){
 if(!loaded.has('src/runtime/session.ts') || !loaded.has('src/runtime/ingress.ts'))throw new Error('B session/validator not executed by pinned loader.');
 if(read('package-lock.json')!==localLock || read('vitest.config.ts')!==loader || tree('node_modules/yaml')!==yaml)throw new Error('Dependency/build identity drift.');
 if(harness.some(([p,s])=>read(p)!==s) || hash(JSON.stringify(tooling.map(([p])=>[p,tree(`node_modules/${p}`)])))!==toolingHash)throw new Error('Harness/toolchain execution identity drift.');
 const expected=JSON.parse(frozenLock).packages['node_modules/yaml'].version;
 if(JSON.parse(read('node_modules/yaml/package.json')).version!==expected)throw new Error('Wrong B yaml dependency version.');
 return {commit:pin,sourceHash:hash(JSON.stringify(source)),dependencyHash:hash(frozenLock+localLock+yaml),buildHash:hash(JSON.stringify([...loaded].sort())+loader+process.version+localLock+yaml+JSON.stringify(harness)+toolingHash)};
}

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { SegmentA } from '../src/core/pipeline';
import { validatePair } from '../src/core/certification';
import { compileDisk, validateCartridge, sha256 } from '../src/vendor/scout/compiler';
import { definition, returned } from './fixtures';
import type { Receiver } from '../src/core/model';
import { buildObservationArtifact } from '../src/vendor/scout/observation-artifacts';
import { acceptThreeFiles } from 'frozen-b:src/runtime/ingress.ts';
import { executeIngress } from 'frozen-b:src/b/engine.ts';

const identity = { runId: 'operator-run', candidateId: 'candidate-test', pipelineVersion: '1.0.0' };
async function committed(p = new SegmentA(), count = 1) {
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned(`OBS-${i}`, `Python systems experience is required ${i}.`)),`return-${i}.txt`);
  p.view().jobs.forEach(j => p.decide(j.key,'PUSH'));
  const warning = p.prepareCommit();
  const tx = await p.commit(warning.token,true,identity);
  return { p, tx };
}
afterEach(()=>vi.useRealTimers());

describe('A1 / A2 reuse and independent dispatch',()=>{
  it('uses unchanged compileDisk/validateCartridge and creates independent outputs',async()=>{
    vi.useFakeTimers();vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2026-10-12T12:00:00Z'));
    const p=new SegmentA();const d=definition();const direct=await validateCartridge(compileDisk(structuredClone(d)));
    const a=await p.compile(d);expect(a.cartridge).toEqual(direct.certified_cartridge);expect(a.validation.checks).toHaveLength(25);
    const b=await p.compile(definition('DSK-000002'));expect(b.invocation).not.toBe(a.invocation);
    b.cartridge.definition.label='changed';expect(p.view().scopes[0].cartridge.definition.label).toBe('Systems scope');
    d.label='mutated input';expect(JSON.parse(p.dispatch(a.invocation)).definition.label).toBe('Systems scope');
  });
  it('preserves batch identity and return instructions only through the existing dispatch adapter',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();const run=await p.compile(definition());
    const first=JSON.parse(p.dispatch(run.invocation,{batchId:'batch-a',runnerId:'runner-a'}));
    const next=JSON.parse(p.dispatch(run.invocation,{batchId:'batch-b',runnerId:'runner-b'}));
    expect(first.batch_id).toBe('batch-a');expect(next.runner_ingress_packages[0].runner_id).toBe('runner-b');
    expect(first.runner_ingress_packages[0].ingress_constraints.output_contract_fields).toHaveLength(13);
    expect(p.dispatch(run.invocation)).not.toContain('runner-a');
  });
  it('refuses invalid compiler definitions without creating an output',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();await expect(p.compile({...definition(),id:'bad'})).rejects.toThrow('Disk ID');expect(p.view().scopes).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('A3 evidence, decisions and pinned text',()=>{
  it('imports multiple variable-length jobs preserving exact CRLF and original payloads',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();const raw='UID: short\r\nDescription:\r\na\r\nUID: long\r\nDescription:\r\n'+('long source '.repeat(5000));
    await p.importPayload(raw,'runner.md');await p.importPayload('Unstructured source','another.txt');
    const v=p.view();expect(v.jobs).toHaveLength(3);expect(v.jobs[0].raw).toBe('a\r\n');expect(v.jobs[1].raw.length).toBeGreaterThan(50000);expect(v.jobs[2].uid).toBeNull();expect(v.payloads[0].raw).toBe(raw);
    expect(v.jobs[0].sourceHash).toBe(await sha256('a\r\n'));expect(v.jobs[0].display).toBe('a\n');
  });
  it('never invents a UID and preserves PUSH, PIN, PENDING states',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();await p.importPayload('Original evidence','paste');const j=p.view().jobs[0];
    expect(j.uid).toBeNull();p.assignUid(j.key,'operator-id');p.decide(j.key,'PUSH');expect(p.view().jobs[0].decision).toBe('PUSH');p.decide(j.key,'PIN');p.decide(j.key,'PENDING');expect(p.view().jobs[0].decision).toBe('PENDING');
  });
  it('detects duplicates without hiding or silently changing rows',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();const raw=JSON.stringify(await returned());await p.importPayload(raw,'one');await p.importPayload(raw,'two');expect(p.view().jobs).toHaveLength(2);expect(p.view().jobs[1].duplicateOf).toBe('job-1');expect(p.view().jobs.every(j=>j.decision==='PENDING')).toBe(true);
  });
  it('requires rejection confirmation and blocks restoration and reimport resurrection',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();const raw=JSON.stringify(await returned());await p.importPayload(raw,'one');const before=p.view().jobs;
    expect(()=>p.decide('job-1','REJECT')).toThrow('confirmation');expect(p.view().jobs).toEqual(before);
    p.decide('job-1','REJECT',true);expect(()=>p.decide('job-1','PUSH')).toThrow('irreversible');await p.importPayload(raw,'again');expect(p.view().jobs.every(j=>j.decision==='REJECT')).toBe(true);
  });
  it('round-trips PIN exports through A3 with UID, exact content and context',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned()),'original');p.decide('job-1','PIN');const exported=p.exportPinned();const q=new SegmentA();await q.importPayload(exported,'saved.txt');
    const a=p.view().jobs[0],b=q.view().jobs[0];expect(b.uid).toBe(a.uid);expect(b.raw).toBe(a.raw);expect(b.batchId).toBe(a.batchId);expect(b.runnerId).toBe(a.runnerId);expect(b.taxonomy).toEqual(a.taxonomy);expect(b.decision).toBe('PIN');
  });
  it('rejects a corrupt saved source without replacing existing staging',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned()),'original');p.decide('job-1','PIN');const exported=p.exportPinned().replace('Python systems experience is required.','Tampered evidence.');const before=p.view().jobs;
    await expect(p.importPayload(exported,'corrupt')).rejects.toThrow('integrity');expect(p.view().jobs).toEqual(before);
  });
});

describe('A3→A4 atomic commitment',()=>{
  it('requires a current warning and affirmative confirmation; cancellation leaves staging unchanged',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned()),'one');p.decide('job-1','PUSH');const before=p.view().jobs;const w=p.prepareCommit();expect(w.warning).toContain('Export PIN');p.cancelCommit();await expect(p.commit(w.token,true,identity)).rejects.toThrow('warning');expect(p.view().jobs).toEqual(before);expect(p.view().transactions).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('rejects stale confirmation and duplicate selected UIDs without clearing evidence',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();const raw=JSON.stringify(await returned());await p.importPayload(raw,'one');p.decide('job-1','PUSH');const w=p.prepareCommit();await p.importPayload(raw,'two');await expect(p.commit(w.token,true,identity)).rejects.toThrow('current warning');p.decide('job-2','PUSH');const w2=p.prepareCommit();await expect(p.commit(w2.token,true,identity)).rejects.toThrow('Duplicate selected');expect(p.view().jobs).toHaveLength(2);expect(p.view().transactions).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('mints one pair per UID with correct source references and preserves excluded evidence',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned('REJECTED','Rejected raw')),'excluded');p.decide('job-1','REJECT',true);
    await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned('PUSHED','Preserved raw')),'selected');p.decide('job-2','PUSH');const tx=await p.commit(p.prepareCommit().token,true,identity);
    expect(tx.pairs).toHaveLength(1);expect(tx.corpus).toHaveLength(2);expect(tx.payloads).toHaveLength(2);expect(p.view().jobs).toHaveLength(2);
    const pair=tx.pairs[0],e=JSON.parse(pair.envelope),s=JSON.parse(pair.sidecar);expect(s.raw_posting).toBe('Preserved raw');expect(e.Z_state.target_id).toBe('PUSHED');expect(e.audit_trail.find((r:any)=>r.target_id==='REJECTED').decision).toBe('REJECT');expect(e.history[0].source_references).toEqual(tx.corpus[1].sourceRefs);expect(await validatePair(pair)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('does not invent taxonomy for plain text and blocks A5 rather than tuning B',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();await p.importPayload('UID: plain\nDescription:\nOriginal source','plain');p.decide('job-1','PUSH');const tx=await p.commit(p.prepareCommit().token,true,identity);const checked=await p.certify(tx.id);expect(checked.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(checked.failures.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);expect(p.view().jobs).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('A5 exact-version acknowledgment and unchanged B compatibility',()=>{
  it('blocks an actual frozen B candidate-identity refusal without moving SDNA into A',async()=>{
    const {p,tx}=await committed();const receiver:Receiver={receive:async(id,pairs)=>{
      const pair=pairs[0];const ctx=await acceptThreeFiles([{name:'SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json',text:pair.envelope},{name:'STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json',text:pair.sidecar},{name:'candidate.sdna.yaml',text:'candidate_id: wrong-independent-candidate\natoms: []\n'}]);await executeIngress(ctx);
      return {transactionId:id,receiver:'frozen-B-test-only',artifacts:[{uid:pair.uid,envelopeHash:await sha256(pair.envelope),sidecarHash:await sha256(pair.sidecar),accepted:ctx.stageProgression.INGRESS==='PASS',reason:ctx.errors.join('; ')}]};
    }};
    const blocked=await p.handoff(tx.id,receiver);expect(blocked.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(blocked.failures[0].reason).toContain('Unverified execution origin');expect(blocked.pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);
  });
  it('does not treat connection acknowledgment as artifact acceptance',async()=>{
    const {p,tx}=await committed();const result=await p.handoff(tx.id,{receive:async(id)=>({transactionId:id,receiver:'connected-only'} as any)});
    expect(result.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(result.failures[0].reason).toContain('Unverified execution origin');expect(result.pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);
  });
  it('routes an actual existing runner artifact builder output into unchanged B intake',async()=>{
    const cart=(await validateCartridge(compileDisk(definition()))).certified_cartridge!;
    const artifact=await buildObservationArtifact(cart,{title:'Engineer',employer:'Example',city:'New York',state:'NY',zip:'10001',lat:40.7,lon:-74,naics_code:'541511',soc_code:'15-1252',source_url:'https://example.com/real-builder',employment_type:'Full time',compensation:'Not supplied',description:'Python systems experience is required. Leadership preferred.',is_active:true,vendor:'Operator test'},'abcd'.repeat(16),1);
    const p=new SegmentA();await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify({runner_id:'independent',observations:[artifact]}),'builder-return.txt');p.decide('job-1','PUSH');const tx=await p.commit(p.prepareCommit().token,true,identity);
    const ctx=await acceptThreeFiles([{name:'SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json',text:tx.pairs[0].envelope},{name:'STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json',text:tx.pairs[0].sidecar},{name:'candidate.sdna.yaml',text:'candidate_id: candidate-test\natoms: []\n'}]);await executeIngress(ctx);
    expect(ctx.stageProgression.INGRESS).toBe('PASS');expect(JSON.parse(tx.pairs[0].sidecar).raw_posting).toBe(artifact.envelope.payload.scout.section_8_source.source_text);
  });
  it('feeds actual A4 artifacts to the frozen B validator; SDNA is supplied only to the test receiver',async()=>{
    const {p,tx}=await committed();
    const receiver: Receiver={receive:async(transactionId,pairs)=>{
      const artifacts=[];
      for(const pair of pairs){
        const ctx=await acceptThreeFiles([{name:'SEALED_A_ENVELOPE.json',text:pair.envelope},{name:'STAGE_A_SIDECAR.json',text:pair.sidecar},{name:'candidate.sdna.yaml',text:'candidate_id: candidate-test\natoms:\n  - atom_id: test-only\n    content: Python systems experience\n    domain: WORK_HISTORY\n'}]);
        await executeIngress(ctx);artifacts.push({uid:pair.uid,envelopeHash:await sha256(pair.envelope),sidecarHash:await sha256(pair.sidecar),accepted:ctx.stageProgression.INGRESS==='PASS'});
      }
      return {transactionId,receiver:'frozen-B-test-only',artifacts};
    }};
    expect((await p.handoff(tx.id,receiver)).state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(p.view().transactions[0].pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);expect(p.view().jobs).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it('local validation and missing live receiver never imply handoff success',async()=>{
    const {p,tx}=await committed();expect((await p.certify(tx.id)).state).toBe('LOCALLY_VERIFIED');const blocked=await p.handoff(tx.id);expect(blocked.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(blocked.failures[0].reason).toContain('zero accepted');expect(blocked.pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);
  });
  it.each(['wrong hash','partial','rejected','wrong transaction','duplicate','transport error'])('blocks %s acknowledgment and preserves all committed versions',async kind=>{
    const {p,tx}=await committed(new SegmentA(),2);
    const receiver:Receiver={receive:async(id,pairs)=>{
      if(kind==='transport error')throw new Error('Transport unavailable');
      const artifacts=pairs.map(pair=>({uid:pair.uid,envelopeHash:pair.envelopeHash,sidecarHash:pair.sidecarHash,accepted:true}));
      if(kind==='wrong hash')artifacts[0].sidecarHash='wrong';if(kind==='partial')artifacts.pop();if(kind==='rejected')artifacts[0].accepted=false;if(kind==='duplicate')artifacts[1]=artifacts[0];
      return {transactionId:kind==='wrong transaction'?'other':id,receiver:'test-only',artifacts};
    }};
    const result=await p.handoff(tx.id,receiver);expect(result.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(result.pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);expect(p.view().jobs).toHaveLength(2);expect(result.failures).not.toEqual([]);
  });
  it('blocks missing acknowledgment rather than clearing staging',async()=>{
    const {p,tx}=await committed();vi.useFakeTimers();let enter!:()=>void;const entered=new Promise<void>(resolve=>{enter=resolve;});const pending=p.handoff(tx.id,{receive:()=>{enter();return new Promise(()=>{});}});await entered;await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1001);const result=await pending;expect(result.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(result.failures[0].reason).toContain('timed out');expect(result.pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);
  });
  it('refuses source and artifact-version mismatches',async()=>{
    const {tx}=await committed();const pair=structuredClone(tx.pairs[0]);const s=JSON.parse(pair.sidecar);s.raw_posting+='tampered';pair.sidecar=JSON.stringify(s);const errors=await validatePair(pair);expect(errors.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const changed=structuredClone(tx.pairs[0]);changed.envelopeHash='0'.repeat(64);expect((await validatePair(changed))[0].reason).toContain('version integrity');
  });
});

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/tests/topology.test.ts

import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ENDPOINTS, GATES, JUNCTIONS, allocateGates, validateGates, validateJunctions } from '../src/core/topology';
import { SegmentA } from '../src/core/pipeline';
import { validateTransaction } from '../src/core/certification';
import { sha256 } from '../src/vendor/scout/compiler';
import { definition, returned } from './fixtures';

describe('GV-01–GV-07 local topology',()=>{
  it('allocates a single shared ingress/egress counter starting G1 per component',()=>{
    expect(validateGates()).toEqual([]);expect(validateJunctions()).toEqual([]);
    expect(GATES.filter(g=>g.component==='A2').map(g=>g.address)).toEqual(['A2<G1:','A2>G2:','A2>G3:','A2>G4:']);
    expect(GATES.filter(g=>g.component==='A5').map(g=>g.address)).toEqual(['A5<G1:','A5<G2:','A5>G3:','A5>G4:','A5_G5:']);
    expect(GATES).toHaveLength(20);expect(new Set(GATES.map(g=>g.address)).size).toBe(20);
  });
  it('requires classification before allocation and preserves reserved endpoints',()=>{
    const draft=structuredClone(ENDPOINTS);draft[0].direction='unknown' as any;
    expect(()=>allocateGates(draft)).toThrow('Classify');
    expect(GATES.filter(g=>g.mode==='RESERVED_DISABLED').map(g=>g.address)).toEqual(['A2>G3:','A2>G4:']);
    expect(GATES.find(g=>g.endpoint==='handoff-control')?.mode).toBe('TEST_ONLY');
  });
  it.each(['duplicate number','wrong direction','missing reserved','wrong component','activated reserve','no evidence'])('refuses %s gate registers',kind=>{
    const g=structuredClone([...GATES]);
    if(kind==='duplicate number'){g[1].number=1;g[1].address='A1>G1:';}
    if(kind==='wrong direction'){g[0].direction='egress';g[0].address='A1>G1:';}
    if(kind==='missing reserved')g.splice(4,1);
    if(kind==='wrong component')g[0].component='B1' as any;
    if(kind==='activated reserve')g[4].mode='LOCAL';
    if(kind==='no evidence')g[0].evidence='';
    expect(validateGates(g).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
  it('separates PIN export source accounting, independent SDNA and external delivery',()=>{
    expect(JUNCTIONS).toHaveLength(17);
    expect(JUNCTIONS.find(j=>j.id==='J17')).toMatchObject({from:'A3>G5:',to:'Operator manual saving → J08'});
    expect(JUNCTIONS.find(j=>j.id==='J06')?.status).toBe('EXTERNAL_UNVERIFIED');
    expect(JUNCTIONS.find(j=>j.id==='J15')?.from).toBe('Operator-supplied SDNA (outside A)');
    expect(GATES.some(g=>(g.component as string).startsWith('B'))).toBe(false);
    expect(JUNCTIONS.find(j=>j.id==='J16')?.to).toBe('A5_G5:');
  });
  it.each(['reversed','missing','promoted','duplicate'])('refuses %s junction certification',kind=>{
    const j=structuredClone([...JUNCTIONS]);
    if(kind==='reversed'){const t=j[1].from;j[1].from=j[1].to;j[1].to=t;}
    if(kind==='missing')j.pop();
    if(kind==='promoted')j.find(x=>x.id==='J06')!.status='LOCAL';
    if(kind==='duplicate')j.push(j[0]);
    expect(validateJunctions(j).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

async function capture(){
  const p=new SegmentA();const scope=await p.compile(definition());
  await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned('ACTIVE')),'active.txt',scope.invocation);p.decide('job-1','PUSH');
  await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned('EXCLUDED','Excluded original source')),'excluded.txt',scope.invocation);p.decide('job-2','REJECT',true);
  await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned('LATER','Pinned source')),'later.txt',scope.invocation);p.decide('job-3','PIN');
  await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned('PENDING','Pending source')),'pending.txt',scope.invocation);
  const tx=await p.commit(p.prepareCommit().token,true,{runId:'run',candidateId:'candidate',pipelineVersion:'1.0.0'});
  return {p,tx};
}

describe('Plan 02 A5 source/UID/exclusion/batch certification',()=>{
  it('preserves certified search-scope context across PIN export/reset/reimport',async()=>{
    const p=new SegmentA();const scope=await p.compile(definition());await p.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned()),'one',scope.invocation);p.decide('job-1','PIN');
    const exported=p.exportPinned();const q=new SegmentA();await q.importPayload(exported,'saved.txt');q.decide('job-1','PUSH');
    const tx=await q.commit(q.prepareCommit().token,true,{runId:'run',candidateId:'candidate',pipelineVersion:'1.0.0'});
    expect(JSON.parse(tx.pairs[0].envelope).history[0].search_scope).toEqual(scope.cartridge);expect(await validateTransaction(tx)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it('preserves source corpus in sidecar and activates exactly the selected UID',async()=>{
    const {p,tx}=await capture();expect(await validateTransaction(tx)).toEqual([]);
    const sidecar=JSON.parse(tx.pairs[0].sidecar);expect(sidecar.A_staging.records).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(sidecar.A_staging.records.filter((r:any)=>r.active)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(sidecar.A_staging.records.find((r:any)=>r.uid==='EXCLUDED')).toMatchObject({decision:'REJECT',excluded:true,active:false,raw_posting:'Excluded original source'});
    expect(sidecar.A_staging.records.find((r:any)=>r.uid==='LATER')).toMatchObject({decision:'PIN',active:false});
    expect(sidecar.A_staging.records.find((r:any)=>r.uid==='PENDING')).toMatchObject({decision:'PENDING',active:false});
    expect(sidecar.A_staging.payloads[0].raw).toBe(tx.payloads[0].raw);
    const before=structuredClone(tx.pairs);expect((await p.certify(tx.id)).state).toBe('LOCALLY_VERIFIED');expect(p.view().transactions[0].pairs).toEqual(before);
  });
  it.each(['exclusion','active UID','batch','source reference','payload','corpus hash','scope','audit','duplicate source'])('blocks %s mismatch even when the transmitted version hash is recomputed',async kind=>{
    const {tx}=await capture();const pair=tx.pairs[0];const s=JSON.parse(pair.sidecar),e=JSON.parse(pair.envelope);
    if(kind==='exclusion')s.A_staging.records[1].excluded=false;
    if(kind==='active UID')s.A_staging.records[1].active=true;
    if(kind==='batch')e.history[0].batch_id='other';
    if(kind==='source reference')s.A_staging.records[0].source_references=['missing'];
    if(kind==='payload')s.A_staging.payloads[0].raw+='tampered';
    if(kind==='corpus hash')tx.corpus[0].sourceHash='0'.repeat(64);
    if(kind==='scope')e.history[0].search_scope.definition.id='DSK-999999';
    if(kind==='audit')e.audit_trail[1].decision='PUSH';
    if(kind==='duplicate source')s.A_staging.payloads[1].ref=s.A_staging.payloads[0].ref;
    pair.sidecar=JSON.stringify(s);pair.sidecarHash=await sha256(pair.sidecar);
    pair.envelope=JSON.stringify(e);pair.envelopeHash=await sha256(pair.envelope);
    expect((await validateTransaction(tx)).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/tests/trusted-handoff.test.ts

import { describe,it,expect,vi,afterEach } from 'vitest';
import { SegmentA } from '../src/core/pipeline';
import { createLocalReceiver } from '../src/integration/local-receiver';
import { returned } from './fixtures';
import { beginLocalAttempt,verifyLocalEvidence } from '../src/core/local-evidence';
import type { Receiver } from '../src/core/model';
import * as attestation from './execution-attestation';
const sdna='candidate_id: candidate-test\natoms: []\n';
async function setup(count=1){const a=new SegmentA();for(let i=0;i<count;i++){await a.importPayload(JSON.stringify(await returned(`OBS-${i}`,`Python required ${i}`)),'runner-fixture');a.decide(`job-${i+1}`,'PUSH');}const tx=await a.commit(a.prepareCommit().token,true,{runId:'run-test',candidateId:'candidate-test',pipelineVersion:'1'});return {a,tx};}
afterEach(()=>{vi.useRealTimers();vi.restoreAllMocks();});
describe('controlled local trusted execution',()=>{
 it('A4→A5→unchanged B session→A5 accepts exact bytes with B1 READY only',async()=>{const {a,tx}=await setup(2);const receiver=createLocalReceiver(sdna);const result=await a.handoff(tx.id,receiver);expect(result.state).toBe('ACCEPTED');expect(result.pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);});
 it('records actual ingress progression and refuses replay across invocations',async()=>{const {tx}=await setup();const r=createLocalReceiver(sdna);const attempt=beginLocalAttempt(r);const receipt=await r.receive(tx.id,tx.pairs);const e=verifyLocalEvidence(r,receipt,attempt,tx.id,tx.pairs);if(process.env.MARA_PRINT_RECORD==='1')console.info(JSON.stringify({evidence:e,receipt}));expect((e.results[0] as any).progression).toMatchObject({INGRESS:'PASS',B1:'READY',B2:'LOCKED'});expect(()=>verifyLocalEvidence(r,receipt,attempt,tx.id,tx.pairs)).toThrow('Replayed');const other=beginLocalAttempt(r);expect(()=>verifyLocalEvidence(r,receipt,other,tx.id,tx.pairs)).toThrow('Replayed');});
 it('preserves exact B candidate refusal diagnostics',async()=>{const {a,tx}=await setup();const result=await a.handoff(tx.id,createLocalReceiver('candidate_id: wrong\natoms: []\n'));expect(result.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(result.failures[0].reason).toContain('candidate_id_match');expect(result.pairs).toEqual(tx.pairs);});
 it.each(['envelope','sidecar','source','uid','taxonomy'])('unchanged B refuses %s without repair',async kind=>{const {tx}=await setup();const p=structuredClone(tx.pairs[0]);if(kind==='envelope')p.envelope='{}';if(kind==='sidecar')p.sidecar='{}';if(kind==='source'){const s=JSON.parse(p.sidecar);s.raw_posting+='altered';p.sidecar=JSON.stringify(s);}if(kind==='uid'){const s=JSON.parse(p.sidecar);s.run_binding.target_id='wrong';p.sidecar=JSON.stringify(s);}if(kind==='taxonomy'){const e=JSON.parse(p.envelope);e.A_scout.requirements_taxonomy.hard_requirements=[];p.envelope=JSON.stringify(e);}const r=createLocalReceiver(sdna);beginLocalAttempt(r);const receipt=await r.receive(tx.id,[p]);expect(receipt.artifacts[0].accepted).toBe(false);expect(receipt.artifacts[0].reason).not.toBe('[]');});
 it.each(['transaction','envelope hash','sidecar hash','receiver','partial','validator','build'])('blocks tampered %s evidence',async kind=>{const {a,tx}=await setup();const r=createLocalReceiver(sdna);const original=r.receive.bind(r);r.receive=async(id,pairs)=>{const receipt=await original(id,pairs);if(kind==='transaction')receipt.transactionId='wrong';if(kind==='receiver')receipt.receiver='forged';if(kind==='partial')receipt.artifacts=[];if(kind==='envelope hash')receipt.artifacts[0].envelopeHash='0'.repeat(64);if(kind==='sidecar hash')receipt.artifacts[0].sidecarHash='0'.repeat(64);if(kind==='validator'||kind==='build')(receipt as any)[kind]='wrong';return receipt;};expect((await a.handoff(tx.id,r)).state).toBe('BLOCKED');});
 it.each(['commit','dependencyHash','buildHash','sourceHash'])('blocks actual attestation mismatch %s',async key=>{const {a,tx}=await setup();const actual=attestation.attest();vi.spyOn(attestation,'attest').mockReturnValue({...actual,[key]:'wrong'});const result=await a.handoff(tx.id,createLocalReceiver(sdna));expect(result.state).toBe('BLOCKED');expect(result.failures[0].reason).toMatch(/identity|version/);});
 it('blocks stale genuine evidence',async()=>{const {tx}=await setup();const r=createLocalReceiver(sdna);const attempt=beginLocalAttempt(r);const receipt=await r.receive(tx.id,tx.pairs);vi.useFakeTimers();vi.setSystemTime(Date.now()+2000);expect(()=>verifyLocalEvidence(r,receipt,attempt,tx.id,tx.pairs)).toThrow('Stale');});
 it('forged plausible receipt lacks origin',async()=>{const {a,tx}=await setup();const r:Receiver={receive:async id=>({transactionId:id,receiver:'CONTROLLED_LOCAL_B_SESSION',artifacts:tx.pairs.map(p=>({uid:p.uid,envelopeHash:p.envelopeHash,sidecarHash:p.sidecarHash,accepted:true}))})};expect((await a.handoff(tx.id,r)).failures[0].reason).toContain('origin');});
 it('unavailable execution and missing receipt block explicitly',async()=>{for(const receive of [async()=>{throw new Error('B execution unavailable');},async()=>undefined as any]){const {a,tx}=await setup();expect((await a.handoff(tx.id,{receive})).state).toBe('BLOCKED');}});
});

import { test, expect, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';

test('Plan 02 shows local addresses and keeps physical B/runner gates unassigned',async({page})=>{
  await page.goto('/');await expect(page.locator('#topology li')).toHaveCount(20);
  await expect(page.locator('#topology')).toContainText('A2>G3: folder · RESERVED_DISABLED');
  await expect(page.locator('#topology')).toContainText('A5_G5: handoff-control · TEST_ONLY');
  await page.locator('#topology summary').click();await expect(page.locator('#topology')).toContainText('gate number undeclared');
  expect(await page.locator('#topology').innerText()).not.toMatch(/B1[<>_]G\d+/);
});

async function upload(page: Page, payload: string, name = 'runner.txt') {
  await page.locator('#incoming-files').setInputFiles({ name, mimeType: 'text/plain', buffer: Buffer.from(payload) });
  await page.locator('#import').click();
}
async function compile(page: Page,id='DSK-000001') {
  for(const [field,value] of Object.entries({'disk-id':id,label:'Operator systems search',version:'1.0.0',zip:'10001',radius:'25',naics:'541511 | Software',soc:'15-1252 | Software developers',keywords:'15-1252 | systems'}))await page.locator(`#${field}`).fill(value);
  await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Compile and validate search scope'}).click();
  await expect(page.locator('#compile-status')).toContainText('25 compiler checks passed');
}

test('A1/A2 repeated compiler outputs and complete manual copy, without a runner',async({page,context})=>{
  await context.grantPermissions(['clipboard-read','clipboard-write']);
  await page.goto('/');await compile(page);
  await page.locator('#prepare-json').click();const first=JSON.parse(await page.locator('#outbound').inputValue());expect(first.certification.status).toBe('CERTIFIED');
  await page.locator('#copy-json').click();expect(await page.evaluate(()=>navigator.clipboard.readText())).toBe(await page.locator('#outbound').inputValue());
  await compile(page,'DSK-000002');await page.locator('#prepare-json').click();expect(JSON.parse(await page.locator('#outbound').inputValue()).definition.id).toBe('DSK-000002');
  await page.locator('#scope').selectOption('scope-1');await page.locator('#prepare-json').click();expect(JSON.parse(await page.locator('#outbound').inputValue()).definition.id).toBe('DSK-000001');
  await expect(page.getByRole('button',{name:'Folder destination · not configured'})).toBeDisabled();await expect(page.getByRole('button',{name:'Automated transport · not configured'})).toBeDisabled();
});

  await page.goto('/');

    {name:'one.txt',mimeType:'text/plain',buffer:Buffer.from('UID: one\nDescription:\nShort source')},

  ]);await page.locator('#import').click();await expect(page.locator('.job')).toHaveCount(2);

  await page.locator('[data-decision="job-1"]').selectOption('REJECT');await page.locator('#affirm-confirm').click();await expect(page.locator('.job').first()).toHaveClass(/rejected/);await expect(page.locator('[data-decision="job-1"]')).toBeDisabled();

  await page.locator('#reset').click();await expect(page.locator('#warning')).toContainText('will be lost');await page.locator('#cancel-confirm').click();await expect(page.locator('.job')).toHaveCount(3);
});

  expect(errors).toEqual([]);expect(await page.evaluate(()=>({local:localStorage.length,session:sessionStorage.length}))).toEqual({local:0,session:0});
  expect(requests.every(r=>r.startsWith('http://127.0.0.1:4183/'))).toBe(true);

FILE: Envoy_Diplomat-integration-a1-b5-vercel/segment-a/vitest.config.ts

import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
import { transformWithEsbuild } from 'vite';
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { posix } from 'node:path';

// Read unchanged B source from its exact Git commit, in memory, for contract
// tests only. No B file or branch is edited and no B runtime is in A's bundle.
const PIN = 'd1b8348ded0190cccc7017526b8a16c84005db2a';
const originals=new Map<string,string>();
const prefix = '\0frozen-b:';
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [{
    name: 'pinned-b-contract-reference',
    resolveId(source, importer) {
      if (source.startsWith('frozen-b:')) return '\0' + source;
      if (importer?.startsWith(prefix) && source.startsWith('.')) {
        const path = posix.join(posix.dirname(importer.slice(prefix.length)), source);
        return prefix + path + (path.endsWith('.ts') ? '' : '.ts');
      }
    },
    load(id) {
      if (id === prefix + 'identity') return `import { attest } from ${JSON.stringify(new URL('./tests/execution-attestation.ts',import.meta.url).pathname)}; export function identity(){return attest();}`;
      if (id.startsWith(prefix)) {
        const path=id.slice(prefix.length);
        const code=execFileSync('git', ['show', `${PIN}:${path}`], { encoding:'utf8' });
        originals.set(id,code); return code;
      }
    },
    async transform(code, id) {
      if (id.startsWith(prefix)) {
        const result = await transformWithEsbuild(code, `tests/reference/${id.slice(prefix.length)}`, { loader: 'ts', tsconfigRaw: { compilerOptions: { target: 'ES2022' } } });
        const digest=(s:string)=>createHash('sha256').update(s).digest('hex');
        const hook=new URL('./tests/execution-attestation.ts',import.meta.url).pathname;
        if(id===prefix+'identity')return {code:result.code,map:null};
        return { code: `import { recordLoaded } from ${JSON.stringify(hook)}; recordLoaded(${JSON.stringify(id.slice(prefix.length))},${JSON.stringify(digest(originals.get(id)!))},${JSON.stringify(digest(result.code))});\n`+result.code, map: null };
      }
    },
  }],
  test: { include: ['tests/**/*.test.ts'] },
});

# Getting Started with Create React App

This project was bootstrapped with [Create React App](https://github.com/facebook/create-react-app).

## Available Scripts

In the project directory, you can run:

### `npm start`

Runs the app in the development mode.\
Open [http://localhost:3000](http://localhost:3000) to view it in the browser.

The page will reload if you make edits.\
You will also see any lint errors in the console.

### `npm test`

Launches the test runner in the interactive watch mode.\
See the section about [running tests](https://facebook.github.io/create-react-app/docs/running-tests) for more information.

### `npm run build`

Builds the app for production to the `build` folder.\
It correctly bundles React in production mode and optimizes the build for the best performance.

The build is minified and the filenames include the hashes.\
Your app is ready to be deployed!

See the section about [deployment](https://facebook.github.io/create-react-app/docs/deployment) for more information.

### `npm run eject`

**Note: this is a one-way operation. Once you `eject`, you can’t go back!**

If you aren’t satisfied with the build tool and configuration choices, you can `eject` at any time. This command will remove the single build dependency from your project.

Instead, it will copy all the configuration files and the transitive dependencies (webpack, Babel, ESLint, etc) right into your project so you have full control over them. All of the commands except `eject` will still work, but they will point to the copied scripts so you can tweak them. At this point you’re on your own.

You don’t have to ever use `eject`. The curated feature set is suitable for small and middle deployments, and you shouldn’t feel obligated to use this feature. However we understand that this tool wouldn’t be useful if you couldn’t customize it when you are ready for it.

## Learn More

You can learn more in the [Create React App documentation](https://facebook.github.io/create-react-app/docs/getting-started).

To learn React, check out the [React documentation](https://reactjs.org/).

<script>
// Bridge placeholder — dynamically generated from __bridgeRegistry.
// Each stub queues calls behind _ready, then forwards to _impl once Penpal connects.
window.bridge = { _ready: new Promise(function(r) { window._bridgeResolve = r; }) };
window.__bridgeRegistry.forEach(function(m) {
  window.bridge[m.name] = function() {
    var args = arguments;
    return window.bridge._ready.then(function() {
      return window.bridge._impl[m.name].apply(null, args);
    });
  };
});
</script>
<script>
// External Navigation Shim — makes window.open() and <a target="_blank"> "just work"
// inside the sandboxed iframe by routing them through the bridge to the outer frame.
// The sandbox attribute blocks popups/navigation directly, so we intercept and delegate.
(function() {
  // Patch window.open to route through bridge.openExternalUrl
  var _nativeOpen = window.open;
  window.open = function(url) {
    if (!url) return null;
    try {
      // If the URL has no protocol, try prepending https://
      var urlStr = String(url);
      if (urlStr && !/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9+\-.]*:/.test(urlStr)) {
        urlStr = 'https://' + urlStr;
      }
      var parsed = new URL(urlStr);
      if (parsed.protocol === 'http:' || parsed.protocol === 'https:') {
        window.bridge.openExternalUrl(parsed.href);
        return null;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('[NavigationShim] Invalid URL passed to window.open:', url);
    }
    // Fall through for non-http URLs (will be blocked by sandbox anyway)
    return null;
  };

  // Intercept <a> clicks with external hrefs and route through bridge
  document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    var anchor = e.target;
    // Walk up to find the nearest <a> element
    while (anchor && anchor.tagName !== 'A') anchor = anchor.parentElement;
    if (!anchor || !anchor.href) return;

    try {
      // anchor.href is already fully resolved by the browser
      var parsed = new URL(anchor.href);
      if (parsed.protocol !== 'http:' && parsed.protocol !== 'https:') return;

      // Only intercept links that would navigate away or open a new tab
      var target = (anchor.target || '').toLowerCase();
      if (target === '_blank' || target === '_top' || target === '_parent') {
        e.preventDefault();
        window.bridge.openExternalUrl(parsed.href);
      } else if (!target || target === '_self') {
        // Same-frame navigation to an external URL — also intercept since
        // the sandbox blocks top-level navigation anyway
        if (parsed.origin !== window.location.origin) {
          e.preventDefault();
          window.bridge.openExternalUrl(parsed.href);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      // Malformed href — let the browser handle it (it'll fail in sandbox)
    }
  }, true); // Use capture phase to intercept before React/app handlers
})();
</script>
<script>
// Storage Sandbox — provides localStorage and sessionStorage
// to sandboxed iframes that cannot access real browser storage.
// Uses an in-memory cache for synchronous reads, with async write-through
// to the MFE via the Penpal bridge for persistence across page reloads.
(function() {
  var _localCache = Object.create(null);
  var _sessionCache = Object.create(null);

  // When opened directly (thumbnail capture, local preview) there is no parent
  // frame so the bridge will never connect. Skip hydration entirely.
  if (window.parent === window) {
    console.log('[StorageSandbox] No parent frame — skipping bridge hydration');
    window._storageReady = Promise.resolve();
  } else {
    // Hydrate caches from MFE storage once bridge is ready.
    window._storageReady = window.bridge.storageGetAll().then(function(data) {
      if (data && data.local) {
        var keys = Object.keys(data.local);
        for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) _localCache[keys[i]] = data.local[keys[i]];
      }
      if (data && data.session) {
        var skeys = Object.keys(data.session);
        for (var j = 0; j < skeys.length; j++) _sessionCache[skeys[j]] = data.session[skeys[j]];
      }
      console.log('[StorageSandbox] Cache hydrated from MFE');
    }).catch(function(e) {
      console.error('[StorageSandbox] Failed to hydrate cache:', e.message, '— rendering with empty cache');
    });
  }

  function createStorageShim(cache, storageType) {
    var storage = {
      getItem: function(key) { return key in cache ? cache[key] : null; },
      setItem: function(key, val) {
        cache[String(key)] = String(val);
        window.bridge.storageSet(storageType + ':' + key, String(val));
      },
      removeItem: function(key) {
        delete cache[String(key)];
        window.bridge.storageRemove(storageType + ':' + key);
      },
      clear: function() {
        var keys = Object.keys(cache);
        for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) delete cache[keys[i]];
        window.bridge.storageClear(storageType);
      },
      key: function(index) {
        var keys = Object.keys(cache);
        return index < keys.length ? keys[index] : null;
      },
      get length() { return Object.keys(cache).length; }
    };
    return new Proxy(storage, {
      get: function(target, prop) {
        if (prop in target) return target[prop];
        return target.getItem(prop);
      },
      set: function(target, prop, value) {
        if (['getItem','setItem','removeItem','clear','key','length'].indexOf(prop) !== -1) return true;
        target.setItem(prop, value);
        return true;
      }
    });
  }

  Object.defineProperty(window, 'localStorage', {
    value: createStorageShim(_localCache, 'local'), configurable: false, writable: false
  });
  Object.defineProperty(window, 'sessionStorage', {
    value: createStorageShim(_sessionCache, 'session'), configurable: false, writable: false
  });

})();
</script>
<script>
// Penpal bridge initialization - establishes connection to middle frame
// Skip entirely when not in an iframe (thumbnail capture, local preview)
(async function() {
  if (window.parent === window) {
    console.log('[Inner] No parent frame — skipping Penpal bridge init');
    return;
  }
  try {
    console.log('[Inner] Initializing Penpal connection to middle frame');
    console.log('[Inner] window.parent:', window.parent);
    console.log('[Inner] window.location.origin:', window.location.origin);

    const messenger = new Penpal.WindowMessenger({
      remoteWindow: window.parent,
      allowedOrigins: ['*']
    });
    console.log('[Inner] WindowMessenger created');

    const connection = Penpal.connect({
      messenger: messenger,
      methods: {
        testFromMiddle: (msg) => {
          console.log('[Inner] Received testFromMiddle():', msg);
          return 'Inner received: ' + msg;
        },
        // ── E2E test bridge (inner leaf) ──────────────────────────────────
        // Delegates to window.__qwTestExec, installed by RuntimeLib's
        // qw-test-exec.iife.js (loaded above). The implementation + its unit
        // tests live in QuickPagesRuntimeLib/src/qw-test-exec.ts — NOT inlined
        // here, so it is testable. It drives THIS app's document by human-visible
        // text / accessible name (Forge emits no data-testid contract and the
        // e2e cannot see this frame's DOM), with optional ``within`` anchoring
        // and ``nth`` indexing for repeated UIs.
        //
        // SAFE to expose unconditionally: acts only on this sandboxed document
        // (no storage internals, host tokens, or cross-frame access). Being
        // exposed is not the capability; INVOKING it is, and invocation is only
        // reachable through the trusted middle→inner Penpal channel, which the
        // QuickPages middle frame only drives when the QuickWork host relayed a
        // gated ``qw-app-test-cmd`` (triple-gated OFF in prod). In the web
        // (non-QuickWork) path the relay never fires, so this is dead code.
        __qwTestExec: (cmd) => {
          if (typeof window.__qwTestExec !== 'function') {
            return Promise.resolve({
              ok: false,
              error: 'window.__qwTestExec not installed (qw-test-exec.iife.js missing)',
            });
          }
          return window.__qwTestExec(cmd);
        }
      },
      timeout: 10000
    });
    console.log('[Inner] Penpal.connect() called, waiting for connection...');

    const middle = await connection.promise;
    console.log('[Inner] Connected to middle frame successfully!');

    // Wire _impl dynamically from the bridge registry.
    window.bridge._impl = {};
    window.__bridgeRegistry.forEach(function(m) {
      window.bridge._impl[m.name] = function() {
        return middle[m.name].apply(middle, arguments);
      };
    });

    // Resolve bridge._ready AFTER _impl is assigned so storageGetAll()
    // (and any other bridge method) can safely access _impl.
    if (window._bridgeResolve) {
      window._bridgeResolve();
      delete window._bridgeResolve;
      console.log('[Inner] Bridge ready');
    }

    if (middle.notifyReady) {
      await middle.notifyReady();
      console.log('[Inner] Sent notifyReady() to middle frame');
    } else {
      console.warn('[Inner] middle.notifyReady is not available');
    }

    if (middle.testFromInner) {
      const response = await middle.testFromInner('Hello from inner!');
      const response2 = await middle.testFromInner('Stimpy');
      console.log('[Inner] Middle replied to Stimpy:', response2);
      console.log('[Inner] Middle replied:', response);
    } else {
      console.warn('[Inner] middle.testFromInner is not available');
    }

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[Inner] Penpal setup failed:', error);
    console.error('[Inner] Error name:', error.name);
    console.error('[Inner] Error message:', error.message);
    console.error('[Inner] Error code:', error.code);
    console.error('[Inner] Error stack:', error.stack);
  }
})();
</script>

  const [showArch, setShowArch] = useState(false);
  const [parachuteStatus, setParachuteStatus] = useState<'READY' | 'GENERATING' | 'DONE' | 'FAILED'>('READY');
  const [parachuteResult, setParachuteResult] = useState<ParachuteResult | null>(null);

  const updateCtx = useCallback((updated: BContext) => {
    setCtx({ ...updated });
  }, []);

  const onContextCreated = useCallback((c: BContext) => {
    setCtx({ ...c });
    setActiveTab(1);
  }, []);

  const handleStageAction = useCallback(async (action: string) => {
    if (!ctx) return;
    setError(null);
    try {
      let updated: BContext;
      switch (action) {
        case 'RUN_B1': updated = await executeB1({ ...ctx }); break;
        case 'SEAL_B1': updated = sealB1({ ...ctx }); setActiveTab(2); break;
        case 'RUN_B2': updated = await executeB2({ ...ctx }); break;
        case 'FREEZE_B2': updated = freezeB2({ ...ctx }); setActiveTab(3); break;
        case 'RUN_B3': updated = executeB3({ ...ctx }); break;
        case 'SEAL_B3': updated = sealB3({ ...ctx }); setActiveTab(4); break;
        case 'RUN_B4': updated = await executeB4({ ...ctx }); break;
        case 'SEAL_B4': updated = sealB4({ ...ctx }); setActiveTab(5); break;
        case 'RUN_B5': updated = executeB5({ ...ctx }); break;
        case 'SEAL_B5': updated = sealB5({ ...ctx }); setActiveTab(6); break;
        default: return;
      }
      setCtx({ ...updated });
    } catch (e: any) {
      setError(e.message);
    }
  }, [ctx]);

  const handleFullRun = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!ctx) return;
    setFullRunning(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const result = await runCompleteB({ ...ctx });
      setCtx({ ...result });
      setActiveTab(6);
    } catch (e: any) { setError(e.message); }
    finally { setFullRunning(false); }
  }, [ctx]);

  const handleParachute = useCallback(async () => {
    setParachuteStatus('GENERATING');
    setParachuteResult(null);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const runtimeState = ctx ? {
        run_id: ctx.identity.run_id,
        target_id: ctx.identity.target_id,
        candidate_id: ctx.identity.candidate_id,
        current_stage: Object.entries(ctx.stageProgression).find(([, v]) => v === 'RUNNING')?.[0] || 'IDLE',
        status: ctx.stageProgression.EXPORT === 'PASS' ? 'COMPLETE' : 'IN_PROGRESS',
        errors: ctx.errors,
      } : undefined;

      const result = await generateParachute(runtimeState);
      setParachuteResult(result);

      if (result.status === 'PASS') {
        await downloadFile(result.filename, result.zipBlob);
        setParachuteStatus('DONE');
      } else {
        setParachuteStatus('FAILED');
        setError('Parachute self-test failed: ' + result.self_test_results.filter(t => !t.passed).map(t => t.check).join(', '));
      }
    } catch (e: any) {
      setParachuteStatus('FAILED');
      setError(e.message);
    }
  }, [ctx]);

  const prog = ctx?.stageProgression;

  return (
    <div style={{ minHeight: '100vh', background: COLORS.bg, color: COLORS.text,
      fontFamily: '-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif', fontSize: 14 }}>

      {/* Header */}
      <div style={{ background: '#0f172a', borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.panelBorder}`, padding: '12px 20px' }}>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: 8 }}>
          <div>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700, color: COLORS.textBright }}>SEGMENT B</span>
            <span style={{ color: COLORS.textDim, marginLeft: 12, fontSize: 12 }}>SPATIAL DNA PIPELINE — APP-IN-FLOW</span>
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
            <button style={btn(COLORS.warn, !ctx || fullRunning)} disabled={!ctx || fullRunning} onClick={handleFullRun}>
              {fullRunning ? '⏳ RUNNING...' : '⚡ RUN FULL B CYCLE'}
            </button>
            <button style={btn('#6366f1', parachuteStatus === 'GENERATING')} onClick={handleParachute} disabled={parachuteStatus === 'GENERATING'}>
              🪂 {parachuteStatus === 'GENERATING' ? 'GENERATING...' : parachuteStatus === 'DONE' ? 'PARACHUTE READY ✓' : parachuteStatus === 'FAILED' ? 'PARACHUTE FAILED ✗' : 'EXPORT PARACHUTE'}
            </button>
            <button style={btn(COLORS.panelBorder)} onClick={() => setShowArch(!showArch)}>
              {showArch ? '✕' : '🏗'} ARCH
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {prog && (
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 4, marginTop: 10 }}>
            {STAGES.map(s => (
              <div key={s} style={{ flex: 1, textAlign: 'center' }}>
                <div style={{ background: STATUS_COLORS[prog[s]] + '33', border: `2px solid ${STATUS_COLORS[prog[s]]}`,
                  borderRadius: 4, padding: '4px 0', fontSize: 11, fontWeight: 600, color: STATUS_COLORS[prog[s]] }}>
                  {s}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontSize: 9, color: STATUS_COLORS[prog[s]], marginTop: 2 }}>{prog[s]}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {ctx && (
          <div style={{ ...mono, fontSize: 10, color: COLORS.textDim, marginTop: 6, display: 'flex', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <span>run: {ctx.identity.run_id}</span>
            <span>candidate: {ctx.identity.candidate_id}</span>
            <span>target: {ctx.identity.target_id}</span>
            <span>v{ctx.identity.pipeline_version}</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Architecture Diagram */}
      {showArch && (
        <div style={{ ...basePanel, margin: '12px 20px', background: COLORS.code }}>
          <pre style={{ ...mono, color: COLORS.text, fontSize: 11, lineHeight: 1.6, margin: 0, overflow: 'auto' }}>
{`                    SEGMENT B — APP-IN-FLOW

SEALED A ENVELOPE ────────┐
                           │
STATIONARY SIDECAR ────────┼──► INGRESS / CONTINUITY GATE
                           │
SDNA AUTHORITY ────────────┘
                                  │
                                  ▼
                           B1 — DECOUPLE (TARGET ONLY)
                                  │  STOP
                                  ▼
                           B2 — TARGET TREE (TARGET ONLY)
                                  │  STOP
                                  ▼
CandidateVault ─────────► B3 — EVIDENCE BINDING (NO SIDECAR)
                                  │  STOP
                                  ▼
CandidateVault ─────────► B4 — TRUTH AUDIT
Target trace ───────────►        │  STOP
                                  ▼
                           B5 — FIVE PRISMS (NO SIDECAR, NO SDNA)
                                  │  STOP
                                  ▼
                           T06 VERIFIER
                                  ▼
                       TERMINATE → SEAL → C1 READINESS
                                  ▼
                       SEALED_B_ENVELOPE → C1`}
          </pre>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Parachute Result */}
      {parachuteResult && (
        <div style={{ ...basePanel, margin: '12px 20px', borderColor: parachuteResult.status === 'PASS' ? COLORS.success : COLORS.fail }}>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, marginBottom: 8 }}>
            <span style={{ fontSize: 14, fontWeight: 700, color: parachuteResult.status === 'PASS' ? COLORS.success : COLORS.fail }}>
              🪂 PARACHUTE {parachuteResult.status}
            </span>
            <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.panelBorder), padding: '2px 8px', fontSize: 11 }} onClick={() => setParachuteResult(null)}>✕</button>
          </div>
          <div style={{ ...mono, fontSize: 11, lineHeight: 1.8, color: COLORS.text }}>
            <div>{parachuteResult.filename}</div>
            <div>SHA-256: <span style={{ color: COLORS.accent }}>{parachuteResult.archive_sha256}</span></div>
            <div>{parachuteResult.included_files_count} files</div>
            <div style={{ marginTop: 4, fontSize: 10, color: COLORS.textDim }}>
              Manifest: {parachuteResult.manifest_validation} | Path traversal: {parachuteResult.path_traversal} |
              Source mutation: {parachuteResult.source_mutation} | B isolation: {parachuteResult.normal_b_isolation} |
              Missing imports: {parachuteResult.missing_imported_files.length}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div style={{ marginTop: 8, maxHeight: 160, overflowY: 'auto' }}>
            {parachuteResult.self_test_results.map((t, i) => (
              <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, fontSize: 11, padding: '1px 0', fontFamily: 'monospace' }}>
                <span style={{ color: t.passed ? COLORS.success : COLORS.fail, minWidth: 36 }}>{t.passed ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL'}</span>
                <span style={{ color: COLORS.text, minWidth: 180 }}>{t.check}</span>
                <span style={{ color: COLORS.textDim }}>{t.detail}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Invariants Bar */}
      <div style={{ background: '#0c0f1a', borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.panelBorder}`, padding: '6px 20px',
        display: 'flex', gap: 6, flexWrap: 'wrap', overflowX: 'auto' }}>
        {INVARIANTS.map(inv => (
          <span key={inv} style={{ fontSize: 9, color: COLORS.warn, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', padding: '2px 6px',
            background: COLORS.warn + '11', borderRadius: 3 }}>
            {inv}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Tabs */}
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', borderBottom: `1px solid ${COLORS.panelBorder}`, background: '#0f172a', overflow: 'auto' }}>
        {TABS.map((tab, i) => (
          <button key={tab} onClick={() => setActiveTab(i)}
            style={{ padding: '10px 14px', border: 'none', borderBottom: activeTab === i ? `2px solid ${COLORS.accent}` : '2px solid transparent',
              background: 'none', color: activeTab === i ? COLORS.textBright : COLORS.textDim,
              fontSize: 12, fontWeight: activeTab === i ? 700 : 400, cursor: 'pointer', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>
            {tab}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {/* Content */}
      <div style={{ padding: 20 }}>
        {error && (
          <div style={{ ...basePanel, borderColor: COLORS.fail, marginBottom: 12, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
            <span style={{ color: COLORS.fail, fontSize: 13, flex: 1 }}>❌ {error}</span>
            <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.panelBorder), padding: '2px 8px', fontSize: 11 }} onClick={() => setError(null)}>✕</button>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 0 && <BIngress onContextCreated={onContextCreated} />}

        {activeTab === 1 && (
          <div>
            <B1Inspector result={ctx?.b1Result} />
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B1 === 'READY' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.accent), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('RUN_B1')}>▶ RUN B1</button>
            )}
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B1 === 'PASS' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.sealed), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('SEAL_B1')}>🔒 SEAL B1 / CONTINUE TO B2</button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 2 && (
          <div>
            <B2TreeView result={ctx?.b2Result} />
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B2 === 'READY' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.accent), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('RUN_B2')}>▶ RUN B2</button>
            )}
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B2 === 'PASS' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.sealed), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('FREEZE_B2')}>❄ FREEZE B2 / CONTINUE TO B3</button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 3 && (
          <div>
            <B3BindingView result={ctx?.b3Result} />
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B3 === 'READY' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.accent), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('RUN_B3')}>▶ RUN B3</button>
            )}
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B3 === 'PASS' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.sealed), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('SEAL_B3')}>🔒 SEAL B3 / CONTINUE TO B4</button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 4 && (
          <div>
            <B4AuditView result={ctx?.b4Result} />
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B4 === 'READY' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.accent), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('RUN_B4')}>▶ RUN B4</button>
            )}
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B4 === 'PASS' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.sealed), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('SEAL_B4')}>🔒 SEAL B4 / CONTINUE TO B5</button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 5 && (
          <div>
            <B5ProjectionView result={ctx?.b5Result} />
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B5 === 'READY' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.accent), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('RUN_B5')}>▶ RUN B5</button>
            )}
            {ctx && ctx.stageProgression.B5 === 'PASS' && (
              <button style={{ ...btn(COLORS.sealed), marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => handleStageAction('SEAL_B5')}>🔒 SEAL B5 / RUN T06</button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {activeTab === 6 && ctx && <TraceHandoff ctx={ctx} setCtx={updateCtx} />}
        {activeTab === 6 && !ctx && <div style={{ color: COLORS.textDim }}>Complete stages B1-B5 first.</div>}
        {activeTab === 7 && <BTestHarness />}
        {activeTab === 8 && <BCodeInspector />}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// Compiled Input Adapter (future A→B)
// ============================================================

import type { SealedAEnvelope, StageASidecar, CandidateVaultState, BContext, StageProgression } from '../types';
import { hydrateEnvelope } from '../authority/envelope';

export function createCompiledBContext(
  existingEnvelope: SealedAEnvelope,
  existingSidecar: StageASidecar,
  mountedCandidateVault: CandidateVaultState
): BContext {
  const envelope = hydrateEnvelope(existingEnvelope);

  const stageProgression: StageProgression = {
    INGRESS: 'READY',
    B1: 'LOCKED', B2: 'LOCKED', B3: 'LOCKED',
    B4: 'LOCKED', B5: 'LOCKED', T06: 'LOCKED', EXPORT: 'LOCKED',
  };

  return {
    identity: {
      run_id: envelope.Z_state.run_id,
      candidate_id: envelope.Z_state.candidate_id,
      target_id: envelope.Z_state.target_id,
      pipeline_version: envelope.Z_state.pipeline_version,
      sdna_manifest_sha256: envelope.Z_state.sdna_manifest_sha256 || mountedCandidateVault.canonical_manifest_sha256,
      target_source_sha256: mountedCandidateVault.raw_yaml_sha256,
    },
    envelope,
    sidecar: existingSidecar,
    vault: mountedCandidateVault,
    stageProgression,
    errors: [],
  };
}
